_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["gying_protocol_v1106"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.10.6 观影 GYING 真实协议适配层。\n"
    "\n"
    "依据 2026-09-01 浏览器 HAR 收口真实调用链：\n"
    "- 搜索优先使用 /search?q=...&type=&mode=1，并保留旧 mode=2 兼容回退；\n"
    "- /res/downurl/{type}/{id} 同时解析 downlist、panlist 与嵌入 Magnet/ED2K；\n"
    "- downlist.list.k == 0 时使用 list.m 的 BTIH 直接构造 Magnet，不需要真的打开 /bt/{u} 新窗口；\n"
    "- panlist 保留真实网盘 URL、名称、类型与提取码，迅雷候选可直接拿到 panlist.p；\n"
    "- downurl JSON 的 login 标志也视为登录失效，不再只看 HTTP 403/登录 HTML；\n"
    "- 已有明确登录态 Cookie 时优先复用，避免每轮都重新 POST 密码触发图形验证码；\n"
    "- 密码登录遇站点图形验证码时返回明确状态，不伪装成普通节点故障；\n"
    "- 对前端 refresh=1 的动态浏览器验证做显式处理，能复用现有 PoW 时自动重试，否则给出可诊断错误。\n"
    "\n"
    "本层只解析用户正常登录后网页本身返回的资源数据，不绕过账号权限、验证码或访问控制。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import html\n"
    "import json\n"
    "import re\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple\n"
    "from urllib.parse import parse_qs, quote, urlparse\n"
    "\n"
    "import requests\n"
    "\n"
    "from .gying_runtime_v193 import _parse_search_payload_result_v11223\n"
    "from .provider_sources_v192 import _dedupe_candidates, _find_links\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_BTIH_RE = re.compile(r\"^(?:[0-9A-Fa-f]{40}|[A-Z2-7a-z2-7]{32})$\")\n"
    "_XUNLEI_RE = re.compile(r\"https?://pan\\.xunlei\\.com/s/[^\\s\\\"'<>，。；;]+\", re.I)\n"
    "_CAPTCHA_HINTS = (\n"
    "    \"验证码\",\n"
    "    \"图形验证\",\n"
    "    \"安全验证\",\n"
    "    \"点击验证\",\n"
    "    \"captcha\",\n"
    ")\n"
    "_BAD_CREDENTIAL_HINTS = (\n"
    "    \"用户名或密码\",\n"
    "    \"账号或密码\",\n"
    "    \"密码错误\",\n"
    "    \"账号密码错误\",\n"
    "    \"invalid password\",\n"
    "    \"user or password\",\n"
    ")\n"
    "_AUTH_COOKIE_MODES = {\"password\", \"cookie\", \"cookie_reuse\", \"configured_cookie\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _safe_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        return int(value)\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "        return default\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _safe_float(value: Any, default: float = 0.0) -> float:\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        return float(value)\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "        return default\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _as_list(value: Any) -> List[Any]:\n"
    "    if isinstance(value, list):\n"
    "        return list(value)\n"
    "    if isinstance(value, tuple):\n"
    "        return list(value)\n"
    "    return []\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _recursive_dict(payload: Any, key: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "    if isinstance(payload, dict):\n"
    "        value = payload.get(key)\n"
    "        if isinstance(value, dict):\n"
    "            return dict(value)\n"
    "        for child in payload.values():\n"
    "            found = _recursive_dict(child, key)\n"
    "            if found:\n"
    "                return found\n"
    "    elif isinstance(payload, (list, tuple)):\n"
    "        for child in payload:\n"
    "            found = _recursive_dict(child, key)\n"
    "            if found:\n"
    "                return found\n"
    "    return {}\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def extract_panlist_v1106(payload: Any) -> Dict[str, List[Any]]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"读取 HAR 中网页实际使用的 panlist，并保留提取码字段 p。\"\"\"\n"
    "    pan = _recursive_dict(payload, \"panlist\")\n"
    "    if not pan:\n"
    "        return {\"url\": [], \"name\": [], \"type\": [], \"p\": [], \"id\": []}\n"
    "    return {\n"
    "        \"url\": _as_list(pan.get(\"url\")),\n"
    "        \"name\": _as_list(pan.get(\"name\")),\n"
    "        \"type\": _as_list(pan.get(\"type\")),\n"
    "        \"p\": _as_list(pan.get(\"p\")),\n"
    "        \"id\": _as_list(pan.get(\"id\")),\n"
    "    }\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def extract_downlist_v1106(payload: Any) -> Dict[str, List[Any]]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"读取网页 _Downlist 组件实际消费的并行数组。\"\"\"\n"
    "    down = _recursive_dict(payload, \"downlist\")\n"
    "    listing = down.get(\"list\") if isinstance(down, dict) else None\n"
    "    if not isinstance(listing, dict):\n"
    "        return {\"t\": [], \"m\": [], \"k\": [], \"u\": [], \"s\": [], \"e\": [], \"p\": [], \"n\": []}\n"
    "    return {\n"
    "        key: _as_list(listing.get(key))\n"
    "        for key in (\"t\", \"m\", \"k\", \"u\", \"s\", \"e\", \"p\", \"n\")\n"
    "    }\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _parallel_value(rows: Dict[str, List[Any]], key: str, index: int, default: Any = \"\") -> Any:\n"
    "    values = rows.get(key) or []\n"
    "    return values[index] if index < len(values) else default\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _build_magnet(btih: Any, title: str) -> str:\n"
    "    raw = str(btih or \"\").strip()\n"
    "    if not _BTIH_RE.fullmatch(raw):\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    return f\"magnet:?xt=urn:btih:{raw}&dn={quote(str(title or '').strip(), safe='')}\"\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def extract_resource_rows_v1106(payload: Any, item: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"把一次 downurl 响应统一为可缓存的原始资源行。\"\"\"\n"
    "    title = str(item.get(\"title\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "    common = {\n"
    "        \"search_title\": title,\n"
    "        \"year\": item.get(\"year\"),\n"
    "        \"resource_type\": str(item.get(\"type\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "        \"resource_id\": str(item.get(\"id\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "    }\n"
    "    output: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "\n"
    "    pan = extract_panlist_v1106(payload)\n"
    "    for index, value in enumerate(pan.get(\"url\") or []):\n"
    "        url = html.unescape(str(value or \"\").strip())\n"
    "        if not url:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        output.append({\n"
    "            **common,\n"
    "            \"url\": url,\n"
    "            \"name\": str(_parallel_value(pan, \"name\", index, title) or title).strip(),\n"
    "            \"pan_type\": _parallel_value(pan, \"type\", index, None),\n"
    "            \"passcode\": str(_parallel_value(pan, \"p\", index, \"\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "            \"pan_id\": str(_parallel_value(pan, \"id\", index, \"\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "            \"resource_kind\": \"pan\",\n"
    "        })\n"
    "\n"
    "    down = extract_downlist_v1106(payload)\n"
    "    size = max((len(value) for value in down.values()), default=0)\n"
    "    for index in range(size):\n"
    "        kind = _safe_int(_parallel_value(down, \"k\", index, -1), -1)\n"
    "        if kind != 0:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        row_title = str(_parallel_value(down, \"t\", index, title) or title).strip()\n"
    "        uri = _build_magnet(_parallel_value(down, \"m\", index, \"\"), row_title)\n"
    "        if not uri:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        output.append({\n"
    "            **common,\n"
    "            \"url\": uri,\n"
    "            \"name\": row_title,\n"
    "            \"resource_kind\": \"magnet\",\n"
    "            \"bt_detail_id\": str(_parallel_value(down, \"u\", index, \"\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "            \"size_text\": str(_parallel_value(down, \"s\", index, \"\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "            \"seeds\": _safe_int(_parallel_value(down, \"e\", index, -1), -1),\n"
    "            \"source_label\": str(_parallel_value(down, \"p\", index, \"\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "        })\n"
    "\n"
    "    # 为未来字段漂移兜底：如果 downurl 直接嵌入完整 Magnet/ED2K，也一起收进统一候选。\n"
    "    for found in _find_links(payload, name=title, provider=\"viewing\"):\n"
    "        uri = str(found.get(\"uri\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not uri:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        output.append({\n"
    "            **common,\n"
    "            \"url\": uri,\n"
    "            \"name\": str(found.get(\"name\") or title).strip(),\n"
    "            \"resource_kind\": str(found.get(\"type\") or \"embedded\"),\n"
    "        })\n"
    "\n"
    "    deduped: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "    seen = set()\n"
    "    for row in output:\n"
    "        key = (str(row.get(\"url\") or \"\").strip(), str(row.get(\"passcode\") or \"\").strip())\n"
    "        if not key[0] or key in seen:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        seen.add(key)\n"
    "        deduped.append(row)\n"
    "    return deduped\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _json_refresh_required(response: requests.Response) -> bool:\n"
    "    text = str(getattr(response, \"text\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "    if not text or not text.startswith(\"{\"):\n"
    "        return False\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        payload = response.json()\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            payload = json.loads(text)\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "    return isinstance(payload, dict) and _safe_int(payload.get(\"refresh\"), 0) == 1\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _login_message(payload: Any, response: requests.Response) -> str:\n"
    "    if isinstance(payload, dict):\n"
    "        for key in (\"msg\", \"message\", \"error\"):\n"
    "            value = str(payload.get(key) or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if value:\n"
    "                return value[:300]\n"
    "    return str(getattr(response, \"text\", \"\") or f\"HTTP {getattr(response, 'status_code', 0)}\")[:300]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _captcha_required(payload: Any, message: str) -> bool:\n"
    "    lowered = str(message or \"\").lower()\n"
    "    if any(token.lower() in lowered for token in _CAPTCHA_HINTS):\n"
    "        return True\n"
    "    if isinstance(payload, dict):\n"
    "        for key in (\"captcha\", \"need_captcha\", \"captcha_required\"):\n"
    "            if payload.get(key):\n"
    "                return True\n"
    "    return False\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _bad_credentials(message: str) -> bool:\n"
    "    lowered = str(message or \"\").lower()\n"
    "    return any(token.lower() in lowered for token in _BAD_CREDENTIAL_HINTS)\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaGyingProtocolV1106Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"HAR 对齐后的搜索、downurl、登录态与资源解析实现。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260902-r17\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_state(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"在 Hardening/Failover 读取前先把历史数值状态规整，避免旧坏值拖垮节点选择。\"\"\"\n"
    "        state = dict(super()._gying_state() or {})\n"
    "        state[\"discovered_at\"] = _safe_float(state.get(\"discovered_at\"), 0.0)\n"
    "        nodes = state.get(\"nodes\") or {}\n"
    "        clean_nodes: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        if isinstance(nodes, dict):\n"
    "            for key, raw in nodes.items():\n"
    "                row = dict(raw) if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}\n"
    "                row[\"last_ok_ts\"] = _safe_float(row.get(\"last_ok_ts\"), 0.0)\n"
    "                row[\"last_checked_ts\"] = _safe_float(row.get(\"last_checked_ts\"), 0.0)\n"
    "                clean_nodes[str(key)] = row\n"
    "        state[\"nodes\"] = clean_nodes\n"
    "        discovered = state.get(\"discovered_nodes\")\n"
    "        state[\"discovered_nodes\"] = [str(value) for value in discovered if str(value or \"\").strip()] if isinstance(discovered, list) else []\n"
    "        return state\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_request(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        session: requests.Session,\n"
    "        node: str,\n"
    "        method: str,\n"
    "        url: str,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        retry_challenge: bool = True,\n"
    "        **kwargs: Any,\n"
    "    ) -> requests.Response:\n"
    "        response = super()._gying_request(\n"
    "            session,\n"
    "            node,\n"
    "            method,\n"
    "            url,\n"
    "            retry_challenge=retry_challenge,\n"
    "            **kwargs,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if not retry_challenge or not _json_refresh_required(response):\n"
    "            return response\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            self._gying_solve_challenge(session, node, response)\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "                \"观影要求动态浏览器验证，自动 PoW 未完成；可在浏览器登录后复制该节点 Cookie 到插件\"\n"
    "            ) from err\n"
    "        retried = super()._gying_request(\n"
    "            session,\n"
    "            node,\n"
    "            method,\n"
    "            url,\n"
    "            retry_challenge=False,\n"
    "            **kwargs,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if _json_refresh_required(retried):\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影动态浏览器验证已执行，但原请求仍要求 refresh 验证\")\n"
    "        return retried\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _configured_cookie_available(self, session: requests.Session, node: str) -> bool:\n"
    "        configured = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_cookie\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not configured or not len(session.cookies):\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        preferred = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_base_url\", \"\") or \"\").rstrip(\"/\")\n"
    "        return not preferred or str(node or \"\").rstrip(\"/\") == preferred\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _saved_authenticated_cookie(self, session: requests.Session, node: str) -> bool:\n"
    "        if not len(session.cookies):\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            row = dict(((self._gying_state().get(\"nodes\") or {}).get(node) or {}))\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            row = {}\n"
    "        return str(row.get(\"login_mode\") or \"\") in _AUTH_COOKIE_MODES\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_login_password(self, session: requests.Session, node: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        username = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_username\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        password = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_password\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        if not (username and password):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": False,\n"
    "                \"mode\": \"credentials_missing\",\n"
    "                \"message\": \"观影登录态已失效，且未配置用户名/密码；请更新 Cookie 或配置账号\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "        login_url = node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/user/login\"\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            self._gying_request(\n"
    "                session,\n"
    "                node,\n"
    "                \"GET\",\n"
    "                login_url,\n"
    "                headers={\"Referer\": node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/\"},\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            # 登录页预热失败不直接判死，POST 仍可能可用。\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "\n"
    "        response = self._gying_request(\n"
    "            session,\n"
    "            node,\n"
    "            \"POST\",\n"
    "            login_url,\n"
    "            data={\n"
    "                \"code\": \"\",\n"
    "                \"siteid\": \"1\",\n"
    "                \"dosubmit\": \"1\",\n"
    "                \"cookietime\": \"10506240\",\n"
    "                \"username\": username,\n"
    "                \"password\": password,\n"
    "            },\n"
    "            headers={\n"
    "                \"Origin\": node.rstrip(\"/\"),\n"
    "                \"Referer\": login_url,\n"
    "                \"Accept\": \"*/*\",\n"
    "            },\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            result = response.json()\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                result = json.loads(response.text or \"{}\")\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                result = {}\n"
    "\n"
    "        code = result.get(\"code\") if isinstance(result, dict) else None\n"
    "        if code in (200, \"200\"):\n"
    "            self._gying_persist_session(\n"
    "                node,\n"
    "                session,\n"
    "                status=\"ok\",\n"
    "                login_mode=\"password\",\n"
    "                login_at=self._now_text(),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return {\"success\": True, \"mode\": \"password\", \"message\": \"观影账号登录成功\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "        message = _login_message(result, response)\n"
    "        if _captcha_required(result, message):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": False,\n"
    "                \"mode\": \"captcha_required\",\n"
    "                \"message\": \"观影账号需要网页图形验证码；请在浏览器完成登录后把该节点 Cookie 填入插件\",\n"
    "                \"status\": int(response.status_code or 0),\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        if _bad_credentials(message):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": False,\n"
    "                \"mode\": \"password\",\n"
    "                \"message\": f\"观影登录失败：{message}\",\n"
    "                \"status\": int(response.status_code or 0),\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": False,\n"
    "            \"mode\": \"password_pending\",\n"
    "            \"message\": f\"观影登录未完成：{message or '站点未返回成功状态；可能要求网页验证码'}\",\n"
    "            \"status\": int(response.status_code or 0),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_login(self, session: requests.Session, node: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        if self._configured_cookie_available(session, node):\n"
    "            return {\"success\": True, \"mode\": \"configured_cookie\", \"message\": \"复用已配置观影 Cookie\"}\n"
    "        if self._saved_authenticated_cookie(session, node):\n"
    "            return {\"success\": True, \"mode\": \"cookie_reuse\", \"message\": \"复用上次成功登录的观影 Cookie\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "        username = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_username\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        password = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_password\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        if username and password:\n"
    "            return self._gying_login_password(session, node)\n"
    "        return {\"success\": True, \"mode\": \"anonymous\", \"message\": \"未配置观影账号/Cookie，先尝试公开访问\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _downurl_login_flag(payload: Any) -> bool:\n"
    "        return isinstance(payload, dict) and bool(payload.get(\"login\"))\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _decode_downurl_response(self, response: requests.Response) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            payload = response.json()\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                payload = json.loads(response.text or \"{}\")\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\"观影 downurl 返回非 JSON\") from err\n"
    "        return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_detail(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        session: requests.Session,\n"
    "        node: str,\n"
    "        resource_type: str,\n"
    "        resource_id: str,\n"
    "        referer: str,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        url = (\n"
    "            f\"{node.rstrip('/')}/res/downurl/\"\n"
    "            f\"{quote(str(resource_type or '').strip())}/{quote(str(resource_id or '').strip())}\"\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        response = self._gying_request(\n"
    "            session,\n"
    "            node,\n"
    "            \"GET\",\n"
    "            url,\n"
    "            headers={\"Referer\": referer},\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        payload = self._decode_downurl_response(response)\n"
    "        needs_login = self._gying_login_required(response) or self._downurl_login_flag(payload)\n"
    "        if needs_login:\n"
    "            login = self._gying_login_password(session, node)\n"
    "            if not login.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(str(login.get(\"message\") or \"观影登录失效\"))\n"
    "            response = self._gying_request(\n"
    "                session,\n"
    "                node,\n"
    "                \"GET\",\n"
    "                url,\n"
    "                headers={\"Referer\": referer},\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            payload = self._decode_downurl_response(response)\n"
    "\n"
    "        if response.status_code >= 400:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(f\"观影 downurl HTTP {response.status_code}\")\n"
    "        if self._downurl_login_flag(payload) or payload.get(\"code\") in (403, \"403\"):\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影 downurl 仍处于未登录状态；请更新 Cookie 或在网页重新登录\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        code = payload.get(\"code\")\n"
    "        has_resources = bool(extract_panlist_v1106(payload).get(\"url\")) or bool(\n"
    "            extract_downlist_v1106(payload).get(\"m\")\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if code not in (None, 200, \"200\") and not has_resources:\n"
    "            message = str(payload.get(\"msg\") or payload.get(\"message\") or \"downurl 返回失败状态\")[:300]\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(f\"观影 downurl 失败：{message}\")\n"
    "        return payload\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_raw_results(self, keyword: str, force: bool = False) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        keyword = \" \".join(str(keyword or \"\").split())\n"
    "        if not keyword:\n"
    "            return [], {\"provider\": \"viewing\", \"success\": False, \"message\": \"观影搜索关键词为空\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "        cache_key_getter = getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache_key_v11223\", None)\n"
    "        cache_key = cache_key_getter(keyword) if callable(cache_key_getter) else keyword\n"
    "        cached = dict(self._gying_search_cache.get(cache_key) or {})\n"
    "        cache_age = time.time() - _safe_float(cached.get(\"ts\"), 0.0) if cached else 0.0\n"
    "        if cached and not force and cache_age < 120:\n"
    "            cached_state = dict(cached.get(\"state\") or {})\n"
    "            cached_state.update({\n"
    "                \"transport\": \"cache\",\n"
    "                \"cache_hit\": True,\n"
    "                \"cache_age_seconds\": round(max(0.0, cache_age), 3),\n"
    "                \"search_http_requests\": 0,\n"
    "                \"downurl_attempts\": 0,\n"
    "                \"downurl_success\": 0,\n"
    "                \"downurl_failures\": 0,\n"
    "                \"message\": (\n"
    "                    f\"观影搜索缓存复用：缓存年龄 {max(0.0, cache_age):.1f}s · \"\n"
    "                    f\"本轮未发起 /search 或 downurl 网络请求\"\n"
    "                ),\n"
    "            })\n"
    "            return list(cached.get(\"rows\") or []), cached_state\n"
    "\n"
    "        session, login = self._viewing_session()\n"
    "        if not login.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "            return [], {\n"
    "                \"provider\": \"viewing\",\n"
    "                **dict(login or {}),\n"
    "                \"transport\": \"network\",\n"
    "                \"cache_hit\": False,\n"
    "                \"search_http_requests\": 0,\n"
    "                \"downurl_attempts\": 0,\n"
    "                \"downurl_success\": 0,\n"
    "                \"downurl_failures\": 0,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        node = str(\n"
    "            login.get(\"node\")\n"
    "            or getattr(self, \"_gying_active_node\", \"\")\n"
    "            or getattr(self, \"_viewing_base_url\", \"\")\n"
    "            or \"\"\n"
    "        ).rstrip(\"/\")\n"
    "        if not node:\n"
    "            return [], {\n"
    "                \"provider\": \"viewing\",\n"
    "                \"success\": False,\n"
    "                \"message\": \"观影没有可用内容节点\",\n"
    "                \"transport\": \"network\",\n"
    "                \"cache_hit\": False,\n"
    "                \"search_http_requests\": 0,\n"
    "                \"downurl_attempts\": 0,\n"
    "                \"downurl_success\": 0,\n"
    "                \"downurl_failures\": 0,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "        search_http_requests = 0\n"
    "        downurl_attempts = 0\n"
    "        downurl_success = 0\n"
    "        downurl_failures = 0\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            query = quote(keyword, safe=\"\")\n"
    "            search_variants = [\n"
    "                (\"browser\", f\"{node}/search?q={query}&type=&mode=1\"),\n"
    "                (\"legacy\", f\"{node}/search?q={query}&type=0&mode=2\"),\n"
    "            ]\n"
    "            cards: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "            response: requests.Response | None = None\n"
    "            search_mode = \"browser\"\n"
    "            for mode, search_url in search_variants:\n"
    "                search_http_requests += 1\n"
    "                current = self._gying_request(\n"
    "                    session,\n"
    "                    node,\n"
    "                    \"GET\",\n"
    "                    search_url,\n"
    "                    headers={\"Referer\": node + \"/\"},\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if self._gying_login_required(current):\n"
    "                    relogin = self._gying_login_password(session, node)\n"
    "                    if not relogin.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "                        raise RuntimeError(str(relogin.get(\"message\") or \"观影登录失效\"))\n"
    "                    search_http_requests += 1\n"
    "                    current = self._gying_request(\n"
    "                        session,\n"
    "                        node,\n"
    "                        \"GET\",\n"
    "                        search_url,\n"
    "                        headers={\"Referer\": node + \"/\"},\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                if current.status_code >= 400:\n"
    "                    if mode == \"browser\":\n"
    "                        continue\n"
    "                    raise RuntimeError(f\"观影搜索 HTTP {current.status_code}\")\n"
    "                parsed_ok, parsed = _parse_search_payload_result_v11223(current.text or \"\")\n"
    "                if not parsed_ok:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                response = current\n"
    "                search_mode = mode\n"
    "                cards = parsed\n"
    "                break\n"
    "\n"
    "            if response is None:\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\"观影搜索没有得到有效响应\")\n"
    "\n"
    "            rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "            limit = max(1, min(_safe_int(getattr(self, \"_provider_result_limit\", 20), 20), 100))\n"
    "            selector = getattr(self, \"_gying_select_detail_cards_v11223\", None)\n"
    "            detail_cards = list(selector(keyword, cards, limit) if callable(selector) else cards[:limit])\n"
    "            target_getter = getattr(self, \"_gying_target_subscribe_v11223\", None)\n"
    "            target_scoped = bool(target_getter()) if callable(target_getter) else False\n"
    "            for item in detail_cards:\n"
    "                resource_type = str(item.get(\"type\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                resource_id = str(item.get(\"id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                if not resource_type or not resource_id:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                detail_referer = f\"{node}/{quote(resource_type)}/{quote(resource_id)}\"\n"
    "                downurl_attempts += 1\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    payload = self._gying_detail(\n"
    "                        session,\n"
    "                        node,\n"
    "                        resource_type,\n"
    "                        resource_id,\n"
    "                        detail_referer,\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                except Exception as err:\n"
    "                    downurl_failures += 1\n"
    "                    logger = getattr(self, \"_gying_obs_log\", None)\n"
    "                    if callable(logger):\n"
    "                        logger(\n"
    "                            \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                            \"资源详情跳过：标题=%s 类型=%s 错误=%s\",\n"
    "                            str(item.get(\"title\") or \"-\")[:80],\n"
    "                            resource_type[:24],\n"
    "                            str(err)[:180],\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                downurl_success += 1\n"
    "                rows.extend(extract_resource_rows_v1106(payload, item))\n"
    "\n"
    "            deduped: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "            seen = set()\n"
    "            for row in rows:\n"
    "                key = (str(row.get(\"url\") or \"\").strip(), str(row.get(\"passcode\") or \"\").strip())\n"
    "                if not key[0] or key in seen:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                seen.add(key)\n"
    "                deduped.append(row)\n"
    "\n"
    "            pan_count = sum(1 for row in deduped if row.get(\"resource_kind\") == \"pan\")\n"
    "            magnet_count = sum(1 for row in deduped if str(row.get(\"url\") or \"\").lower().startswith(\"magnet:?\"))\n"
    "            ed2k_count = sum(1 for row in deduped if str(row.get(\"url\") or \"\").lower().startswith(\"ed2k://|file|\"))\n"
    "            xunlei_count = sum(1 for row in deduped if \"pan.xunlei.com/s/\" in str(row.get(\"url\") or \"\").lower())\n"
    "\n"
    "            self._gying_persist_session(\n"
    "                node,\n"
    "                session,\n"
    "                status=\"ok\",\n"
    "                login_mode=str(login.get(\"mode\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                last_search_at=self._now_text(),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            state = {\n"
    "                \"provider\": \"viewing\",\n"
    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"search_complete\": True,\n"
    "                \"node\": node,\n"
    "                \"login_mode\": login.get(\"mode\"),\n"
    "                \"cards\": len(cards),\n"
    "                \"raw_cards\": len(cards),\n"
    "                \"matched_cards\": len(detail_cards) if target_scoped else len(cards),\n"
    "                \"detail_cards\": len(detail_cards),\n"
    "                \"target_scoped\": target_scoped,\n"
    "                \"target_match\": bool(detail_cards) if target_scoped else None,\n"
    "                \"resources\": len(deduped),\n"
    "                \"pan_resources\": pan_count,\n"
    "                \"magnet_resources\": magnet_count,\n"
    "                \"ed2k_resources\": ed2k_count,\n"
    "                \"xunlei_resources\": xunlei_count,\n"
    "                \"search_mode\": search_mode,\n"
    "                \"transport\": \"network\",\n"
    "                \"cache_hit\": False,\n"
    "                \"cache_age_seconds\": 0.0,\n"
    "                \"search_http_requests\": search_http_requests,\n"
    "                \"downurl_attempts\": downurl_attempts,\n"
    "                \"downurl_success\": downurl_success,\n"
    "                \"downurl_failures\": downurl_failures,\n"
    "                \"message\": (\n"
    "                    f\"观影真实网络搜索完成：/search请求 {search_http_requests} · \"\n"
    "                    f\"downurl尝试 {downurl_attempts} 成功 {downurl_success} 失败 {downurl_failures} · \"\n"
    "                    f\"模糊卡片 {len(cards)} · 当前媒体卡片 \"\n"
    "                    f\"{len(detail_cards) if target_scoped else '未限定'} · 已展开 {len(detail_cards)} · \"\n"
    "                    f\"未核验链接：网盘 {pan_count} · 迅雷 {xunlei_count} · \"\n"
    "                    f\"Magnet {magnet_count} · ED2K {ed2k_count}\"\n"
    "                ),\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            self._gying_search_cache[cache_key] = {\"ts\": time.time(), \"rows\": deduped, \"state\": state}\n"
    "            return deduped, state\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._gying_mark_node(node, \"search_error\", str(err))\n"
    "            return [], {\n"
    "                \"provider\": \"viewing\",\n"
    "                \"success\": False,\n"
    "                \"search_complete\": False,\n"
    "                \"node\": node,\n"
    "                \"login_mode\": login.get(\"mode\"),\n"
    "                \"transport\": \"network\",\n"
    "                \"cache_hit\": False,\n"
    "                \"cache_age_seconds\": 0.0,\n"
    "                \"search_http_requests\": search_http_requests,\n"
    "                \"downurl_attempts\": downurl_attempts,\n"
    "                \"downurl_success\": downurl_success,\n"
    "                \"downurl_failures\": downurl_failures,\n"
    "                \"message\": str(err)[:400],\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _search_viewing_xunlei(self, keyword: str):\n"
    "        output, state = super()._search_viewing_xunlei(keyword)\n"
    "        raw_rows, _ = self._gying_raw_results(keyword)\n"
    "        passcodes: Dict[str, str] = {}\n"
    "        for item in raw_rows:\n"
    "            url = html.unescape(str(item.get(\"url\") or \"\"))\n"
    "            match = _XUNLEI_RE.search(url)\n"
    "            if not match:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            parsed = urlparse(match.group(0).rstrip(\").]】）}\"))\n"
    "            share = re.search(r\"^/s/([^/?#]+)\", parsed.path or \"\", re.I)\n"
    "            if not share:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            code = str(item.get(\"passcode\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if not code:\n"
    "                query = parse_qs(parsed.query or \"\")\n"
    "                for key in (\"pwd\", \"passcode\", \"pass_code\", \"code\"):\n"
    "                    values = query.get(key) or []\n"
    "                    if values and str(values[0] or \"\").strip():\n"
    "                        code = str(values[0]).strip()\n"
    "                        break\n"
    "            if code:\n"
    "                passcodes[share.group(1).strip()] = code\n"
    "        for row in output or []:\n"
    "            share_id = str((row or {}).get(\"share_id\") or (row or {}).get(\"identity\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if share_id and not str((row or {}).get(\"passcode\") or \"\").strip() and share_id in passcodes:\n"
    "                row[\"passcode\"] = passcodes[share_id]\n"
    "        return output, state\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\n"
    "    \"GuangYaGyingProtocolV1106Mixin\",\n"
    "    \"extract_panlist_v1106\",\n"
    "    \"extract_downlist_v1106\",\n"
    "    \"extract_resource_rows_v1106\",\n"
    "]\n"
    "\n"
)

_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["xunlei_hardening_v193"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.9.3 迅雷分享秒传完整性收口。\n"
    "\n"
    "参考用户提供的迅雷->光鸭秒传脚本补齐真实运行中最容易失败的两层：\n"
    "- captcha_token 与 device/client 是一组运行时身份；没有可复用 token 时尝试 shield/captcha/init；\n"
    "- 新版 share/detail 可能隐藏 hash，file_info 不可用时用同 parent_id 的 detail(with_audit=false) 补 GCID。\n"
    "\n"
    "匿名分享接口始终不携带 Authorization；只取得分享元数据/少量 CID 样本并尝试光鸭秒传，\n"
    "不增加 OSS PUT、本地整文件中转或 MoviePilot 下载器路径。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"

    "XUNLEI_BROWSER_PACKAGE = \"com.xunlei.browser\"\n"
    "XUNLEI_BROWSER_REDIRECT = \"xlaccsdk01://xunlei.com/callback?state=harbor\"\n"
    "_XUNLEI_CAPTCHA_SALTS = (\n"
    "    \"uWRwO7gPfdPB/0NfPtfQO+71\",\n"

    "    \"ThTWPG5eC0UBqlbQ+04nZAptqGCdpv9o55A\",\n"
    ")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def build_xunlei_captcha_signature(\n"
    "    client_id: str,\n"
    "    client_version: str,\n"
    "    package_name: str,\n"
    "    device_id: str,\n"
    "    timestamp_ms: Optional[int] = None,\n"
    ") -> tuple[str, str]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"复现迅雷浏览器 captcha_sign：连续九轮 MD5，便于无浏览器环境初始化 token。\"\"\"\n"
    "    timestamp = str(int(timestamp_ms if timestamp_ms is not None else time.time() * 1000))\n"
    "    content = f\"{client_id}{client_version}{package_name}{device_id}{timestamp}\"\n"
    "    for salt in _XUNLEI_CAPTCHA_SALTS:\n"
    "        content = hashlib.md5((content + salt).encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()  # noqa: S324 - 协议签名，不用于密码学安全\n"
    "    return timestamp, \"1.\" + content\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaXunleiHardeningMixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"最终迅雷分享身份、captcha 初始化与 hash 回退层。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260901-r8\"\n"
    "    _xunlei_runtime_client_id = \"\"\n"
    "    _xunlei_runtime_device_id = \"\"\n"
    "    _xunlei_captcha_status: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        config = dict(config or {})\n"
    "        super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "        self._xunlei_runtime_client_id = str(getattr(self, \"_xunlei_client_id\", \"\") or XUNLEI_DEFAULT_CLIENT_ID).strip()\n"
    "        self._xunlei_captcha_status = {}\n"
    "\n"
    "        configured_device = str(getattr(self, \"_xunlei_device_id\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not configured_device:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                init_payload = json.loads(str(getattr(self, \"_xunlei_captcha_init_json\", \"\") or \"{}\"))\n"
    "                if isinstance(init_payload, dict):\n"
    "                    configured_device = str(init_payload.get(\"device_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "\n"
    "        state = self.get_data(\"xunlei_runtime_state\") or {}\n"
    "        if not isinstance(state, dict):\n"
    "            state = {}\n"
    "        persisted_device = str(state.get(\"device_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if configured_device:\n"
    "            runtime_device = configured_device\n"
    "        elif persisted_device:\n"
    "            runtime_device = persisted_device\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            runtime_device = secrets.token_hex(16)\n"
    "        self._xunlei_runtime_device_id = runtime_device\n"
    "        state.update({\"schema\": 1, \"device_id\": runtime_device, \"updated_at\": self._now_text()})\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"xunlei_runtime_state\", state)\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 手工 captcha_token 通常与浏览器 device_id 绑定。没有对应 device 时不要带着一个随机\n"
    "        # device 去请求；改为清空运行态 token，让下方自动初始化创建一对一致的身份。\n"
    "        if str(getattr(self, \"_xunlei_captcha_token\", \"\") or \"\").strip() and not configured_device:\n"
    "            self._xunlei_runtime_captcha_token = \"\"\n"
    "            self._xunlei_captcha_status = {\n"
    "                \"mode\": \"manual_token_missing_device\",\n"
    "                \"success\": False,\n"
    "                \"message\": \"已填写迅雷 captcha_token 但没有对应 Device ID，运行时将优先尝试自动初始化\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_runtime_state_save(self, **values: Any) -> None:\n"
    "        state = self.get_data(\"xunlei_runtime_state\") or {}\n"
    "        if not isinstance(state, dict):\n"
    "            state = {}\n"
    "        state.update({\"schema\": 1, \"device_id\": self._xunlei_runtime_device_id, \"updated_at\": self._now_text(), **values})\n"
    "        # 这里只保存诊断和稳定 device；captcha_token 本身不写进公开状态数据。\n"
    "        state.pop(\"captcha_token\", None)\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"xunlei_runtime_state\", state)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _refresh_xunlei_captcha(self, action: str) -> str:\n"
    "        # 用户从浏览器复制的 init 请求体最贴近实际浏览器，优先复用父实现。\n"
    "        if str(getattr(self, \"_xunlei_captcha_init_json\", \"\") or \"\").strip():\n"
    "            token = str(super()._refresh_xunlei_captcha(action) or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if token:\n"
    "                self._xunlei_runtime_client_id = str(getattr(self, \"_xunlei_client_id\", \"\") or XUNLEI_DEFAULT_CLIENT_ID)\n"
    "                self._xunlei_captcha_status = {\"mode\": \"configured_init\", \"success\": True, \"message\": \"迅雷 captcha_token 已从配置请求体刷新\"}\n"
    "                self._xunlei_runtime_state_save(captcha_mode=\"configured_init\", captcha_ok=True)\n"
    "                return token\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 无浏览器环境下使用公开迅雷浏览器 profile 生成 captcha_sign。先试插件配置 client，\n"
    "        # 再试已验证可用于匿名分享检查的浏览器 client；成功后后续请求必须沿用同一 client/device。\n"
    "        profiles = []\n"
    "        configured_client = str(getattr(self, \"_xunlei_client_id\", \"\") or XUNLEI_DEFAULT_CLIENT_ID).strip()\n"
    "        if configured_client:\n"
    "            profiles.append(configured_client)\n"
    "        if XUNLEI_BROWSER_CLIENT_ID not in profiles:\n"
    "            profiles.append(XUNLEI_BROWSER_CLIENT_ID)\n"
    "\n"
    "        session = self._xunlei_session()\n"
    "        last_error = \"\"\n"
    "        for client_id in profiles:\n"
    "            timestamp, signature = build_xunlei_captcha_signature(\n"
    "                client_id,\n"
    "                XUNLEI_BROWSER_CLIENT_VERSION,\n"
    "                XUNLEI_BROWSER_PACKAGE,\n"
    "                self._xunlei_runtime_device_id,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            payload = {\n"
    "                \"action\": str(action or \"get:/drive/v1/share\"),\n"
    "                \"captcha_token\": \"\",\n"
    "                \"client_id\": client_id,\n"
    "                \"device_id\": self._xunlei_runtime_device_id,\n"
    "                \"meta\": {\n"
    "                    \"timestamp\": timestamp,\n"
    "                    \"captcha_sign\": signature,\n"
    "                    \"client_version\": XUNLEI_BROWSER_CLIENT_VERSION,\n"
    "                    \"package_name\": XUNLEI_BROWSER_PACKAGE,\n"
    "                },\n"
    "                \"redirect_uri\": XUNLEI_BROWSER_REDIRECT,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                response = session.post(\n"
    "                    XUNLEI_CAPTCHA_INIT,\n"
    "                    json=payload,\n"
    "                    headers={\n"
    "                        \"Accept\": \"application/json;charset=UTF-8\",\n"
    "                        \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n"
    "                        \"x-device-id\": self._xunlei_runtime_device_id,\n"
    "                        \"x-client-id\": client_id,\n"
    "                        \"x-client-version\": XUNLEI_BROWSER_CLIENT_VERSION,\n"
    "                    },\n"
    "                    timeout=int(getattr(self, \"_provider_timeout\", 15) or 15),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                body = response.json() if response.content else {}\n"
    "                token = str((body or {}).get(\"captcha_token\") or \"\").strip() if isinstance(body, dict) else \"\"\n"
    "                verify_url = str((body or {}).get(\"url\") or \"\").strip() if isinstance(body, dict) else \"\"\n"
    "                if token:\n"
    "                    self._xunlei_runtime_captcha_token = token\n"
    "                    self._xunlei_runtime_client_id = client_id\n"
    "                    self._xunlei_captcha_status = {\"mode\": \"signed_init\", \"success\": True, \"message\": \"迅雷 captcha_token 自动初始化成功\"}\n"
    "                    self._xunlei_runtime_state_save(captcha_mode=\"signed_init\", captcha_ok=True, client_profile=client_id)\n"
    "                    return token\n"
    "                if verify_url:\n"
    "                    last_error = \"迅雷 captcha/init 要求额外人工验证\"\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    last_error = str((body or {}).get(\"error_description\") or (body or {}).get(\"error\") or f\"HTTP {response.status_code}\")[:240]\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                last_error = str(err)[:240]\n"
    "\n"
    "        self._xunlei_captcha_status = {\"mode\": \"signed_init\", \"success\": False, \"message\": last_error or \"迅雷 captcha_token 自动初始化失败\"}\n"
    "        self._xunlei_runtime_state_save(captcha_mode=\"signed_init\", captcha_ok=False, captcha_error=last_error[:240])\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_headers(self, action: str, *, refresh: bool = False) -> Dict[str, str]:\n"
    "        token = \"\" if refresh else str(getattr(self, \"_xunlei_runtime_captcha_token\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        configured_token = str(getattr(self, \"_xunlei_captcha_token\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        configured_device = str(getattr(self, \"_xunlei_device_id\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not token and configured_token and configured_device:\n"
    "            token = configured_token\n"
    "            self._xunlei_runtime_client_id = str(getattr(self, \"_xunlei_client_id\", \"\") or XUNLEI_DEFAULT_CLIENT_ID)\n"
    "        if not token:\n"
    "            token = self._refresh_xunlei_captcha(action)\n"
    "\n"
    "        headers = {\n"
    "            \"Accept\": \"application/json;charset=UTF-8\",\n"
    "            \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n"
    "            \"x-client-id\": str(self._xunlei_runtime_client_id or getattr(self, \"_xunlei_client_id\", \"\") or XUNLEI_DEFAULT_CLIENT_ID),\n"
    "            \"x-device-id\": str(self._xunlei_runtime_device_id or configured_device),\n"
    "            \"x-guid\": str(self._xunlei_runtime_device_id or configured_device),\n"
    "            \"x-client-version\": XUNLEI_BROWSER_CLIENT_VERSION,\n"
    "            \"Referer\": \"https://pan.xunlei.com/\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        if token:\n"
    "            headers[\"x-captcha-token\"] = token\n"
    "        # 分享链接采用匿名接口，绝不能把用户自己的迅雷 Bearer 带到他人分享请求。\n"
    "        headers.pop(\"Authorization\", None)\n"
    "        headers.pop(\"authorization\", None)\n"
    "        return headers\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _merge_xunlei_file(base: Dict[str, Any], extra: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        merged = dict(base or {})\n"
    "        for key in (\"gcid\", \"md5\", \"cid\", \"download_url\", \"size\", \"name\", \"path\", \"parent_id\"):\n"
    "            value = extra.get(key)\n"
    "            if value not in (None, \"\", 0):\n"
    "                merged[key] = value\n"
    "        return merged\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_detail_hash_fallback(self, share_id: str, pass_code_token: str, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"参考脚本：同 parent_id 再查 with_audit=false，按 file id 精确补 hash。\"\"\"\n"
    "        file_id = str(row.get(\"id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not file_id:\n"
    "            return row\n"
    "        parent_id = str(row.get(\"parent_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            body = self._xunlei_get(\n"
    "                \"/drive/v1/share/detail\",\n"
    "                {\n"
    "                    \"share_id\": share_id,\n"
    "                    \"parent_id\": parent_id,\n"
    "                    \"pass_code_token\": pass_code_token,\n"
    "                    \"limit\": 200,\n"
    "                    \"with_audit\": \"false\",\n"
    "                    \"usage\": \"CONSUME\",\n"
    "                },\n"
    "                action=\"get:/drive/v1/share/detail\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return row\n"
    "        files = body.get(\"files\") or (body.get(\"data\") or {}).get(\"files\") or []\n"
    "        if not isinstance(files, list):\n"
    "            return row\n"
    "        prefix = str(row.get(\"path\") or \"\").rsplit(\"/\", 1)[0]\n"
    "        for raw in files:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(raw, dict) or str(raw.get(\"id\") or raw.get(\"file_id\") or \"\") != file_id:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            normalized = self._xunlei_normalize_file(raw, prefix, parent_id)\n"
    "            return self._merge_xunlei_file(row, normalized)\n"
    "        return row\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_file_info(self, share_id: str, pass_code_token: str, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        result = dict(row or {})\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            result = dict(super()._xunlei_file_info(share_id, pass_code_token, result) or result)\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        if not str(result.get(\"gcid\") or \"\").strip():\n"
    "            result = self._xunlei_detail_hash_fallback(share_id, pass_code_token, result)\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_xunlei_runtime_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        status = dict(self._xunlei_captcha_status or {})\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": True,\n"
    "            \"data\": {\n"
    "                \"flash_enabled\": bool(getattr(self, \"_xunlei_flash_enabled\", True)),\n"
    "                \"captcha_ready\": bool(getattr(self, \"_xunlei_runtime_captcha_token\", \"\")),\n"
    "                \"captcha_mode\": str(status.get(\"mode\") or \"not_initialized\"),\n"
    "                \"captcha_ok\": bool(status.get(\"success\")),\n"
    "                \"message\": str(status.get(\"message\") or \"\")[:240],\n"
    "                \"device_ready\": bool(self._xunlei_runtime_device_id),\n"
    "            },\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_api(self):\n"
    "        apis = list(super().get_api() or [])\n"
    "        if not any(str(item.get(\"path\") or \"\") == \"/xunlei/runtime/status\" for item in apis if isinstance(item, dict)):\n"
    "            apis.append({\n"
    "                \"path\": \"/xunlei/runtime/status\",\n"
    "                \"endpoint\": self.api_xunlei_runtime_status,\n"
    "                \"methods\": [\"GET\"],\n"
    "                \"summary\": \"查看迅雷秒传运行时状态\",\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        return apis\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaXunleiHardeningMixin\", \"build_xunlei_captcha_signature\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== xunlei_integrity_v1116.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["xunlei_integrity_v1116"] = (
    "\"\"\"迅雷秒传完整性校验与空占位清理。\n"
    "\n"
    "真实运行发现 get_res_center_token 会先创建 upload task；当后续\n"
    "check_can_flash_upload 未真正命中时，任务/占位文件可能仍短暂出现在目标目录。\n"
    "旧实现又只凭 get_info_by_task_id 返回 fileId 就判定秒传成功，没有校验 fileSize，\n"
    "因此 0B/尺寸异常占位存在被误判为成功的风险。\n"
    "\n"
    "本层坚持“迅雷只做秒传，不做 OSS/本地下载”：\n"
    "- get_res_center_token code=156 仍视为秒传命中信号，但必须再确认目标目录出现同名且大小一致的文件；\n"
    "- check_can_flash_upload 只有真实 40-hex CID 才进入探测；无真实 CID 时不做无意义 check；\n"
    "- canFlashUpload=true 或真实 CID check 返回可轮询 taskId 时，最终仍必须校验 fileId + 文件大小；\n"
    "- 失败时删除 upload task，并只清理由本次请求新产生的同名 0B/尺寸异常占位，不碰原有文件；\n"
    "- 同一批次相同 gcid/size/cid 只探测一次，避免不同分享重复制造 upload task。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple\n"
    "\n"
    "from .xunlei_flash_v193 import _HEX32_RE, _HEX40_RE, _safe_int\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaXunleiIntegrityV1116Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"在 ViewingDispatch 与旧 XunleiFlash 之间收紧秒传成功合同。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260902-r28\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 光鸭目录完整性检查\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _xunlei_parent_rows_v1116(self, parent_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        client, _ = self._get_guangya_runtime()\n"
    "        if not client:\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "        getter = getattr(client, \"get_file_list\", None)\n"
    "        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        if callable(getter):\n"
    "            for page in range(3):\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    body = getter(\n"
    "                        parent_id=str(parent_id or \"\"),\n"
    "                        page_size=100,\n"
    "                        order_by=3,\n"
    "                        sort_type=1,\n"
    "                        file_types=[],\n"
    "                        page=page,\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                except TypeError:\n"
    "                    try:\n"
    "                        body = getter(parent_id=str(parent_id or \"\"), page_size=100, page=page)\n"
    "                    except Exception:\n"
    "                        break\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    break\n"
    "                data = body.get(\"data\") if isinstance(body, dict) else None\n"
    "                listing = data.get(\"list\") if isinstance(data, dict) else None\n"
    "                page_rows = [dict(row) for row in (listing or []) if isinstance(row, dict)]\n"
    "                rows.extend(page_rows)\n"
    "                total = _safe_int(data.get(\"total\") if isinstance(data, dict) else 0, 0)\n"
    "                if not page_rows or len(page_rows) < 100 or (total and len(rows) >= total):\n"
    "                    break\n"
    "            return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 兼容少数旧版 GuangYaClient 没有公开 get_file_list 的运行态。\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            body = self._guangya_userres_request(\n"
    "                \"/userres/v1/file/get_file_list\",\n"
    "                {\n"
    "                    \"parentId\": str(parent_id or \"\"),\n"
    "                    \"page\": 0,\n"
    "                    \"pageSize\": 100,\n"
    "                    \"orderBy\": 3,\n"
    "                    \"sortType\": 1,\n"
    "                    \"fileTypes\": [],\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            data = body.get(\"data\") if isinstance(body, dict) else None\n"
    "            return [dict(row) for row in ((data or {}).get(\"list\") or []) if isinstance(row, dict)]\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _xunlei_remote_meta_v1116(row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[str, str, int, bool]:\n"
    "        file_id = str(row.get(\"fileId\") or row.get(\"fileid\") or row.get(\"file_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        name = str(row.get(\"fileName\") or row.get(\"name\") or row.get(\"file_name\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        size = _safe_int(row.get(\"fileSize\") or row.get(\"size\") or row.get(\"file_size\"), 0)\n"
    "        is_dir = row.get(\"resType\") == 2 or str(row.get(\"kind\") or \"\").lower() in {\"dir\", \"folder\", \"drive#folder\"}\n"
    "        return file_id, name, size, is_dir\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_snapshot_v1116(self, parent_id: str, name: str) -> Dict[str, int]:\n"
    "        snapshot: Dict[str, int] = {}\n"
    "        for row in self._xunlei_parent_rows_v1116(parent_id):\n"
    "            file_id, row_name, row_size, is_dir = self._xunlei_remote_meta_v1116(row)\n"
    "            if is_dir or not file_id or row_name != name:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            snapshot[file_id] = row_size\n"
    "        return snapshot\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_find_exact_file_v1116(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        parent_id: str,\n"
    "        name: str,\n"
    "        expected_size: int,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        for row in self._xunlei_parent_rows_v1116(parent_id):\n"
    "            file_id, row_name, row_size, is_dir = self._xunlei_remote_meta_v1116(row)\n"
    "            if is_dir or not file_id or row_name != name:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if row_size == int(expected_size or 0) and row_size > 0:\n"
    "                return {\n"
    "                    \"file_id\": file_id,\n"
    "                    \"name\": row_name,\n"
    "                    \"size\": row_size,\n"
    "                    \"row\": row,\n"
    "                }\n"
    "        return {}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_wait_exact_file_v1116(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        parent_id: str,\n"
    "        name: str,\n"
    "        expected_size: int,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        attempts: int = 10,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        for index in range(max(1, int(attempts or 1))):\n"
    "            found = self._xunlei_find_exact_file_v1116(parent_id, name, expected_size)\n"
    "            if found:\n"
    "                return found\n"
    "            if index + 1 < attempts:\n"
    "                time.sleep(0.2 if index < 4 else 0.35)\n"
    "        return {}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_delete_upload_task_v1116(self, task_id: str) -> bool:\n"
    "        task_id = str(task_id or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not task_id:\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            body = self._guangya_userres_request(\n"
    "                \"/userres/v1/file/delete_upload_task\",\n"
    "                {\"taskIds\": [task_id]},\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            code = body.get(\"code\") if isinstance(body, dict) else None\n"
    "            msg = str(body.get(\"msg\") or body.get(\"message\") or \"\") if isinstance(body, dict) else \"\"\n"
    "            ok = code in (None, 0, \"0\", 200, \"200\") or msg.lower() in {\"success\", \"ok\"}\n"
    "            if not ok:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【迅雷秒传】upload task 清理未确认：task=%s code=%s msg=%s\",\n"
    "                    task_id[:80],\n"
    "                    code,\n"
    "                    msg[:180] or \"-\",\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            return bool(ok)\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【迅雷秒传】upload task 清理异常：task=%s error=%s\",\n"
    "                task_id[:80],\n"
    "                str(err)[:220],\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_cleanup_placeholders_v1116(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        parent_id: str,\n"
    "        name: str,\n"
    "        expected_size: int,\n"
    "        before: Dict[str, int],\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        task_id: str = \"\",\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        self._xunlei_delete_upload_task_v1116(task_id)\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 删除前再看一次：如果任务其实已经落成完整文件，就绝不清理，并把它当成功。\n"
    "        exact = self._xunlei_find_exact_file_v1116(parent_id, name, expected_size)\n"
    "        if exact:\n"
    "            return exact\n"
    "\n"
    "        victims: List[str] = []\n"
    "        for row in self._xunlei_parent_rows_v1116(parent_id):\n"
    "            file_id, row_name, row_size, is_dir = self._xunlei_remote_meta_v1116(row)\n"
    "            if is_dir or not file_id or row_name != name or file_id in before:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            # 只碰本轮新出现且尺寸错误的同名文件，避免误删历史正常内容。\n"
    "            if row_size <= 0 or row_size != int(expected_size or 0):\n"
    "                victims.append(file_id)\n"
    "        if not victims:\n"
    "            return {}\n"
    "\n"
    "        client, _ = self._get_guangya_runtime()\n"
    "        delete_file = getattr(client, \"delete_file\", None) if client else None\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            if callable(delete_file):\n"
    "                response = delete_file(victims)\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                response = self._guangya_userres_request(\n"
    "                    \"/userres/v1/file/delete_file\",\n"
    "                    {\"fileIds\": victims},\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【迅雷秒传】已清理本轮新生成的空/尺寸异常占位：name=%s count=%s expected=%s response=%s\",\n"
    "                name[:180],\n"
    "                len(victims),\n"
    "                int(expected_size or 0),\n"
    "                str((response or {}).get(\"msg\") or (response or {}).get(\"code\") or \"submitted\")[:120] if isinstance(response, dict) else \"submitted\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【迅雷秒传】占位文件清理失败：name=%s count=%s error=%s\",\n"
    "                name[:180],\n"
    "                len(victims),\n"
    "                str(err)[:220],\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        return {}\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # task 最终完成必须验证大小，fileId 本身不再代表成功\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _xunlei_poll_task_integrity_v1116(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        task_id: str,\n"
    "        parent_id: str,\n"
    "        name: str,\n"
    "        expected_size: int,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        for index in range(30):\n"
    "            body = self._guangya_userres_request(\n"
    "                \"/userres/v1/file/get_info_by_task_id\",\n"
    "                {\"taskId\": str(task_id)},\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            data = body.get(\"data\") if isinstance(body, dict) else None\n"
    "            if isinstance(data, dict):\n"
    "                file_id = str(data.get(\"fileId\") or data.get(\"fileid\") or data.get(\"file_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                observed_size = _safe_int(data.get(\"fileSize\") or data.get(\"size\") or data.get(\"file_size\"), 0)\n"
    "                if file_id and observed_size == int(expected_size or 0) and observed_size > 0:\n"
    "                    return {\n"
    "                        \"file_id\": file_id,\n"
    "                        \"size\": observed_size,\n"
    "                        \"task_data\": data,\n"
    "                    }\n"
    "                if file_id:\n"
    "                    found = self._xunlei_find_exact_file_v1116(parent_id, name, expected_size)\n"
    "                    if found:\n"
    "                        return found\n"
    "            elif index % 5 == 4:\n"
    "                found = self._xunlei_find_exact_file_v1116(parent_id, name, expected_size)\n"
    "                if found:\n"
    "                    return found\n"
    "            time.sleep(0.25 if index < 4 else 0.45)\n"
    "\n"
    "        return self._xunlei_find_exact_file_v1116(parent_id, name, expected_size)\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 严格秒传：没有完整文件就绝不返回 success=True\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _rapid_transfer_xunlei_file(self, subscribe: Any, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        gcid = str(row.get(\"gcid\") or \"\").strip().upper()\n"
    "        size = _safe_int(row.get(\"size\"), 0)\n"
    "        if not _HEX40_RE.match(gcid) or size <= 0:\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"reason\": \"缺少有效 GCID 或文件大小\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "        md5 = str(row.get(\"md5\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "        if not _HEX32_RE.match(md5):\n"
    "            # 与用户已验证脚本保持兼容：只作为 get_res_center_token 索引兜底，\n"
    "            # 不把这 32 位值伪装成 CID 发送给 check_can_flash_upload。\n"
    "            md5 = gcid.lower()[:32]\n"
    "\n"
    "        cid_candidates: List[str] = []\n"
    "        raw_cid = str(row.get(\"cid\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "        if _HEX40_RE.match(raw_cid) and raw_cid != gcid.lower():\n"
    "            cid_candidates.append(raw_cid)\n"
    "        if row.get(\"download_url\"):\n"
    "            triple = self._xunlei_compute_triple_cid(str(row.get(\"download_url\") or \"\"), size)\n"
    "            if _HEX40_RE.match(triple) and triple.lower() != gcid.lower() and triple.lower() not in cid_candidates:\n"
    "                cid_candidates.append(triple.lower())\n"
    "\n"
    "        _, parent_id = self._xunlei_target_parent(\n"
    "            subscribe,\n"
    "            str(row.get(\"path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\"),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        name = str(\n"
    "            row.get(\"name\")\n"
    "            or str(row.get(\"path\") or \"\").rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1]\n"
    "            or \"file\"\n"
    "        ).strip()\n"
    "        before = self._xunlei_snapshot_v1116(parent_id, name)\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 同一预检批次内，相同内容 + 相同真实 CID 不重复创建 upload task。\n"
    "        dedupe_key = f\"{gcid}:{size}:{cid_candidates[0] if cid_candidates else '-'}\"\n"
    "        seen = getattr(self, \"_xunlei_flash_integrity_seen_v1116\", None)\n"
    "        if isinstance(seen, set):\n"
    "            if dedupe_key in seen:\n"
    "                return {\n"
    "                    \"success\": False,\n"
    "                    \"reason\": \"同批次相同 GCID/size/CID 已探测，跳过重复秒传请求\",\n"
    "                    \"duplicate\": True,\n"
    "                }\n"
    "            seen.add(dedupe_key)\n"
    "\n"
    "        token_candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for cid in cid_candidates:\n"
    "            token_candidates.extend([\n"
    "                {\"gcid\": gcid.lower(), \"cid\": cid.lower(), \"md5\": md5.lower(), \"fileSize\": size},\n"
    "                {\"gcid\": gcid.upper(), \"cid\": cid.upper(), \"md5\": md5.upper(), \"fileSize\": size},\n"
    "                {\"gcid\": gcid.lower(), \"cid\": cid.lower(), \"fileSize\": size},\n"
    "            ])\n"
    "        token_candidates.extend([\n"
    "            {\"gcid\": gcid.lower(), \"md5\": md5.lower(), \"fileSize\": size},\n"
    "            {\"gcid\": gcid.lower(), \"fileSize\": size},\n"
    "            {\"md5\": md5.lower(), \"fileSize\": size},\n"
    "        ])\n"
    "\n"
    "        task_id = \"\"\n"
    "        used_res: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n"
    "        last_error = \"\"\n"
    "        for res in token_candidates:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                body = self._guangya_userres_request(\n"
    "                    \"/userres/v1/get_res_center_token\",\n"
    "                    {\n"
    "                        \"capacity\": 2,\n"
    "                        \"res\": res,\n"
    "                        \"name\": name,\n"
    "                        \"parentId\": parent_id,\n"
    "                    },\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                last_error = str(err)[:240]\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            code = body.get(\"code\") if isinstance(body, dict) else None\n"
    "            if code in (156, \"156\"):\n"
    "                exact = self._xunlei_wait_exact_file_v1116(\n"
    "                    parent_id,\n"
    "                    name,\n"
    "                    size,\n"
    "                    attempts=12,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if exact:\n"
    "                    return {\n"
    "                        \"success\": True,\n"
    "                        \"instant\": True,\n"
    "                        \"file_id\": exact.get(\"file_id\"),\n"
    "                        \"verified_size\": exact.get(\"size\"),\n"
    "                        \"reason\": \"get_res_center_token code=156，且目标文件大小校验通过\",\n"
    "                    }\n"
    "                # 156 本应瞬时完成；看不到完整文件时不能凭业务码宣称成功。\n"
    "                self._xunlei_cleanup_placeholders_v1116(\n"
    "                    parent_id,\n"
    "                    name,\n"
    "                    size,\n"
    "                    before,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                return {\n"
    "                    \"success\": False,\n"
    "                    \"reason\": \"get_res_center_token 返回 156，但目标目录未确认到同名同大小文件；已拒绝空壳成功\",\n"
    "                }\n"
    "\n"
    "            candidate_task = self._guangya_task_id(body)\n"
    "            if candidate_task:\n"
    "                task_id = candidate_task\n"
    "                used_res = dict(res)\n"
    "                break\n"
    "            last_error = str(\n"
    "                body.get(\"msg\")\n"
    "                or body.get(\"message\")\n"
    "                or body.get(\"error\")\n"
    "                or code\n"
    "                or \"秒传令牌未命中\"\n"
    "            )[:240]\n"
    "\n"
    "        if not task_id:\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"reason\": last_error or \"光鸭未创建秒传任务\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 没有真实 CID 时，check_can_flash_upload 没有可靠命中条件；插件又明确禁止 OSS/普通上传，\n"
    "        # 因此立即清理任务并回退，不制造空轮询/空占位。\n"
    "        if not cid_candidates:\n"
    "            recovered = self._xunlei_cleanup_placeholders_v1116(\n"
    "                parent_id,\n"
    "                name,\n"
    "                size,\n"
    "                before,\n"
    "                task_id=task_id,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            if recovered:\n"
    "                return {\n"
    "                    \"success\": True,\n"
    "                    \"instant\": True,\n"
    "                    \"task_id\": task_id,\n"
    "                    \"file_id\": recovered.get(\"file_id\"),\n"
    "                    \"verified_size\": recovered.get(\"size\"),\n"
    "                    \"reason\": \"任务清理前已确认目标文件完整，按完整性结果记为成功\",\n"
    "                }\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": False,\n"
    "                \"task_id\": task_id,\n"
    "                \"reason\": \"缺少真实 CID；仅保留秒传模式，已清理 upload task 并回退下一来源\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "        accepted_task = \"\"\n"
    "        check_message = \"\"\n"
    "        for cid in cid_candidates:\n"
    "            checks = [\n"
    "                {\"taskId\": task_id, \"gcid\": gcid.lower(), \"cid\": cid.lower(), \"fileSize\": size},\n"
    "                {\"taskId\": task_id, \"gcid\": gcid.upper(), \"cid\": cid.upper(), \"fileSize\": size},\n"
    "                {\"taskId\": task_id, \"gcid\": gcid.lower(), \"cid\": cid.lower(), \"md5\": md5.lower(), \"fileSize\": size},\n"
    "            ]\n"
    "            for check in checks:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    body = self._guangya_userres_request(\n"
    "                        \"/userres/v1/check_can_flash_upload\",\n"
    "                        check,\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                except Exception as err:\n"
    "                    check_message = str(err)[:220]\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                data = body.get(\"data\") if isinstance(body, dict) else None\n"
    "                if isinstance(data, dict) and data.get(\"canFlashUpload\") is True:\n"
    "                    accepted_task = str(data.get(\"taskId\") or task_id)\n"
    "                    break\n"
    "\n"
    "                # 有真实 CID 的情况下，服务端可能只给“处理中 taskId”；允许轮询，\n"
    "                # 但绝不再把 taskId/fileId 本身当成功，最终仍由文件大小验真。\n"
    "                candidate_task = self._guangya_task_id(body)\n"
    "                code = body.get(\"code\") if isinstance(body, dict) else None\n"
    "                if candidate_task and code in (None, 0, \"0\", 147, \"147\"):\n"
    "                    accepted_task = candidate_task\n"
    "                    break\n"
    "                check_message = str(\n"
    "                    body.get(\"msg\")\n"
    "                    or body.get(\"message\")\n"
    "                    or body.get(\"error\")\n"
    "                    or code\n"
    "                    or \"未命中\"\n"
    "                )[:220]\n"
    "            if accepted_task:\n"
    "                break\n"
    "\n"
    "        if accepted_task:\n"
    "            verified = self._xunlei_poll_task_integrity_v1116(\n"
    "                accepted_task,\n"
    "                parent_id,\n"
    "                name,\n"
    "                size,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            if verified:\n"
    "                return {\n"
    "                    \"success\": True,\n"
    "                    \"instant\": True,\n"
    "                    \"task_id\": accepted_task,\n"
    "                    \"file_id\": verified.get(\"file_id\"),\n"
    "                    \"verified_size\": verified.get(\"size\"),\n"
    "                    \"reason\": \"光鸭秒传完成，fileId 与文件大小校验通过\",\n"
    "                }\n"
    "\n"
    "        recovered = self._xunlei_cleanup_placeholders_v1116(\n"
    "            parent_id,\n"
    "            name,\n"
    "            size,\n"
    "            before,\n"
    "            task_id=accepted_task or task_id,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if recovered:\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"instant\": True,\n"
    "                \"task_id\": accepted_task or task_id,\n"
    "                \"file_id\": recovered.get(\"file_id\"),\n"
    "                \"verified_size\": recovered.get(\"size\"),\n"
    "                \"reason\": \"轮询未确认但目标目录已出现同名同大小完整文件，按完整性结果记为成功\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": False,\n"
    "            \"task_id\": accepted_task or task_id,\n"
    "            \"reason\": f\"光鸭秒传未命中或完整性校验失败；upload task/新空占位已清理，回退下一来源{('：' + check_message) if check_message else ''}\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _dispatch_xunlei_flash(self, subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        previous = getattr(self, \"_xunlei_flash_integrity_seen_v1116\", None)\n"
    "        self._xunlei_flash_integrity_seen_v1116 = set()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return super()._dispatch_xunlei_flash(subscribe)\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            self._xunlei_flash_integrity_seen_v1116 = previous\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaXunleiIntegrityV1116Mixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== xunlei_json_pipeline_v1117.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["xunlei_json_pipeline_v1117"] = (
    "\"\"\"按用户已验证秒传脚本复刻“迅雷生成 JSON -> 光鸭导入 JSON”两阶段协议。\n"
    "\n"
    "不要再把迅雷分享读取和光鸭秒传接口拼成一条自创流程。稳定脚本的真实边界是：\n"
    "1. 迅雷侧把分享文件导出成标准 JSON（sourceTag=xunlei，顶层/行级同时保留\n"
    "   shareId/passCodeToken，并保留 gcid/md5/cid/tripleCid/wholeCid/downloadUrl）；\n"
    "2. 光鸭侧 importMd5Json 解析该 JSON，再按既定候选顺序探测 get_res_center_token /\n"
    "   check_can_flash_upload。\n"
    "\n"
    "MoviePilot 版本唯一主动裁掉的是 capacity=1/OSS 普通上传兜底：迅雷在插件里只作为\n"
    "“秒传来源”，秒传不命中必须回退光鸭分享/Magnet/ED2K，不能把大文件下载到 MP。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import json\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Tuple\n"
    "\n"
    "import requests\n"
    "\n"
    "from .xunlei_flash_v193 import _HEX32_RE, _HEX40_RE, _pick_download_url, _safe_int\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaXunleiJsonPipelineV1117Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"复刻稳定脚本的 Xunlei JSON 生产/消费合同。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260902-r30\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # Stage A: 迅雷分享 -> 标准 JSON 模板\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _xunlei_share_files(self, share_id: str, pass_code_token: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        rows = list(super()._xunlei_share_files(share_id, pass_code_token) or [])\n"
    "        for row in rows:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(row, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            row[\"shareId\"] = str(share_id or \"\")\n"
    "            row[\"passCodeToken\"] = str(pass_code_token or \"\")\n"
    "            row[\"sourceTag\"] = \"xunlei\"\n"
    "            row[\"sourceXunlei\"] = True\n"
    "            if row.get(\"id\") and not row.get(\"fileId\"):\n"
    "                row[\"fileId\"] = str(row.get(\"id\") or \"\")\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_post_json_v1117(self, endpoint: str, body: Dict[str, Any], *, action: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"分享下载链接 POST 路径；保持匿名分享头，不携带用户 Bearer。\"\"\"\n"
    "        url = f\"https://api-pan.xunlei.com{endpoint}\"\n"
    "        last_error = \"\"\n"
    "        for attempt in range(2):\n"
    "            headers = dict(self._xunlei_headers(action, refresh=False) or {})\n"
    "            if not headers.get(\"x-captcha-token\"):\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\"迅雷 captcha_token 不可用\")\n"
    "            session = self._xunlei_session()\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                response = session.post(\n"
    "                    url,\n"
    "                    json=dict(body or {}),\n"
    "                    headers=headers,\n"
    "                    timeout=int(getattr(self, \"_provider_timeout\", 15) or 15),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    payload = response.json()\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    payload = {}\n"
    "                if response.status_code < 400:\n"
    "                    return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}\n"
    "                last_error = str(\n"
    "                    (payload or {}).get(\"error_description\")\n"
    "                    or (payload or {}).get(\"error\")\n"
    "                    or (payload or {}).get(\"message\")\n"
    "                    or response.text\n"
    "                    or f\"HTTP {response.status_code}\"\n"
    "                )[:300]\n"
    "                captcha_error = False\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    captcha_error = bool(self._xunlei_captcha_error(response, payload))\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    captcha_error = response.status_code in (400, 401, 403) and \"captcha\" in last_error.lower()\n"
    "                if attempt == 0 and captcha_error:\n"
    "                    self._xunlei_runtime_captcha_token = \"\"\n"
    "                    refreshed = str(self._refresh_xunlei_captcha(action) or \"\")\n"
    "                    if refreshed:\n"
    "                        continue\n"
    "                break\n"
    "            except requests.RequestException as err:\n"
    "                last_error = str(err)[:300]\n"
    "                break\n"
    "        raise RuntimeError(f\"迅雷分享下载链接接口失败：{last_error or 'unknown error'}\")\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_file_info(self, share_id: str, pass_code_token: str, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"等价稳定脚本 getFileDetail/_fetchFileHash 的服务端精简版。\"\"\"\n"
    "        merged = dict(row or {})\n"
    "        merged[\"shareId\"] = str(share_id or \"\")\n"
    "        merged[\"passCodeToken\"] = str(pass_code_token or \"\")\n"
    "        merged[\"sourceTag\"] = \"xunlei\"\n"
    "        merged[\"sourceXunlei\"] = True\n"
    "        if merged.get(\"id\") and not merged.get(\"fileId\"):\n"
    "            merged[\"fileId\"] = str(merged.get(\"id\") or \"\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 快速通道：与脚本 _fetchFileHash 一致，优先 share/file_info。\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            first = dict(super()._xunlei_file_info(share_id, pass_code_token, merged) or merged)\n"
    "            for key, value in first.items():\n"
    "                if value not in (None, \"\", [], {}):\n"
    "                    merged[key] = value\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "\n"
    "        file_id = str(merged.get(\"id\") or merged.get(\"fileId\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        parent_id = str(merged.get(\"parent_id\") or merged.get(\"parentId\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 如果 file_info 没给真实下载地址，按脚本继续查当前 parent 的 share/detail。\n"
    "        if file_id and not str(merged.get(\"download_url\") or merged.get(\"downloadUrl\") or \"\").strip():\n"
    "            for with_audit in (\"true\", \"false\"):\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    detail = self._xunlei_get(\n"
    "                        \"/drive/v1/share/detail\",\n"
    "                        {\n"
    "                            \"share_id\": share_id,\n"
    "                            \"parent_id\": parent_id,\n"
    "                            \"pass_code_token\": pass_code_token,\n"
    "                            \"limit\": 200,\n"
    "                            \"with_audit\": with_audit,\n"
    "                            \"thumbnail_size\": \"SIZE_LARGE\",\n"
    "                            \"usage\": \"CONSUME\",\n"
