    "_XUNLEI_SHARE_RE_V1125 = re.compile(r\"https?://pan\\.xunlei\\.com/s/[^\\s\\\"'<>，。；;]+\", re.I)\n"
    "_XUNLEI_PASSCODE_RE_V1125 = re.compile(\n"
    "    r\"(?:提取码|访问码|密码|口令|pass\\s*code|passcode|pwd)\\s*[:：=]?\\s*([A-Za-z0-9]{1,16})\",\n"
    "    re.I,\n"
    ")\n"
    "_SEASON_EVIDENCE_RE_V1125 = re.compile(\n"
    "    r\"(?i)(?:\\bS(?:eason)?[ ._-]*0*(\\d{1,2})(?=[^0-9]|$)|第\\s*0*(\\d{1,2})\\s*季)\"\n"
    ")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def canonical_gying_node(value: str) -> str:\n"
    "    \"\"\"把中文域名和 punycode 统一成 ASCII 节点身份，同时丢弃路径/凭据。\"\"\"\n"
    "    raw = str(value or \"\").strip().strip(\"`\\\"'()[]{}，。；;\")\n"
    "    if not raw:\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    if not raw.lower().startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n"
    "        raw = \"https://\" + raw\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        parsed = urlparse(raw)\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    if parsed.scheme not in {\"http\", \"https\"} or not parsed.hostname:\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    if parsed.username or parsed.password:\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    host = parsed.hostname.strip(\".\")\n"
    "    lowered = host.lower()\n"
    "    if lowered in {\"localhost\", \"localhost.localdomain\"} or lowered.startswith(\"127.\") or lowered.endswith(\".local\"):\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        ascii_host = host.encode(\"idna\").decode(\"ascii\").lower()\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    port = parsed.port\n"
    "    netloc = ascii_host if port is None else f\"{ascii_host}:{port}\"\n"
    "    return urlunparse((parsed.scheme.lower(), netloc, \"\", \"\", \"\", \"\")).rstrip(\"/\")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def gying_keyword_variants(keyword: str) -> List[str]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"GYING 对附加年份/季号并不稳定；只在零结果时按从严到宽顺序降级。\"\"\"\n"
    "    original = \" \".join(str(keyword or \"\").split())\n"
    "    if not original:\n"
    "        return []\n"
    "    rows = [original]\n"
    "    current = re.sub(r\"\\s+S\\d{1,2}\\s*$\", \"\", original, flags=re.I).strip()\n"
    "    if current and current not in rows:\n"
    "        rows.append(current)\n"
    "    current = re.sub(r\"\\s+(?:19|20)\\d{2}\\s*$\", \"\", current, flags=re.I).strip()\n"
    "    if current and current not in rows:\n"
    "        rows.append(current)\n"
    "    return rows[:3]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _gying_rank_text_v1125(value: Any) -> str:\n"
    "    return re.sub(r\"[^0-9a-z\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+\", \"\", str(value or \"\").casefold())\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _gying_query_identity_v1125(keyword: str) -> Tuple[str, str, int]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"解析插件生成的 `标题 年份 Sxx`，避免把《1984》《1899》这种数字片名当年份。\"\"\"\n"
    "    raw = \" \".join(str(keyword or \"\").split())\n"
    "    season = 0\n"
    "    season_match = _SEASON_EVIDENCE_RE_V1125.search(raw)\n"
    "    if season_match:\n"
    "        for value in season_match.groups():\n"
    "            if value:\n"
    "                season = int(value)\n"
    "                break\n"
    "\n"
    "    without_season = re.sub(\n"
    "        r\"(?i)\\s+(?:S(?:eason)?[ ._-]*0*\\d{1,2}(?:[ ._-]*E0*\\d{1,4})?|第\\s*0*\\d{1,2}\\s*季)\\s*$\",\n"
    "        \"\",\n"
    "        raw,\n"
    "    ).strip()\n"
    "    year = \"\"\n"
    "    title = without_season\n"
    "    # 年份只认“非空标题 + 末尾 4 位年份”这一结构；单独的 1984/1899 永远保留为片名。\n"
    "    year_match = re.match(r\"^(?P<title>.+?)\\s+(?P<year>(?:19|20)\\d{2})$\", without_season)\n"
    "    if year_match:\n"
    "        title = str(year_match.group(\"title\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        year = str(year_match.group(\"year\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "    return _gying_rank_text_v1125(title), year, season\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _gying_card_score_v1125(keyword: str, item: Dict[str, Any]) -> int:\n"
    "    \"\"\"只改变详情展开顺序，不丢弃搜索卡片；目标卡可从第 20 条之后被提前。\"\"\"\n"
    "    expected_title, expected_year, expected_season = _gying_query_identity_v1125(keyword)\n"
    "    raw_title = str((item or {}).get(\"title\") or \"\")\n"
    "    raw_info = str((item or {}).get(\"info\") or \"\")\n"
    "    actual_title = _gying_rank_text_v1125(raw_title)\n"
    "    score = 0\n"
    "    if expected_title and actual_title:\n"
    "        if actual_title == expected_title:\n"
    "            score += 1000\n"
    "        elif expected_title in actual_title:\n"
    "            score += 850\n"
    "        elif len(actual_title) >= 3 and actual_title in expected_title:\n"
    "            score += 700\n"
    "\n"
    "    # 排序阶段只把结构化 year 和 info 中的年份当元数据；标题里的 1984/1899 可能就是片名。\n"
    "    actual_year = str((item or {}).get(\"year\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "    year_evidence = set(re.findall(r\"(?<!\\d)(19\\d{2}|20\\d{2})(?!\\d)\", raw_info))\n"
    "    if re.fullmatch(r\"(?:19|20)\\d{2}\", actual_year):\n"
    "        year_evidence.add(actual_year)\n"
    "    if expected_year:\n"
    "        if expected_year in year_evidence:\n"
    "            score += 120\n"
    "        elif year_evidence:\n"
    "            score -= 240\n"
    "\n"
    "    seasons = {\n"
    "        int(value)\n"
    "        for pair in _SEASON_EVIDENCE_RE_V1125.findall(f\"{raw_title} {raw_info}\")\n"
    "        for value in pair if value\n"
    "    }\n"
    "    if expected_season:\n"
    "        if expected_season in seasons:\n"
    "            score += 90\n"
    "        elif seasons:\n"
    "            score -= 180\n"
    "    return score\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def rank_gying_cards_v1125(keyword: str, cards: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"稳定排序：高置信目标卡优先，同分保持站点原顺序。\"\"\"\n"
    "    indexed = list(enumerate(list(cards or [])))\n"
    "    indexed.sort(key=lambda pair: (-_gying_card_score_v1125(keyword, pair[1]), pair[0]))\n"
    "    return [dict(item or {}) for _, item in indexed]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _xunlei_candidates_from_rows_v1125(rows: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]], limit: int = 80) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"从一次 downurl 已返回的数据中完整提取迅雷，不增加观影请求。\"\"\"\n"
    "    dedup: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "    for item in rows or []:\n"
    "        value = html.unescape(str((item or {}).get(\"url\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        for match in _XUNLEI_SHARE_RE_V1125.finditer(value):\n"
    "            raw_url = match.group(0).rstrip(\").]】）}\")\n"
    "            parsed = urlparse(raw_url)\n"
    "            share_match = re.search(r\"^/s/([^/?#]+)\", parsed.path or \"\", re.I)\n"
    "            if not share_match:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            share_id = share_match.group(1).strip()\n"
    "            passcode = str((item or {}).get(\"passcode\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if not passcode:\n"
    "                query = parse_qs(parsed.query or \"\")\n"
    "                for key in (\"pwd\", \"passcode\", \"pass_code\", \"code\"):\n"
    "                    values = query.get(key) or []\n"
    "                    if values and str(values[0] or \"\").strip():\n"
    "                        passcode = str(values[0]).strip()\n"
    "                        break\n"
    "            if not passcode:\n"
    "                nearby = f\"{(item or {}).get('name') or ''} {value}\"\n"
    "                code_match = _XUNLEI_PASSCODE_RE_V1125.search(nearby)\n"
    "                if code_match:\n"
    "                    passcode = code_match.group(1).strip()\n"
    "            row = {\n"
    "                \"type\": \"xunlei\",\n"
    "                \"uri\": raw_url,\n"
    "                \"identity\": share_id,\n"
    "                \"share_id\": share_id,\n"
    "                \"passcode\": passcode,\n"
    "                \"name\": str((item or {}).get(\"name\") or (item or {}).get(\"search_title\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "                \"search_title\": str((item or {}).get(\"search_title\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "                \"year\": (item or {}).get(\"year\"),\n"
    "                \"provider\": \"viewing\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            old = dedup.get(share_id)\n"
    "            if not old or (not old.get(\"passcode\") and passcode):\n"
    "                dedup[share_id] = row\n"
    "    return list(dedup.values())[: max(1, int(limit or 80))]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaGyingHardeningMixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"最终 GYING 节点身份、Cookie 边界和搜索降级策略。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260904-r51\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        config = dict(config or {})\n"
    "        super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "        preferred = canonical_gying_node(str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_base_url\", \"\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        # v1.9.2 曾把 gying.org 作为固定默认值；现在它只是换址入口时，不应每轮都被当成\n"
    "        # 首选内容节点。自动切换开启时把这个旧默认迁移为空，让节点池自行选择。\n"
    "        if bool(getattr(self, \"_viewing_auto_switch\", True)) and preferred == canonical_gying_node(LEGACY_GYING_DEFAULT):\n"
    "            preferred = \"\"\n"
    "        self._viewing_base_url = preferred\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_state(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        state = dict(super()._gying_state() or {})\n"
    "        changed = False\n"
    "        active = canonical_gying_node(str(state.get(\"active_node\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        if active != str(state.get(\"active_node\") or \"\"):\n"
    "            state[\"active_node\"] = active\n"
    "            changed = True\n"
    "\n"
    "        old_nodes = state.get(\"nodes\") or {}\n"
    "        if isinstance(old_nodes, dict):\n"
    "            migrated: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "            for raw_node, raw_row in old_nodes.items():\n"
    "                node = canonical_gying_node(str(raw_node or \"\"))\n"
    "                if not node:\n"
    "                    changed = True\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                row = dict(raw_row or {}) if isinstance(raw_row, dict) else {}\n"
    "                old = migrated.get(node)\n"
    "                if old:\n"
    "                    old_ok = float(old.get(\"last_ok_ts\") or 0)\n"
    "                    new_ok = float(row.get(\"last_ok_ts\") or 0)\n"
    "                    if new_ok >= old_ok:\n"
    "                        migrated[node] = row\n"
    "                    changed = True\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    migrated[node] = row\n"
    "                if node != str(raw_node or \"\"):\n"
    "                    changed = True\n"
    "            state[\"nodes\"] = migrated\n"
    "\n"
    "        discovered = []\n"
    "        for raw in list(state.get(\"discovered_nodes\") or []):\n"
    "            node = canonical_gying_node(str(raw or \"\"))\n"
    "            if node and node not in discovered:\n"
    "                discovered.append(node)\n"
    "            if node != str(raw or \"\"):\n"
    "                changed = True\n"
    "        state[\"discovered_nodes\"] = discovered\n"
    "        if changed:\n"
    "            self._save_gying_state(state)\n"
    "        return state\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _discover_gying_nodes(self, force: bool = False) -> List[str]:\n"
    "        base = list(super()._discover_gying_nodes(force=force) or [])\n"
    "        state = self._gying_state()\n"
    "        rows: List[str] = []\n"
    "        for raw in (\n"
    "            str(state.get(\"active_node\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_base_url\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            *str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_node_urls\", \"\") or \"\").splitlines(),\n"
    "            *CURRENT_CONTENT_SEEDS,\n"
    "            *base,\n"
    "        ):\n"
    "            node = canonical_gying_node(str(raw or \"\"))\n"
    "            if node and node not in rows:\n"
    "                rows.append(node)\n"
    "        if rows != list(state.get(\"discovered_nodes\") or []):\n"
    "            state[\"discovered_nodes\"] = rows[:30]\n"
    "            state[\"discovered_at\"] = time.time()\n"
    "            self._save_gying_state(state)\n"
    "        return rows[:30]\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_new_session(self, node: str, saved_cookie: str = \"\") -> requests.Session:\n"
    "        \"\"\"配置 Cookie 只跟随首选节点；运行时持久 Cookie 仍按 node 单独恢复。\"\"\"\n"
    "        node = canonical_gying_node(node)\n"
    "        session = requests.Session()\n"
    "        session.headers.update({\n"
    "            \"User-Agent\": \"Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/140.0.0.0 Safari/537.36\",\n"
    "            \"Accept\": \"text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/json;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8\",\n"
    "            \"Accept-Language\": \"zh-CN,zh;q=0.9,en;q=0.7\",\n"
    "            \"Cache-Control\": \"no-cache\",\n"
    "            \"Pragma\": \"no-cache\",\n"
    "            \"sec-ch-ua\": '\\\"Chromium\\\";v=\\\"140\\\", \\\"Google Chrome\\\";v=\\\"140\\\", \\\"Not_A Brand\\\";v=\\\"99\\\"',\n"
    "            \"sec-ch-ua-mobile\": \"?0\",\n"
    "            \"sec-ch-ua-platform\": '\\\"Windows\\\"',\n"
    "        })\n"
    "        proxies = _proxy_dict(bool(getattr(self, \"_provider_proxy\", False)))\n"
    "        if proxies:\n"
    "            session.proxies.update(proxies)\n"
    "        if saved_cookie:\n"
    "            _apply_cookie_header(session, saved_cookie)\n"
    "        configured_cookie = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_cookie\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        preferred = canonical_gying_node(str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_base_url\", \"\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        if configured_cookie and preferred and node == preferred:\n"
    "            _apply_cookie_header(session, configured_cookie)\n"
    "        return session\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_request(self, session: requests.Session, node: str, method: str, url: str, **kwargs: Any) -> requests.Response:\n"
    "        response = super()._gying_request(session, node, method, url, **kwargs)\n"
    "        text = str(response.text or \"\")\n"
    "        lowered = text.lower()\n"
    "        if response.status_code in {403, 404} and any(marker.lower() in lowered for marker in _BLOCK_PAGE_MARKERS):\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(f\"观影节点当前出口被阻断：HTTP {response.status_code}\")\n"
    "        return response\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_raw_results(self, keyword: str, force: bool = False):\n"
    "        variants = gying_keyword_variants(keyword)\n"
    "        if not variants:\n"
    "            return super()._gying_raw_results(keyword, force=force)\n"
    "        last_rows = []\n"
    "        last_state: Dict[str, Any] = {\"success\": False, \"message\": \"观影搜索失败\"}\n"
    "        for index, variant in enumerate(variants):\n"
    "            rows, state = super()._gying_raw_results(variant, force=force)\n"
    "            last_rows, last_state = rows, dict(state or {})\n"
    "            if not state.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "                return rows, state\n"
    "            target_scoped = bool(state.get(\"target_scoped\"))\n"
    "            target_hit = int(state.get(\"matched_cards\") or 0) > 0 if target_scoped else int(state.get(\"cards\") or 0) > 0\n"
    "            if target_hit or rows:\n"
    "                if variant != variants[0]:\n"
    "                    last_state[\"query_fallback\"] = variant\n"
    "                    last_state[\"message\"] = f\"{last_state.get('message') or '观影搜索成功'} · 已自动使用纯标题查询\"\n"
    "                return rows, last_state\n"
    "        return last_rows, last_state\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_xunlei_precise_variant_v1125(self, keyword: str) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"针对一个关键词做一次精准迅雷召回；首轮请求量不高于旧实现。\"\"\"\n"
    "        keyword = \" \".join(str(keyword or \"\").split())\n"
    "        if not keyword:\n"
    "            return [], {\"provider\": \"viewing_xunlei\", \"success\": False, \"message\": \"观影搜索关键词为空\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "        cache_key_getter = getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache_key_v11223\", None)\n"
    "        cache_key = cache_key_getter(keyword) if callable(cache_key_getter) else keyword\n"
    "        cached = dict(getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache\", {}).get(cache_key) or {})\n"
    "        cached_state = dict(cached.get(\"state\") or {})\n"
    "        if (\n"
    "            cached\n"
    "            and (\n"
    "                cached_state.get(\"recall_ranked_v1125\")\n"
    "                or (\n"
    "                    cached_state.get(\"target_scoped\")\n"
    "                    and \"matched_cards\" in cached_state\n"
    "                    and \"detail_cards\" in cached_state\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            and time.time() - float(cached.get(\"ts\") or 0) < 120\n"
    "        ):\n"
    "            rows = list(cached.get(\"rows\") or [])\n"
    "            limit = max(40, min(120, int(getattr(self, \"_provider_result_limit\", 20) or 20) * 4))\n"
    "            candidates = _xunlei_candidates_from_rows_v1125(rows, limit=limit)\n"
    "            cached_state.update({\n"
    "                \"provider\": \"viewing_xunlei\",\n"
    "                \"xunlei_resources\": len(candidates),\n"
    "                \"detail_snapshot_reused_v11223\": True,\n"
    "            })\n"
    "            return candidates, cached_state\n"
    "\n"
    "        session, login = self._viewing_session()\n"
    "        if not login.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "            return [], {\"provider\": \"viewing_xunlei\", **dict(login or {})}\n"
    "        node = str(\n"
    "            login.get(\"node\")\n"
    "            or getattr(self, \"_gying_active_node\", \"\")\n"
    "            or getattr(self, \"_viewing_base_url\", \"\")\n"
    "            or \"\"\n"
    "        ).rstrip(\"/\")\n"
    "        if not node:\n"
    "            return [], {\"provider\": \"viewing_xunlei\", \"success\": False, \"message\": \"观影没有可用内容节点\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            query = quote(keyword, safe=\"\")\n"
    "            search_variants = [\n"
    "                (\"browser\", f\"{node}/search?q={query}&type=&mode=1\"),\n"
    "                (\"legacy\", f\"{node}/search?q={query}&type=0&mode=2\"),\n"
    "            ]\n"
    "            cards: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "            response = None\n"
    "            search_mode = \"browser\"\n"
    "            for mode, search_url in search_variants:\n"
    "                current = self._gying_request(\n"
    "                    session,\n"
    "                    node,\n"
    "                    \"GET\",\n"
    "                    search_url,\n"
    "                    headers={\"Referer\": node + \"/\"},\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if self._gying_login_required(current):\n"
    "                    relogin = self._gying_login_password(session, node)\n"
    "                    if not relogin.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "                        raise RuntimeError(str(relogin.get(\"message\") or \"观影登录失效\"))\n"
    "                    current = self._gying_request(\n"
    "                        session,\n"
    "                        node,\n"
    "                        \"GET\",\n"
    "                        search_url,\n"
    "                        headers={\"Referer\": node + \"/\"},\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                if current.status_code >= 400:\n"
    "                    if mode == \"browser\":\n"
    "                        continue\n"
    "                    raise RuntimeError(f\"观影搜索 HTTP {current.status_code}\")\n"
    "                parsed_ok, parsed = _parse_search_payload_result_v11223(current.text or \"\")\n"
    "                if not parsed_ok:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                response = current\n"
    "                search_mode = mode\n"
    "                cards = parsed\n"
    "                break\n"
    "            if response is None:\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\"观影搜索没有得到有效响应\")\n"
    "\n"
    "            ranked_cards = rank_gying_cards_v1125(keyword, cards)\n"
    "            detail_limit = max(1, min(int(getattr(self, \"_provider_result_limit\", 20) or 20), 100))\n"
    "            selector = getattr(self, \"_gying_select_detail_cards_v11223\", None)\n"
    "            detail_cards = list(\n"
    "                selector(keyword, ranked_cards, detail_limit)\n"
    "                if callable(selector) else ranked_cards[:detail_limit]\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            target_getter = getattr(self, \"_gying_target_subscribe_v11223\", None)\n"
    "            target_scoped = bool(target_getter()) if callable(target_getter) else False\n"
    "            rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "            for item in detail_cards:\n"
    "                resource_type = str(item.get(\"type\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                resource_id = str(item.get(\"id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                if not resource_type or not resource_id:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                detail_referer = f\"{node}/{quote(resource_type)}/{quote(resource_id)}\"\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    payload = self._gying_detail(session, node, resource_type, resource_id, detail_referer)\n"
    "                except Exception as err:\n"
    "                    logger = getattr(self, \"_gying_obs_log\", None)\n"
    "                    if callable(logger):\n"
    "                        logger(\n"
    "                            \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                            \"迅雷召回详情跳过：标题=%s 类型=%s 错误=%s\",\n"
    "                            str(item.get(\"title\") or \"-\")[:80],\n"
    "                            resource_type[:24],\n"
    "                            str(err)[:180],\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                rows.extend(extract_resource_rows_v1106(payload, item))\n"
    "\n"
    "            deduped: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "            seen = set()\n"
    "            for row in rows:\n"
    "                key = (str(row.get(\"url\") or \"\").strip(), str(row.get(\"passcode\") or \"\").strip())\n"
    "                if not key[0] or key in seen:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                seen.add(key)\n"
    "                deduped.append(row)\n"
    "\n"
    "            xunlei_limit = max(40, min(120, int(getattr(self, \"_provider_result_limit\", 20) or 20) * 4))\n"
    "            candidates = _xunlei_candidates_from_rows_v1125(deduped, limit=xunlei_limit)\n"
    "            self._gying_persist_session(\n"
    "                node,\n"
    "                session,\n"
    "                status=\"ok\",\n"
    "                login_mode=str(login.get(\"mode\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                last_search_at=self._now_text(),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            state = {\n"
    "                \"provider\": \"viewing_xunlei\",\n"
    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"search_complete\": True,\n"
    "                \"node\": node,\n"
    "                \"login_mode\": login.get(\"mode\"),\n"
    "                \"cards\": len(cards),\n"
    "                \"raw_cards\": len(cards),\n"
    "                \"matched_cards\": len(detail_cards) if target_scoped else len(cards),\n"
    "                \"detail_cards\": len(detail_cards),\n"
    "                \"target_scoped\": target_scoped,\n"
    "                \"target_match\": bool(detail_cards) if target_scoped else None,\n"
    "                \"resources\": len(deduped),\n"
    "                \"xunlei_resources\": len(candidates),\n"
    "                \"search_mode\": search_mode,\n"
    "                \"recall_ranked_v1125\": True,\n"
    "                \"message\": (\n"
    "                    f\"观影迅雷请求完成：模糊卡片 {len(cards)} · 当前媒体卡片 \"\n"
    "                    f\"{len(detail_cards) if target_scoped else '未限定'} · 展开 {len(detail_cards)} · \"\n"
    "                    f\"未核验迅雷 {len(candidates)}\"\n"
    "                ),\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            # 与 Magnet 复用本次详情结果；后续来源不需要再重复打开同一批 downurl。\n"
    "            self._gying_search_cache[cache_key] = {\"ts\": time.time(), \"rows\": deduped, \"state\": state}\n"
    "            return candidates, state\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._gying_mark_node(node, \"search_error\", str(err))\n"
    "            return [], {\n"
    "                \"provider\": \"viewing_xunlei\",\n"
    "                \"success\": False,\n"
    "                \"search_complete\": False,\n"
    "                \"node\": node,\n"
    "                \"login_mode\": login.get(\"mode\"),\n"
    "                \"message\": str(err)[:400],\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _xunlei_candidate_priority_v1125(subscribe: Any, row: Dict[str, Any], missing: set[int]) -> Tuple[int, int]:\n"
    "        if not missing:\n"
    "            return 0, 0\n"
    "        label = \" \".join(\n"
    "            str(value or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            for value in (row.get(\"name\"), row.get(\"search_title\"))\n"
    "            if str(value or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            parsed = resolve_episode(label, season_hint=getattr(subscribe, \"season\", None))\n"
    "            episodes = reliable_episode_set(parsed, AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE)\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            episodes = set()\n"
    "        if episodes.intersection(missing):\n"
    "            return 0, min(episodes.intersection(missing))\n"
    "        if not episodes:\n"
    "            return 1, 0\n"
    "        return 2, min(episodes)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _search_viewing_xunlei(self, keyword: str):\n"
    "        \"\"\"只在当前订阅没有可用迅雷候选时放宽关键词，不因“搜到别的卡片”提前停止。\"\"\"\n"
    "        variants = gying_keyword_variants(keyword)\n"
    "        if not variants:\n"
    "            return super()._search_viewing_xunlei(keyword)\n"
    "        context = getattr(self, \"_gying_xunlei_context_v1125\", None)\n"
    "        subscribe = getattr(context, \"subscribe\", None) if context is not None else None\n"
    "        last_state: Dict[str, Any] = {\"provider\": \"viewing_xunlei\", \"success\": False, \"message\": \"观影迅雷搜索失败\"}\n"
    "        for variant in variants:\n"
    "            candidates, state = self._gying_xunlei_precise_variant_v1125(variant)\n"
    "            last_state = dict(state or {})\n"
    "            if not last_state.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "                return candidates, last_state\n"
    "            matched = list(candidates or [])\n"
    "            if subscribe is not None:\n"
    "                matched = [row for row in matched if self._provider_candidate_matches(subscribe, row)]\n"
    "            if matched:\n"
    "                missing: set[int] = set()\n"
    "                if subscribe is not None and not self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "                    try:\n"
    "                        missing = {\n"
    "                            int(value)\n"
    "                            for value in (self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or [])\n"
    "                            if int(value or 0) > 0\n"
    "                        }\n"
    "                    except Exception:\n"
    "                        missing = set()\n"
    "                    matched.sort(key=lambda row: self._xunlei_candidate_priority_v1125(subscribe, row, missing))\n"
    "                last_state[\"matched_candidates\"] = len(matched)\n"
    "                if variant != variants[0]:\n"
    "                    last_state[\"query_fallback\"] = variant\n"
    "                    last_state[\"message\"] = (\n"
    "                        f\"{last_state.get('message') or '观影迅雷搜索成功'} · 严格关键词无当前订阅可用迅雷，已降级到 {variant}\"\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                return matched, last_state\n"
    "        last_state[\"searched_variants\"] = variants\n"
    "        last_state[\"message\"] = (\n"
    "            f\"观影可访问，但 {len(variants)} 级关键词均没有当前订阅可用迅雷分享\"\n"
    "            if last_state.get(\"success\") else last_state.get(\"message\")\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return [], last_state\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _dispatch_xunlei_flash(self, subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"给迅雷搜索提供线程隔离的订阅上下文，业务转存仍完全交回原链。\"\"\"\n"
    "        context = getattr(self, \"_gying_xunlei_context_v1125\", None)\n"
    "        if context is None:\n"
    "            context = threading.local()\n"
    "            self._gying_xunlei_context_v1125 = context\n"
    "        previous = getattr(context, \"subscribe\", None)\n"
    "        context.subscribe = subscribe\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return super()._dispatch_xunlei_flash(subscribe)\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            if previous is None:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    delattr(context, \"subscribe\")\n"
    "                except AttributeError:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                context.subscribe = previous\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _provider_candidate_matches(self, subscribe: Any, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n"
    "        base_matcher = getattr(super(), \"_provider_candidate_matches\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(base_matcher) and bool(base_matcher(subscribe, row)):\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "\n"
    "        row = dict(row or {})\n"
    "        structured = getattr(self, \"_structured_candidate_match_v11217\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(structured):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                matched, detail = structured(subscribe, row)\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【资源召回v1.12.17】结构化候选匹配异常，回退到基础匹配：%s\",\n"
    "                    str(err)[:220],\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                if matched:\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                        \"INFO\",\n"
    "                        \"【光鸭转存助手】【资源召回v1.12.17】#%s %s 搜索候选由结构化匹配救回：alias=%s score=%s reason=%s\",\n"
    "                        int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0),\n"
    "                        str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                        str(detail.get(\"matched_alias\") or \"-\")[:100],\n"
    "                        int(detail.get(\"score\") or 0),\n"
    "                        str(detail.get(\"reason\") or \"\")[:220],\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                    return True\n"
    "\n"
    "        raw_actual = \" \".join(\n"
    "            str(value or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            for value in (\n"
    "                row.get(\"search_title\"),\n"
    "                row.get(\"name\"),\n"
    "                row.get(\"label\"),\n"
    "                row.get(\"display_title\"),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            if str(value or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        actual = _normalize_media_text(raw_actual)\n"
    "        if not actual:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "\n"
    "        alias_values: List[str] = []\n"
    "        for field in (\n"
    "            \"name\", \"title\", \"original_name\", \"original_title\", \"en_name\", \"cn_name\",\n"
    "            \"media_name\", \"aka\", \"aliases\", \"alias\",\n"
    "        ):\n"
    "            raw = getattr(subscribe, field, None)\n"
    "            if raw in (None, \"\"):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if isinstance(raw, dict):\n"
    "                candidates = list(raw.values())\n"
    "            elif isinstance(raw, (list, tuple, set)):\n"
    "                candidates = list(raw)\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                candidates = [raw]\n"
    "            for candidate in candidates:\n"
    "                text = str(candidate or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                if text:\n"
    "                    alias_values.append(text)\n"
    "\n"
    "        expected_keys: List[str] = []\n"
    "        seen = set()\n"
    "        for alias in alias_values:\n"
    "            key = _normalize_media_text(alias)\n"
    "            if len(key) < 2 or key in seen:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            seen.add(key)\n"
    "            expected_keys.append(key)\n"
    "        if not expected_keys:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        if not any(key in actual or actual in key for key in expected_keys):\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "\n"
    "        expected_year = str(getattr(subscribe, \"year\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        actual_years = set(re.findall(r\"(?<!\\d)(19\\d{2}|20\\d{2})(?!\\d)\", raw_actual))\n"
    "        row_year = str(row.get(\"year\") or row.get(\"year_hint\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if row_year and row_year.isdigit():\n"
    "            actual_years.add(row_year)\n"
    "        if expected_year and actual_years and expected_year not in actual_years:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "\n"
    "        seasons = {\n"
    "            int(value)\n"
    "            for pair in re.findall(r\"(?i)(?:\\bS(?:eason)?\\s*0*(\\d{1,2})\\b|第\\s*0*(\\d{1,2})\\s*季)\", raw_actual)\n"
    "            for value in pair if value\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        is_movie = \"movie\" in str(getattr(subscribe, \"type\", \"\") or \"\").lower() or \"电影\" in str(getattr(subscribe, \"type\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        if is_movie and seasons:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            expected_season = int(getattr(subscribe, \"season\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            expected_season = 0\n"
    "        if not is_movie and expected_season > 0 and seasons and expected_season not in seasons:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        return True\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\n"
    "    \"GuangYaGyingHardeningMixin\",\n"
    "    \"canonical_gying_node\",\n"
    "    \"gying_keyword_variants\",\n"
    "    \"rank_gying_cards_v1125\",\n"
    "]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== gying_observability_v1104.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["gying_observability_v1104"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.10.4 观影 GYING 可观测性层。\n"
    "\n"
    "观影链路此前主要把最终结果写入 ``viewing_session_state``，节点发现、PoW、登录、搜索、\n"
    "downurl 和故障切换缺少统一插件日志。这里仅补可观测性，不改变节点选择、认证、搜索、\n"
    "迅雷秒传或 Magnet/ED2K 业务决策。\n"
    "\n"
    "v1.12.5 追加“当前订阅召回”诊断：在不记录分享 ID、提取码、Cookie/Token 的前提下，\n"
    "把关键词、影视卡片数、实际展开数、迅雷候选数、媒体匹配数、当前真实缺集、直接覆盖数、\n"
    "关键词降级与最终秒传结果串成一条可定位日志，用来区分“搜索没召回 / 匹配被拦 / 缺集解析\n"
    "未覆盖 / 迅雷秒传失败”。\n"
    "\n"
    "日志和公开状态只记录节点、阶段、结果计数与错误摘要；Cookie、密码、Token、PoW 参数、\n"
    "迅雷分享 ID 与提取码不会写入日志或公开状态。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple\n"
    "from urllib.parse import urlparse\n"
    "\n"
    "from .gying_protocol_v1106 import extract_downlist_v1106, extract_panlist_v1106\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaGyingObservabilityV1104Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"给最终 GYING 调用链补齐可判断的过程日志与最近运行状态。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260904-r51\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _gying_node_label(value: Any) -> str:\n"
    "        raw = str(value or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not raw:\n"
    "            return \"-\"\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            parsed = urlparse(raw if \"://\" in raw else \"https://\" + raw)\n"
    "            if parsed.hostname:\n"
    "                port = f\":{parsed.port}\" if parsed.port else \"\"\n"
    "                return f\"{parsed.scheme or 'https'}://{parsed.hostname}{port}\"\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        return \"-\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_obs_log(self, level: str, message: str, *args: Any) -> None:\n"
    "        writer = getattr(self, \"_plugin_log\", None)\n"
    "        if not callable(writer):\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            writer(level, \"【光鸭转存助手】【观影】\" + message, *args)\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_obs_record(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        stage: str,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        success: bool | None = None,\n"
    "        node: str = \"\",\n"
    "        message: str = \"\",\n"
    "        **extra: Any,\n"
    "    ) -> None:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            old = self.get_data(\"viewing_observability_state\") or {}\n"
    "            state = dict(old) if isinstance(old, dict) else {}\n"
    "            state.update({\n"
    "                \"stage\": str(stage or \"\")[:40],\n"
    "                \"success\": success,\n"
    "                \"node\": self._gying_node_label(node),\n"
    "                \"message\": str(message or \"\")[:300],\n"
    "                \"updated_at\": self._now_text(),\n"
    "                \"updated_ts\": time.time(),\n"
    "            })\n"
    "            for key in (\n"
    "                \"mode\", \"cards\", \"detail_cards\", \"resources\", \"pan\", \"magnet\", \"ed2k\", \"xunlei\", \"nodes\",\n"
    "                \"matched\", \"direct_cover\", \"missing\", \"query_fallback\", \"shares\", \"successful_files\", \"episodes\",\n"
    "                \"transport\", \"cache_hit\", \"cache_age_seconds\", \"search_http_requests\",\n"
    "                \"downurl_attempts\", \"downurl_success\", \"downurl_failures\",\n"
    "            ):\n"
    "                if key in extra:\n"
    "                    value = extra.get(key)\n"
    "                    if isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)) or value is None:\n"
    "                        state[key] = value\n"
    "            self.save_data(\"viewing_observability_state\", state)\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "        enabled = bool(getattr(self, \"_viewing_enabled\", False))\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"运行时初始化：启用=%s 自动切节点=%s 自动PoW=%s 首选节点=%s\",\n"
    "            enabled,\n"
    "            bool(getattr(self, \"_viewing_auto_switch\", True)),\n"
    "            bool(getattr(self, \"_viewing_auto_challenge\", True)),\n"
    "            self._gying_node_label(getattr(self, \"_viewing_base_url\", \"\")),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_record(\n"
    "            \"init\",\n"
    "            success=True if enabled else None,\n"
    "            node=getattr(self, \"_viewing_base_url\", \"\"),\n"
    "            message=\"观影已启用\" if enabled else \"观影未启用\",\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _discover_gying_nodes(self, force: bool = False) -> List[str]:\n"
    "        started = time.monotonic()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            rows = list(super()._discover_gying_nodes(force=force) or [])\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_log(\"WARNING\", \"节点发现失败：%s\", str(err)[:240])\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_record(\"discover\", success=False, message=str(err))\n"
    "            raise\n"
    "        if force:\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_log(\"INFO\", \"节点刷新完成：候选=%s 耗时=%.2fs\", len(rows), time.monotonic() - started)\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_record(\n"
    "            \"discover\",\n"
    "            success=bool(rows),\n"
    "            node=rows[0] if rows else \"\",\n"
    "            message=f\"发现 {len(rows)} 个候选节点\",\n"
    "            nodes=len(rows),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_solve_challenge(self, session, node: str, response):\n"
    "        label = self._gying_node_label(node)\n"
    "        started = time.monotonic()\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_log(\"INFO\", \"检测到浏览器 PoW：节点=%s，开始计算验证\", label)\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_record(\"pow\", success=None, node=node, message=\"开始计算浏览器 PoW\")\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            result = dict(super()._gying_solve_challenge(session, node, response) or {})\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_log(\"WARNING\", \"PoW失败：节点=%s 错误=%s\", label, str(err)[:240])\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_record(\"pow\", success=False, node=node, message=str(err))\n"
    "            raise\n"
    "        mode = str(result.get(\"mode\") or \"unknown\")\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_log(\"INFO\", \"PoW通过：节点=%s 模式=%s 耗时=%.2fs\", label, mode, time.monotonic() - started)\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_record(\"pow\", success=True, node=node, message=\"浏览器 PoW 已通过\", mode=mode)\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_login(self, session, node: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        label = self._gying_node_label(node)\n"
    "        configured = bool(\n"
    "            str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_username\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            and str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_password\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"登录检查：节点=%s 账号登录=%s 现有Cookie=%s\",\n"
    "            label,\n"
    "            configured,\n"
    "            bool(len(session.cookies)),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            result = dict(super()._gying_login(session, node) or {})\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_log(\"WARNING\", \"登录调用异常：节点=%s 错误=%s\", label, str(err)[:240])\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_record(\"login\", success=False, node=node, message=str(err))\n"
    "            raise\n"
    "        ok = bool(result.get(\"success\"))\n"
    "        mode = str(result.get(\"mode\") or \"\")\n"
    "        message = str(result.get(\"message\") or \"\")[:240]\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\" if ok else \"WARNING\",\n"
    "            \"登录结果：节点=%s 成功=%s 模式=%s 信息=%s\",\n"
    "            label,\n"
    "            ok,\n"
    "            mode or \"-\",\n"
    "            message or \"-\",\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_record(\"login\", success=ok, node=node, message=message, mode=mode)\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_mark_node(self, node: str, status: str, message: str = \"\") -> None:\n"
    "        super()._gying_mark_node(node, status, message)\n"
    "        status = str(status or \"\")\n"
    "        if status and status != \"ok\":\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"节点状态：节点=%s 状态=%s 信息=%s\",\n"
    "                self._gying_node_label(node),\n"
    "                status,\n"
    "                str(message or \"\")[:200] or \"-\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_record(\n"
    "                \"node\",\n"
    "                success=False,\n"
    "                node=node,\n"
    "                message=f\"{status}: {str(message or '')[:220]}\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _viewing_session(self) -> Tuple[Any, Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        if not bool(getattr(self, \"_viewing_enabled\", False)):\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_log(\"INFO\", \"会话检查跳过：观影未启用\")\n"
    "        started = time.monotonic()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            session, status = super()._viewing_session()\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_log(\"WARNING\", \"会话建立异常：%s\", str(err)[:240])\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_record(\"session\", success=False, message=str(err))\n"
    "            raise\n"
    "        status = dict(status or {})\n"
    "        ok = bool(status.get(\"success\"))\n"
    "        node = str(status.get(\"node\") or \"\")\n"
    "        mode = str(status.get(\"mode\") or \"\")\n"
    "        message = str(status.get(\"message\") or \"\")[:240]\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\" if ok else \"WARNING\",\n"
    "            \"会话结果：成功=%s 节点=%s 模式=%s 耗时=%.2fs 信息=%s\",\n"
    "            ok,\n"
    "            self._gying_node_label(node),\n"
    "            mode or \"-\",\n"
    "            time.monotonic() - started,\n"
    "            message or \"-\",\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_record(\n"
    "            \"session\",\n"
    "            success=ok,\n"
    "            node=node,\n"
    "            message=message or (\"会话可用\" if ok else \"会话不可用\"),\n"
    "            mode=mode,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return session, status\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_detail(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        session,\n"
    "        node: str,\n"
    "        resource_type: str,\n"
    "        resource_id: str,\n"
    "        referer: str,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            payload = dict(\n"
    "                super()._gying_detail(session, node, resource_type, resource_id, referer) or {}\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"详情接口请求失败：节点=%s 类型=%s 错误=%s\",\n"
    "                self._gying_node_label(node),\n"
    "                str(resource_type or \"-\")[:24],\n"
    "                str(err)[:220],\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            raise\n"
    "        panlist = extract_panlist_v1106(payload)\n"
    "        downlist = extract_downlist_v1106(payload)\n"
    "        pan_urls = list(panlist.get(\"url\") or [])\n"
    "        kinds = list(downlist.get(\"k\") or [])\n"
    "        hashes = list(downlist.get(\"m\") or [])\n"
    "        magnet = 0\n"
    "        for index, value in enumerate(hashes):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                kind = int(kinds[index]) if index < len(kinds) else -1\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                kind = -1\n"
    "            if kind == 0 and str(value or \"\").strip():\n"
    "                magnet += 1\n"
    "        xunlei = sum(1 for value in pan_urls if \"pan.xunlei.com/s/\" in str(value or \"\").lower())\n"
    "        pan = len(pan_urls)\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"详情接口响应（仅表示请求成功，尚未证明属于当前订阅）：节点=%s 类型=%s 未核验网盘=%s 未核验迅雷=%s 未核验Magnet=%s\",\n"
    "            self._gying_node_label(node),\n"
    "            str(resource_type or \"-\")[:24],\n"
    "            pan,\n"
    "            xunlei,\n"
    "            magnet,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_record(\n"
    "            \"downurl\",\n"
    "            success=True,\n"
    "            node=node,\n"
    "            message=f\"接口成功；未核验网盘 {pan} · 未核验迅雷 {xunlei} · 未核验 Magnet {magnet}\",\n"
    "            pan=pan,\n"
    "            xunlei=xunlei,\n"
    "            magnet=magnet,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return payload\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_raw_results(self, keyword: str, force: bool = False):\n"
    "        clean_keyword = \" \".join(str(keyword or \"\").split())[:120]\n"
    "        started = time.monotonic()\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_log(\"INFO\", \"搜索开始：关键词=%s 强制刷新=%s\", clean_keyword or \"-\", bool(force))\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            rows, state = super()._gying_raw_results(keyword, force=force)\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_log(\"WARNING\", \"搜索异常：关键词=%s 错误=%s\", clean_keyword or \"-\", str(err)[:240])\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_record(\"search\", success=False, message=str(err))\n"
    "            raise\n"
    "        state = dict(state or {})\n"
    "        ok = bool(state.get(\"success\"))\n"
    "        node = str(state.get(\"node\") or \"\")\n"
    "        cards = int(state.get(\"raw_cards\") or state.get(\"cards\") or 0)\n"
    "        detail_cards = int(state.get(\"detail_cards\") or 0)\n"
    "        target_scoped = bool(state.get(\"target_scoped\"))\n"
    "        matched_cards = int(state.get(\"matched_cards\") or 0)\n"
    "        raw_links = len(rows or [])\n"
    "        card_match_text = str(matched_cards) if target_scoped else \"未限定\"\n"
    "        message = str(state.get(\"message\") or \"\")[:240]\n"
    "        transport = str(state.get(\"transport\") or \"unknown\")\n"
    "        cache_hit = bool(state.get(\"cache_hit\"))\n"
    "        search_http_requests = int(state.get(\"search_http_requests\") or 0)\n"
    "        downurl_attempts = int(state.get(\"downurl_attempts\") or 0)\n"
    "        downurl_success = int(state.get(\"downurl_success\") or 0)\n"
    "        downurl_failures = int(state.get(\"downurl_failures\") or 0)\n"
    "        cache_age_seconds = float(state.get(\"cache_age_seconds\") or 0.0)\n"
    "        transport_label = (\n"
    "            \"真实网络\"\n"
    "            if transport == \"network\" and search_http_requests > 0\n"
    "            else \"缓存复用\"\n"
    "            if transport == \"cache\"\n"
    "            else transport\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\" if ok else \"WARNING\",\n"
    "            \"搜索请求完成：传输=%s cache_hit=%s /search真实请求=%s downurl尝试=%s 成功=%s 失败=%s \"\n"
    "            \"接口健康=%s 节点=%s 模糊卡片=%s 当前媒体卡片=%s 已展开=%s 目标卡原始链接（待订阅行核验）=%s \"\n"
    "            \"缓存年龄=%.1fs 耗时=%.2fs 信息=%s\",\n"
    "            transport_label,\n"
    "            cache_hit,\n"
    "            search_http_requests,\n"
    "            downurl_attempts,\n"
    "            downurl_success,\n"
    "            downurl_failures,\n"
    "            ok,\n"
    "            self._gying_node_label(node),\n"
    "            cards,\n"
    "            card_match_text,\n"
    "            detail_cards,\n"
    "            raw_links,\n"
    "            cache_age_seconds,\n"
    "            time.monotonic() - started,\n"
    "            message or \"-\",\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_record(\n"
    "            \"search\",\n"
    "            success=ok,\n"
    "            node=node,\n"
    "            message=message,\n"
    "            cards=cards,\n"
    "            matched_cards=matched_cards,\n"
    "            detail_cards=detail_cards,\n"
    "            resources=raw_links,\n"
    "            candidate_stage=\"detail_unverified\",\n"
    "            target_scoped=target_scoped,\n"
    "            card_target_match=bool(state.get(\"target_match\")) if target_scoped else None,\n"
    "            mode=str(state.get(\"login_mode\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            transport=transport,\n"
    "            cache_hit=cache_hit,\n"
    "            cache_age_seconds=cache_age_seconds,\n"
    "            search_http_requests=search_http_requests,\n"
    "            downurl_attempts=downurl_attempts,\n"
    "            downurl_success=downurl_success,\n"
    "            downurl_failures=downurl_failures,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return rows, state\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _search_viewing(self, keyword: str):\n"
    "        rows, state = super()._search_viewing(keyword)\n"
    "        magnet = sum(\n"
    "            1 for row in rows or [] if str((row or {}).get(\"type\") or \"\") == \"magnet\"\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        ed2k = sum(\n"
    "            1 for row in rows or [] if str((row or {}).get(\"type\") or \"\") == \"ed2k\"\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"协议候选预筛（不是入库结果）：Magnet=%s ED2K=%s；仍需真实文件身份与入队回执核验\",\n"
    "            magnet,\n"
    "            ed2k,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_record(\n"
    "            \"candidates\",\n"
    "            success=bool((state or {}).get(\"success\")),\n"
    "            node=str((state or {}).get(\"node\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            message=f\"预筛 Magnet {magnet} · ED2K {ed2k}；不是入库结果\",\n"
    "            magnet=magnet,\n"
    "            ed2k=ed2k,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return rows, state\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _search_viewing_xunlei(self, keyword: str):\n"
    "        started = time.monotonic()\n"
    "        rows, state = super()._search_viewing_xunlei(keyword)\n"
    "        state = dict(state or {})\n"
    "        count = len(rows or [])\n"
    "\n"
    "        context = getattr(self, \"_gying_xunlei_context_v1125\", None)\n"
    "        subscribe = getattr(context, \"subscribe\", None) if context is not None else None\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0) if subscribe is not None else 0\n"
    "        name = str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\").strip() if subscribe is not None else \"\"\n"
    "        missing_values: List[int] = []\n"
    "        if subscribe is not None:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                if not self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "                    missing_values = sorted({\n"
    "                        int(value)\n"
    "                        for value in (self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or [])\n"
    "                        if int(value or 0) > 0\n"
    "                    })\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                missing_values = []\n"
    "\n"
    "        direct_cover = 0\n"
    "        priority = getattr(self, \"_xunlei_candidate_priority_v1125\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(priority) and subscribe is not None and missing_values:\n"
    "            missing_set = set(missing_values)\n"
    "            for row in rows or []:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    if int(priority(subscribe, row, missing_set)[0]) == 0:\n"
    "                        direct_cover += 1\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "\n"
    "        fallback = str(state.get(\"query_fallback\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        missing_text = \",\".join(f\"E{value:02d}\" for value in missing_values[:20]) or (\"movie\" if subscribe is not None else \"-\")\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\" if state.get(\"success\") else \"WARNING\",\n"
    "            \"迅雷召回：订阅=%s%s 关键词=%s 影视卡片=%s 展开=%s 迅雷原始=%s 媒体匹配=%s 当前缺集=%s 直接覆盖=%s 降级=%s 耗时=%.2fs\",\n"
    "            f\"#{sid} \" if sid else \"\",\n"
    "            name or \"-\",\n"
    "            \" \".join(str(keyword or \"\").split())[:120] or \"-\",\n"
    "            int(state.get(\"cards\") or 0),\n"
    "            int(state.get(\"detail_cards\") or 0),\n"
    "            int(state.get(\"xunlei_resources\") or count),\n"
    "            count,\n"
    "            missing_text,\n"
    "            direct_cover,\n"
    "            fallback or \"否\",\n"
    "            time.monotonic() - started,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if rows:\n"
    "            preview = []\n"
    "            for row in list(rows)[:3]:\n"
    "                label = str((row or {}).get(\"name\") or (row or {}).get(\"search_title\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                if label:\n"
    "                    preview.append(label[:80])\n"
    "            if preview:\n"
    "                self._gying_obs_log(\"INFO\", \"迅雷候选预览：%s\", \" | \".join(preview))\n"
    "        elif state.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "            self._gying_obs_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"

    "    def _dispatch_xunlei_flash(self, subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        started = time.monotonic()\n"
    "        result = dict(super()._dispatch_xunlei_flash(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        name = str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        episodes = sorted({\n"

    "            if str(value).isdigit() and int(value) > 0\n"
    "        })\n"
    "        episode_text = \",\".join(f\"E{value:02d}\" for value in episodes) or (\"movie\" if result.get(\"movie\") else \"无\")\n"
    "        errors = list(result.get(\"errors\") or [])\n"
    "        self._gying_obs_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\" if result.get(\"success\") else \"WARNING\",\n"
    "            \"迅雷执行：订阅=#%s %s 尝试分享=%s 成功文件=%s 覆盖=%s handled=%s 错误数=%s 耗时=%.2fs 结果=%s\",\n"

    "            int(result.get(\"shares\") or 0),\n"
    "            int(result.get(\"successful_files\") or 0),\n"
    "            episode_text,\n"
    "            bool(result.get(\"handled\")),\n"
    "            len(errors),\n"

    "        )\n"

    "            \"xunlei_dispatch\",\n"
    "            success=bool(result.get(\"success\")),\n"
    "            message=f\"#{sid} {name} · 分享 {int(result.get('shares') or 0)} · 成功文件 {int(result.get('successful_files') or 0)} · 覆盖 {episode_text}\",\n"
    "            shares=int(result.get(\"shares\") or 0),\n"
    "            successful_files=int(result.get(\"successful_files\") or 0),\n"
    "            episodes=episode_text,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return result\n"

    "                if GuangYaGyingObservabilityV1104Mixin._inject_viewing_test_button(item, action):\n"
    "                    return True\n"
    "        elif isinstance(node, dict):\n"
    "            for value in node.values():\n"
    "                if GuangYaGyingObservabilityV1104Mixin._inject_viewing_test_button(value, action):\n"
    "                    return True\n"
    "        return False\n"

    "            \"test\",\n"

    "            node=node,\n"
    "            message=message or (\"测试通过\" if ok else \"测试失败\"),\n"
    "            mode=str(result.get(\"mode\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            cards=int(result.get(\"cards\") or 0),\n"
    "            resources=int(result.get(\"resources\") or 0),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return result\n"

    "    def _viewing_gap_v1113(self, subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"covered = 目标已被 completed/pending/reservation/claim 真实覆盖；与 handled 无关。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            reservations = dict(self._pending_reservations(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "            active_movie = any(\n"
    "                isinstance(row, dict)\n"
    "                and int(row.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0) == sid\n"
    "                and str(row.get(\"state\") or \"\") in {\"new\", \"retry\", \"dispatching\", \"submitted\", \"queued\", \"waiting\", \"completed\"}\n"
    "                for row in (self._source_store().get(\"items\") or {}).values()\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            library_exists = False\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                checker = getattr(self, \"_movie_needs_pull_v1125\", None)\n"
    "                if callable(checker):\n"
    "                    library_exists = not bool(checker(subscribe))\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                library_exists = False\n"
    "            covered = bool(reservations.get(\"movie\")) or active_movie or library_exists\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"covered\": covered,\n"
    "                \"missing\": [] if covered else [\"movie\"],\n"
    "                \"reserved\": [\"movie\"] if reservations.get(\"movie\") else [],\n"
    "                \"claimed\": [\"movie\"] if active_movie else [],\n"
    "                \"uncovered\": [] if covered else [\"movie\"],\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 退出 due-scope：covered 必须相对真实缺集，不能被当日 due 裁剪伪造成“已齐”。\n"
    "        without_scope = getattr(self, \"_without_due_scope_v1120\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(without_scope):\n"
    "            with without_scope():\n"
    "                missing = set(int(v) for v in (self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or []) if int(v or 0) > 0)\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            missing = set(int(v) for v in (self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or []) if int(v or 0) > 0)\n"
    "        reservations = dict(self._pending_reservations(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "        reserved = set(int(v) for v in (reservations.get(\"episodes\") or set()) if int(v or 0) > 0)\n"
    "        claimed = set(int(v) for v in (self._active_source_claims(int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)) or set()) if int(v or 0) > 0)\n"
    "        uncovered = missing - reserved - claimed\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"covered\": not uncovered,\n"
    "            \"missing\": sorted(missing),\n"
    "            \"reserved\": sorted(reserved),\n"
    "            \"claimed\": sorted(claimed),\n"
    "            \"uncovered\": sorted(uncovered),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _try_transfer_subscription_inner(\n"
    "        self,\n"

    "        force: bool = False,\n"

    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"

    "\n"
    "        # completed = 订阅级完成合同；pending 仅表示前序来源有在途任务，\n"

    "\n"
    "        gap = self._viewing_gap_v1113(subscribe)\n"

    "        if bool(gap.get(\"covered\")):\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【观影执行】#%s 当前目标已真实覆盖(covered=True)，停止 Magnet/ED2K；handled=%s pending=%s missing=%s reserved=%s claimed=%s\",\n"
    "                sid,\n"
    "                bool(result.get(\"handled\")),\n"
    "                bool(result.get(\"pending\")),\n"

    "                \",\".join(str(v) for v in (gap.get(\"reserved\") or [])) or \"-\",\n"
    "                \",\".join(str(v) for v in (gap.get(\"claimed\") or [])) or \"-\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        if bool(result.get(\"pending\")):\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【观影执行】#%s 前序 pending=True 但 uncovered=%s；继续内部 Magnet/ED2K 补齐剩余缺集（仍禁止 native）\",\n"
    "                sid,\n"
    "                \",\".join(str(v) for v in (gap.get(\"uncovered\") or [])) or \"movie\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "        if bool(result.get(\"handled\")):\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【观影执行】#%s handled=True 仅表示托管所有权，covered=False remaining=%s；继续内部 Magnet/ED2K（仍禁止 native）；信息=%s\",\n"
    "                sid,\n"
    "                \",\".join(str(v) for v in (gap.get(\"uncovered\") or [])) or \"movie\",\n"
    "                str(result.get(\"message\") or \"-\")[:260],\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【观影执行】#%s 前序链未完整覆盖，继续观影 Magnet/ED2K；uncovered=%s 前序success=%s handled=%s pending=%s 信息=%s\",\n"
    "            sid,\n"

    "            bool(result.get(\"success\")),\n"

    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"handled\": True,\n"
    "                \"viewing_external\": viewing,\n"
    "                \"message\": f\"{str(result.get('message') or '前序来源已检查')}；{viewing.get('message')}\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        return {**result, \"viewing_external\": viewing}\n"

# ===== core_pipeline_v11214.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["core_pipeline_v11214"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.12.14 核心资源链统一收口。\n"
    "\n"
    "目标不是再为单个资源打补丁，而是建立所有来源共同的不变量：\n"
    "1. 发现：频道与观影都可以贡献 GuangYa / Xunlei / Magnet / ED2K；\n"
    "2. 识别：搜索标题只是发现证据，真正写盘前必须使用分享文件/resolve 文件做最终身份判断；\n"
    "3. 缺口：TV 最终允许集只允许由 MoviePilot 媒体库事实、成功事实、reservation、其它来源 claim\n"
    "   继续缩小，任何中间缓存都不能把已有集重新放回；\n"
    "4. 物理文件：一个不可分割视频的全部集号必须属于最终允许集。E09-E11 只缺 E11 时整文件拒绝；\n"
    "5. 幂等：同一规则同时覆盖 GuangYa 直接分享、Xunlei、Magnet、ED2K，来源切换不能绕过。\n"
    "\n"
    "本层嵌套在 ManualCheck 与 v1.12.13 Xunlei fence 之间，不移动最终插件顶层 MRO。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"

    "import hashlib\n"
    "import re\n"

    "import time\n"

    "\n"
    "from .episode_resolver_v190 import AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE, reliable_episode_set, resolve_episode\n"

    ")\n"

    "    title_key_v1111,\n"
    ")\n"
    "from .media_source_v209 import is_tmdb_source, normalize_media_source_token\n"
    "from .xunlei_existing_fence_v11213 import GuangYaXunleiExistingEpisodeFenceV11213Mixin\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_ACTIVE_EXTERNAL_STATES_V11214 = {\"new\", \"retry\", \"dispatching\", \"submitted\", \"queued\", \"waiting\", \"completed\"}\n"
    "_GENERIC_SHARE_ROOTS_V11214 = {\n"
    "    \"season\", \"season1\", \"season2\", \"s01\", \"s02\", \"tv\", \"movie\", \"movies\", \"video\", \"videos\", \"media\",\n"
    "    \"电视剧\", \"电影\", \"视频\", \"资源\", \"全集\", \"全季\",\n"
    "}\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _positive_episode_set_v11214(values: Iterable[Any]) -> Set[int]:\n"
    "    result: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "    for raw in values or []:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            value = int(raw or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        if value > 0:\n"
    "            result.add(value)\n"
    "    return result\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _physical_episode_subset_v11214(episodes: Iterable[Any], allowed: Iterable[Any]) -> bool:\n"
    "    \"\"\"物理文件只能在“全部集号都属于允许集”时写盘；交集非空远远不够。\"\"\"\n"
    "    actual = _positive_episode_set_v11214(episodes)\n"
    "    target = _positive_episode_set_v11214(allowed)\n"
    "    return bool(actual and target and actual.issubset(target))\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaCorePipelineV11214Mixin(GuangYaXunleiExistingEpisodeFenceV11213Mixin):\n"
    "    \"\"\"统一来源发现、TV 官方别名召回和最终物理文件缺集栅栏。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    plugin_version = \"1.12.14\"\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260905-r60\"\n"
    "    _tv_alias_cache_ttl_v11214 = 24 * 60 * 60\n"
    "    _tv_alias_failure_ttl_v11214 = 10 * 60\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        self._core_pipeline_local_v11214 = threading.local()\n"
    "        self._tv_alias_lock_v11214 = threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._tv_alias_cache_v11214: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        return super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # Thread-local provider entries: GYING GuangYa share enters legacy direct-share chain\n"
    "    # without polluting persistent Telegram channel_index.\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _core_local_v11214(self) -> threading.local:\n"
    "        local = getattr(self, \"_core_pipeline_local_v11214\", None)\n"
    "        if local is None:\n"
    "            local = threading.local()\n"
    "            self._core_pipeline_local_v11214 = local\n"
    "        return local\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_data(self, key: str):\n"
    "        base = super().get_data(key)\n"
    "        if str(key or \"\") != \"channel_index\":\n"
    "            return base\n"
    "        local = getattr(self, \"_core_pipeline_local_v11214\", None)\n"
    "        extras = list(getattr(local, \"provider_share_entries\", []) or []) if local is not None else []\n"
    "        if not extras:\n"
    "            return base\n"
    "        payload = copy.deepcopy(base) if isinstance(base, dict) else {}\n"
    "        items = list(payload.get(\"items\") or [])\n"
    "        seen = {\n"
    "            (_share_identity(str(row.get(\"share_url\") or \"\")), str(row.get(\"resource_group_id\") or \"\"))\n"
    "            for row in items if isinstance(row, dict)\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        for raw in extras:\n"
    "            row = dict(raw or {})\n"
    "            marker = (_share_identity(str(row.get(\"share_url\") or \"\")), str(row.get(\"resource_group_id\") or \"\"))\n"
    "            if marker[0] and marker not in seen:\n"
    "                seen.add(marker)\n"
    "                items.append(row)\n"
    "        payload[\"items\"] = items\n"
    "        return payload\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _route_mode_v11214(self) -> str:\n"
    "        reader = getattr(self, \"_route_source_mode_value_v1115\", None)\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return str(reader() if callable(reader) else getattr(self, \"_route_source_mode_v1115\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _try_transfer_subscription_inner(self, subscribe: Any, force: bool = False, refresh_channel: bool = True):\n"
    "        local = self._core_local_v11214()\n"
    "        previous_entries = getattr(local, \"provider_share_entries\", None)\n"
    "        previous_subscribe = getattr(local, \"subscribe\", None)\n"
    "        local.provider_share_entries = []\n"
    "        local.subscribe = subscribe\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return super()._try_transfer_subscription_inner(subscribe, force=force, refresh_channel=refresh_channel)\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            if previous_entries is None:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    delattr(local, \"provider_share_entries\")\n"
    "                except AttributeError:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                local.provider_share_entries = previous_entries\n"
    "            if previous_subscribe is None:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    delattr(local, \"subscribe\")\n"
    "                except AttributeError:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                local.subscribe = previous_subscribe\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # Channel Xunlei -> existing Xunlei flash chain. First attempt consumes channel arrivals;\n"
    "    # passive channel mode never falls through into a network GYING search.\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _channel_xunlei_candidates_v11214(self, subscribe: Any) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        seen = set()\n"
    "        for entry in list((super().get_data(\"channel_index\") or {}).get(\"items\") or []):\n"
    "            if not isinstance(entry, dict) or entry.get(\"stale\"):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            rows = list(entry.get(\"xunlei_sources\") or [])\n"
    "            if not rows:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                matched, _ = _entry_match_reason(entry, subscribe)\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                matched = False\n"
    "            if not matched:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            discovery_title = str(entry.get(\"display_title\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if not discovery_title:\n"
    "                discovery_title = str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            for raw in rows:\n"
    "                if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                share_id = str(raw.get(\"share_id\") or raw.get(\"identity\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                passcode = str(raw.get(\"passcode\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                if not share_id or (share_id, passcode) in seen:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                seen.add((share_id, passcode))\n"
    "                candidates.append({\n"
    "                    \"type\": \"xunlei\",\n"
    "                    \"uri\": str(raw.get(\"uri\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "                    \"identity\": share_id,\n"
    "                    \"share_id\": share_id,\n"
    "                    \"passcode\": passcode,\n"
    "                    \"passcode_present\": bool(passcode),\n"
    "                    \"name\": discovery_title,\n"
    "                    \"search_title\": discovery_title,\n"
    "                    \"year\": entry.get(\"year_hint\"),\n"
    "                    \"provider\": \"channel\",\n"
    "                    \"source_label\": str(entry.get(\"source_label\") or \"频道\"),\n"
    "                    \"message_id\": str(entry.get(\"message_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    \"resource_group_id\": str(entry.get(\"resource_group_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    \"resource_trace_id\": str(entry.get(\"resource_trace_id\") or entry.get(\"trace_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    \"candidate_trace_id\": f\"xunlei:{share_id}\",\n"
    "                })\n"
    "        return candidates\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _dispatch_xunlei_flash(self, subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        local = self._core_local_v11214()\n"
    "        previous = getattr(local, \"xunlei_round\", None)\n"
    "        local.xunlei_round = {\n"
    "            \"subscribe\": subscribe,\n"
    "            \"channel_consumed\": False,\n"
    "            \"mode\": self._route_mode_v11214(),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            result = dict(super()._dispatch_xunlei_flash(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "            if not result.get(\"handled\") and self._route_mode_v11214() != \"channel_event\":\n"
    "                self._hydrate_viewing_guangya_shares_v11214(subscribe)\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            if previous is None:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    delattr(local, \"xunlei_round\")\n"
    "                except AttributeError:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                local.xunlei_round = previous\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _search_viewing_xunlei(self, keyword: str):\n"
    "        local = getattr(self, \"_core_pipeline_local_v11214\", None)\n"
    "        round_state = getattr(local, \"xunlei_round\", None) if local is not None else None\n"
    "        if isinstance(round_state, dict):\n"
    "            subscribe = round_state.get(\"subscribe\")\n"
    "            if not round_state.get(\"channel_consumed\"):\n"
    "                round_state[\"channel_consumed\"] = True\n"
    "                rows = self._channel_xunlei_candidates_v11214(subscribe) if subscribe is not None else []\n"
    "                if rows:\n"
    "                    return rows, {\n"
    "                        \"provider\": \"channel_xunlei\",\n"
    "                        \"success\": True,\n"
    "                        \"channel_only\": round_state.get(\"mode\") == \"channel_event\",\n"
    "                        \"resources\": len(rows),\n"
    "                        \"message\": f\"频道找到 {len(rows)} 个迅雷分享候选\",\n"
    "                    }\n"
    "            if str(round_state.get(\"mode\") or \"\") == \"channel_event\":\n"
    "                return [], {\n"
    "                    \"provider\": \"channel_xunlei\",\n"
    "                    \"success\": True,\n"
    "                    \"channel_only\": True,\n"
    "                    \"resources\": 0,\n"
    "                    \"message\": \"频道事件未找到可用迅雷分享；不主动访问 GYING\",\n"
    "                }\n"
    "        return super()._search_viewing_xunlei(keyword)\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # GYING panlist GuangYa share -> temporary direct-share entry.\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _hydrate_viewing_guangya_shares_v11214(self, subscribe: Any) -> int:\n"
    "        if not bool(getattr(self, \"_viewing_enabled\", False)):\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "        keyword = str(self._provider_keyword(subscribe) or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not keyword:\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            rows, state = self._gying_raw_results(keyword, force=False)\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\"WARNING\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【观影光鸭分享v1.12.14】读取 GYING 结果失败：%s\", str(err)[:240])\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "        if not bool((state or {}).get(\"success\")):\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "\n"
    "        entries: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        seen = set()\n"
    "        for raw in rows or []:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            search_title = str(raw.get(\"search_title\") or raw.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            discovery = {\n"
    "                \"search_title\": search_title,\n"
    "                \"name\": str(raw.get(\"name\") or search_title),\n"
    "                \"year\": raw.get(\"year\"),\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                if not self._provider_candidate_matches(subscribe, discovery):\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            value = str(raw.get(\"url\") or raw.get(\"uri\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            share_url = _canonical_share_url(value, f\"{search_title} {raw.get('name') or ''}\")\n"
    "            share_key = _share_identity(share_url)\n"
    "            if not share_key or share_key in seen:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            seen.add(share_key)\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            group_id = hashlib.sha256(f\"viewing-guangya|{sid}|{share_key}\".encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()[:24]\n"
    "            entry = {\n"
    "                \"share_url\": share_url,\n"
    "                \"share_id\": share_key.split(\"|\", 1)[0],\n"
    "                \"text\": \"\\n\".join(value for value in (search_title, str(raw.get(\"name\") or \"\")) if value)[:2200],\n"
    "                \"source_url\": \"provider://viewing\",\n"
    "                \"source_label\": \"观影光鸭分享\",\n"
    "                \"priority\": 0,\n"
    "                \"link_style\": \"观影网盘\",\n"
    "                \"stale\": False,\n"
    "                \"cached_index\": True,\n"
    "                \"display_title\": search_title or str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                \"year_hint\": raw.get(\"year\"),\n"
    "                \"episode_hint\": \"\",\n"
    "                \"message_id\": f\"viewing-{group_id}\",\n"
    "                \"resource_group_id\": group_id,\n"
    "                \"external_sources\": [],\n"
    "                \"candidate_types\": [\"guangya\"],\n"
    "                \"provider_origin_v11214\": True,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            media_id = str(getattr(subscribe, \"media_id\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            # 只在订阅本身就是 TMDB 精确身份且 GYING discovery 已通过 provider matcher 时附加该身份。\n"
    "            # 最终真实分享文件仍会经过下面的 actual-content gate，TMDB discovery 不能覆盖硬冲突。\n"
    "            if media_id.isdigit() and is_tmdb_source(getattr(subscribe, \"media_source\", None)):\n"
    "                entry[\"tmdb_id\"] = media_id\n"
    "            entries.append(entry)\n"
    "\n"
    "        local = self._core_local_v11214()\n"
    "        local.provider_share_entries = entries\n"
    "        if entries:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【观影光鸭分享v1.12.14】#%s %s 发现 %s 个光鸭分享，进入直接转存候选\",\n"
    "                getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0), getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\"), len(entries),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        return len(entries)\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # Exact TMDB official aliases for TV/anime GYING recall.\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _tmdb_id_tv_v11214(subscribe: Any) -> str:\n"
