    "        raw_type = str(getattr(subscribe, \"type\", \"\") or \"\").lower()\n"
    "        if \"movie\" in raw_type or \"电影\" in str(getattr(subscribe, \"type\", \"\") or \"\"):\n"
    "            return \"\"\n"
    "        for field in (\"tmdb_id\", \"tmdbid\"):\n"
    "            value = str(getattr(subscribe, field, \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if value.isdigit():\n"
    "                return value\n"
    "        media_id = str(getattr(subscribe, \"media_id\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if media_id.isdigit() and is_tmdb_source(getattr(subscribe, \"media_source\", None)):\n"
    "            return media_id\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _flatten_aliases_v11214(value: Any) -> List[str]:\n"
    "        if value in (None, \"\"):\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "        if isinstance(value, str):\n"
    "            return [value]\n"
    "        if isinstance(value, dict):\n"
    "            output: List[str] = []\n"
    "            for item in value.values():\n"
    "                output.extend(GuangYaCorePipelineV11214Mixin._flatten_aliases_v11214(item))\n"
    "            return output\n"
    "        if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):\n"
    "            output: List[str] = []\n"
    "            for item in value:\n"
    "                output.extend(GuangYaCorePipelineV11214Mixin._flatten_aliases_v11214(item))\n"
    "            return output\n"
    "        return [str(value)]\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _tv_tmdb_aliases_v11214(self, subscribe: Any) -> List[str]:\n"
    "        tmdb_id = self._tmdb_id_tv_v11214(subscribe)\n"
    "        if not tmdb_id:\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "        key = f\"tv:{tmdb_id}\"\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "        lock = getattr(self, \"_tv_alias_lock_v11214\", None) or threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._tv_alias_lock_v11214 = lock\n"
    "        cache = getattr(self, \"_tv_alias_cache_v11214\", None)\n"
    "        if not isinstance(cache, dict):\n"
    "            cache = {}\n"
    "            self._tv_alias_cache_v11214 = cache\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            cached = dict(cache.get(key) or {})\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                age = now - float(cached.get(\"at\") or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                age = 10**9\n"
    "            ttl = self._tv_alias_cache_ttl_v11214 if cached.get(\"ok\") else self._tv_alias_failure_ttl_v11214\n"
    "            if cached and age < float(ttl):\n"
    "                return list(cached.get(\"aliases\") or [])\n"
    "\n"
    "        info = None\n"
    "        error = \"\"\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            recognize = getattr(self, \"_recognize_media_cached_v208\", None)\n"
    "            if callable(recognize):\n"
    "                info = recognize(mtype=MediaType.TV, media_source=MediaSource.TMDB, media_id=tmdb_id)\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                info = MediaChain().recognize_media(mtype=MediaType.TV, media_source=MediaSource.TMDB, media_id=tmdb_id)\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            error = str(err)\n"
    "        valid = bool(info)\n"
    "        aliases: List[str] = []\n"
    "        if info:\n"
    "            returned_id = str(\n"
    "                (info.get(\"tmdb_id\") or info.get(\"media_id\") or \"\") if isinstance(info, dict)\n"
    "                else (getattr(info, \"tmdb_id\", None) or getattr(info, \"media_id\", None) or \"\")\n"
    "            ).strip()\n"
    "            if returned_id and returned_id != tmdb_id:\n"
    "                valid = False\n"
    "                error = f\"TMDB返回身份不一致：期望={tmdb_id} 实际={returned_id}\"\n"
    "            if valid:\n"
    "                values: List[str] = []\n"
    "                for field in (\"title\", \"name\", \"en_title\", \"original_title\", \"original_name\", \"cn_name\", \"hk_title\", \"tw_title\", \"aka\", \"aliases\", \"alias\"):\n"
    "                    raw = info.get(field) if isinstance(info, dict) else getattr(info, field, None)\n"
    "                    values.extend(self._flatten_aliases_v11214(raw))\n"
    "                seen = set()\n"
    "                for raw in values:\n"
    "                    text = \" \".join(str(raw or \"\").split())\n"
    "                    token = text.casefold()\n"
    "                    if len(text) >= 2 and token not in seen:\n"
    "                        seen.add(token)\n"
    "                        aliases.append(text)\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            cache[key] = {\"at\": now, \"ok\": bool(valid and aliases), \"aliases\": aliases, \"error\": error[:240]}\n"
    "        return aliases if valid else []\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_alias_keywords_v11212(self, subscribe: Any, primary: str) -> List[str]:\n"
    "        rows = list(super()._gying_alias_keywords_v11212(subscribe, primary) or [])\n"
    "        if subscribe is None or self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return rows\n"
    "        aliases = self._tv_tmdb_aliases_v11214(subscribe)\n"
    "        if not aliases:\n"
    "            return rows\n"
    "        year = str(getattr(subscribe, \"year\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            season = int(getattr(subscribe, \"season\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            season = 0\n"
    "        seen = {re.sub(r\"[^0-9a-z\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+\", \"\", str(value or \"\").casefold()) for value in rows}\n"
    "        limit = max(2, int(getattr(self, \"_gying_alias_query_limit_v11212\", 4) or 4))\n"
    "        for alias in aliases:\n"
    "            title = \" \".join(str(alias or \"\").split())\n"
    "            if not title:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            parts = [title]\n"
    "            if year:\n"
    "                parts.append(year)\n"
    "            if season > 0:\n"
    "                parts.append(f\"S{season:02d}\")\n"
    "            candidate = \" \".join(parts)\n"
    "            token = re.sub(r\"[^0-9a-z\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+\", \"\", candidate.casefold())\n"
    "            if token and token not in seen:\n"
    "                seen.add(token)\n"
    "                rows.append(candidate)\n"
    "            if len(rows) >= limit:\n"
    "                break\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # Authoritative TV gap. This is used at the final physical-file boundary.\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _other_source_claims_v11214(self, subscribe_id: int, current_source_id: str = \"\") -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        claims: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            items = (self._source_store() or {}).get(\"items\") or {}\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return claims\n"
    "        for source_id, row in items.items():\n"
    "            if not isinstance(row, dict) or int(row.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0) != int(subscribe_id or 0):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if str(source_id or row.get(\"id\") or \"\") == str(current_source_id or \"\"):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if str(row.get(\"state\") or \"new\") not in _ACTIVE_EXTERNAL_STATES_V11214:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            claims.update(_positive_episode_set_v11214(row.get(\"resolved_episodes\") or row.get(\"target_episodes\") or []))\n"
    "        return claims\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _authoritative_missing_v11214(self, subscribe: Any, *, current_source_id: str = \"\") -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return set()\n"
    "        # Reuse v1.12.13 fail-closed library reader; it both refreshes MoviePilot and validates the returned gap.\n"
    "        library_missing = set(self._library_missing_v11213(subscribe))\n"
    "        logical_missing = _positive_episode_set_v11214(self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or [])\n"
    "        if logical_missing:\n"
    "            allowed = library_missing.intersection(logical_missing)\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            allowed = set(library_missing)\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            reservations = self._pending_reservations(subscribe)\n"
    "            allowed -= _positive_episode_set_v11214((reservations or {}).get(\"episodes\") or [])\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        allowed -= self._other_source_claims_v11214(sid, current_source_id=current_source_id)\n"
    "        return allowed\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _resolved_episode_set_v11214(self, subscribe: Any, name: str, package_paths: Sequence[str], episode_hint: str = \"\") -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        result = resolve_episode(\n"
    "            str(name or \"\"),\n"
    "            package_paths=list(package_paths or []),\n"
    "            season_hint=getattr(subscribe, \"season\", None),\n"
    "            episode_hint=str(episode_hint or \"\"),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        threshold = float(getattr(self, \"_episode_auto_confidence\", AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE) or AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE)\n"
    "        return set(reliable_episode_set(result, threshold))\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # GuangYa direct share: actual-content hard conflict + authoritative physical subset.\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _direct_share_primary_roots_v11214(paths: Sequence[str], expected_year: Any = None) -> List[str]:\n"
    "        roots = {\n"
    "            str(path or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").split(\"/\", 1)[0].strip()\n"
    "            for path in paths or [] if \"/\" in str(path or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\")\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        if len(roots) != 1:\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "        root = next(iter(roots))\n"
    "        key = title_key_v1111(root, expected_year=expected_year).casefold()\n"
    "        if len(key) < 3 or key in _GENERIC_SHARE_ROOTS_V11214 or re.fullmatch(r\"s\\d{1,2}|season\\d{1,2}\", key):\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "        return [root]\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _identity_stats_snapshot_v208(self, probe: Dict[str, Any], video_paths: Sequence[str], *, reason: str = \"\") -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        files = [row for row in (probe.get(\"files\") or []) if isinstance(row, dict)]\n"
    "        subtitle_paths = [\n"
    "            str(row.get(\"relative_path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\")\n"
    "            for row in files\n"
    "            if _is_subtitle(str(row.get(\"relative_path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"total\": len(files),\n"
    "            \"video\": len(video_paths),\n"
    "            \"subtitle\": len(subtitle_paths),\n"
    "            \"eligible\": 0,\n"
    "            \"episode\": 0,\n"
    "            \"identity_reject_v11214\": 1,\n"
    "            \"identity_reason\": str(reason or \"\")[:320],\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _plan_incremental_files(self, probe: Dict[str, Any], assets: Dict[str, Any], subscribe: Any = None, target_path: str = \"\", stats: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None):\n"
    "        if subscribe is None or self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return super()._plan_incremental_files(probe, assets, subscribe=subscribe, target_path=target_path, stats=stats)\n"
    "\n"
    "        video_paths = [\n"
    "            str(row.get(\"relative_path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\")\n"
    "            for row in (probe.get(\"files\") or []) if isinstance(row, dict)\n"
    "            and _is_video(str(row.get(\"relative_path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        primary_roots = self._direct_share_primary_roots_v11214(video_paths, getattr(subscribe, \"year\", None))\n"
    "        share_identity = extract_share_media_identity_v1111(list(primary_roots) + list(video_paths[:300]))\n"
    "        source_raw = getattr(subscribe, \"media_source\", None)\n"
    "        source_normalized = normalize_media_source_token(source_raw) or \"-\"\n"
    "        subscription_tmdb = str(self._tmdb_id_tv_v11214(subscribe) or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        share_tmdb_ids = [str(value).strip() for value in (share_identity.get(\"tmdb_ids\") or []) if str(value).strip().isdigit()]\n"
    "        share_tmdb = str(share_identity.get(\"tmdb_id\") or (share_tmdb_ids[0] if share_tmdb_ids else \"\")).strip()\n"
    "\n"
    "        if subscription_tmdb and share_tmdb_ids:\n"
    "            unique_share = sorted(set(share_tmdb_ids))\n"
    "            if len(unique_share) == 1 and unique_share[0] == subscription_tmdb:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"INFO\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【最终身份】sid=%s source_raw=%s source_normalized=%s subscription_tmdb=%s share_tmdb=%s alias_match=- decision=confirmed reason=TMDB_MATCH\",\n"
    "                    getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0),\n"
    "                    source_raw if source_raw not in (None, \"\") else \"-\",\n"
    "                    source_normalized,\n"
    "                    subscription_tmdb,\n"
    "                    unique_share[0],\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                assessment = {\n"
    "                    \"ok\": True, \"hard_conflict\": False, \"reason\": \"TMDB_MATCH\",\n"
    "                    \"reason_code\": \"TMDB_MATCH\", \"stage\": \"IDENTITY\", \"decision\": \"confirmed\",\n"
    "                    \"evidence\": {\"expected_tmdb\": subscription_tmdb, \"actual_tmdb\": unique_share[0]},\n"
    "                }\n"
    "                if stats is not None:\n"
    "                    stats[\"identity_reason_code\"] = \"TMDB_MATCH\"\n"
    "                    stats[\"identity_diag\"] = assessment\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                reason = f\"tmdb_mismatch subscription={subscription_tmdb} share={','.join(unique_share)}\"\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【最终身份】sid=%s source_raw=%s source_normalized=%s subscription_tmdb=%s share_tmdb=%s alias_match=- decision=hard_reject reason=TMDB_ID_MISMATCH\",\n"
    "                    getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0),\n"
    "                    source_raw if source_raw not in (None, \"\") else \"-\",\n"
    "                    source_normalized,\n"
    "                    subscription_tmdb,\n"
    "                    \",\".join(unique_share),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if stats is not None:\n"
    "                    stats.update(self._identity_stats_snapshot_v208(probe, video_paths, reason=reason))\n"
    "                    stats[\"identity_reason_code\"] = \"TMDB_ID_MISMATCH\"\n"
    "                    stats[\"identity_diag\"] = {\n"
    "                        \"ok\": False, \"reason_code\": \"TMDB_ID_MISMATCH\", \"stage\": \"IDENTITY\",\n"
    "                        \"state\": \"FAILED_FINAL\",\n"
    "                        \"evidence\": {\"expected_tmdb\": subscription_tmdb, \"actual_tmdb\": unique_share},\n"
    "                    }\n"
    "                return []\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            aliases = list(self._identity_aliases_v1111(subscribe) or []) if hasattr(self, \"_identity_aliases_v1111\") else [str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\")]\n"
    "            seen = {str(value or \"\").strip().casefold() for value in aliases if str(value or \"\").strip()}\n"
    "            for value in self._tv_tmdb_aliases_v11214(subscribe):\n"
    "                text = str(value or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                key = text.casefold()\n"
    "                if text and key not in seen:\n"
    "                    seen.add(key)\n"
    "                    aliases.append(text)\n"
    "            assessment = assess_media_identity_v1111(\n"
    "                aliases=aliases,\n"
    "                expected_year=getattr(subscribe, \"year\", None),\n"
    "                expected_season=getattr(subscribe, \"season\", None),\n"
    "                is_movie=False,\n"
    "                primary_evidences=primary_roots,\n"
    "                file_evidences=video_paths[:300],\n"
    "                discovery_evidences=(),\n"
    "                threshold=100,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            if assessment.get(\"hard_conflict\"):\n"
    "                reason = str(assessment.get(\"reason\") or \"title_unconfirmed\")\n"
    "                reason_code = str(assessment.get(\"reason_code\") or \"MEDIA_IDENTITY_UNCONFIRMED\")\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【最终身份】sid=%s source_raw=%s source_normalized=%s subscription_tmdb=%s share_tmdb=%s alias_match=%s decision=hard_reject reason=%s reason_code=%s\",\n"
    "                    getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0),\n"
    "                    source_raw if source_raw not in (None, \"\") else \"-\",\n"
    "                    source_normalized,\n"
    "                    subscription_tmdb or \"-\",\n"
    "                    share_tmdb or \"-\",\n"
    "                    \"true\" if any(a for a in aliases[1:]) else \"-\",\n"
    "                    reason[:240],\n"
    "                    reason_code,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if stats is not None:\n"
    "                    stats.update(self._identity_stats_snapshot_v208(probe, video_paths, reason=reason))\n"
    "                    stats[\"identity_reason_code\"] = reason_code\n"
    "                    stats[\"identity_diag\"] = {\n"
    "                        \"ok\": False,\n"
    "                        \"reason_code\": reason_code,\n"
    "                        \"stage\": \"IDENTITY\",\n"
    "                        \"state\": \"FAILED_FINAL\",\n"
    "                        \"evidence\": assessment.get(\"evidence\") or {},\n"
    "                        \"message\": reason[:320],\n"
    "                    }\n"
    "                return []\n"
    "            if assessment.get(\"ok\"):\n"
    "                reason_code = str(assessment.get(\"reason_code\") or \"OFFICIAL_ALIAS_MATCH\")\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"INFO\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【最终身份】sid=%s source_raw=%s source_normalized=%s subscription_tmdb=%s share_tmdb=%s alias_match=%s decision=confirmed reason=%s reason_code=%s\",\n"
    "                    getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0),\n"
    "                    source_raw if source_raw not in (None, \"\") else \"-\",\n"
    "                    source_normalized,\n"
    "                    subscription_tmdb or \"-\",\n"
    "                    share_tmdb or \"-\",\n"
    "                    \"true\",\n"
    "                    str(assessment.get(\"reason\") or \"alias_or_title\")[:240],\n"
    "                    reason_code,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if stats is not None:\n"
    "                    stats[\"identity_reason_code\"] = reason_code\n"
    "                    stats[\"identity_diag\"] = {\n"
    "                        \"ok\": True,\n"
    "                        \"reason_code\": reason_code,\n"
    "                        \"stage\": \"IDENTITY\",\n"
    "                        \"state\": \"CONTINUE\",\n"
    "                    }\n"
    "\n"
    "        allowed = self._authoritative_missing_v11214(subscribe)\n"
    "        planned = list(super()._plan_incremental_files(probe, assets, subscribe=subscribe, target_path=target_path, stats=stats) or [])\n"
    "        if not planned:\n"
    "            return planned\n"
    "        package_paths = [str(row.get(\"relative_path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\") for row in (probe.get(\"files\") or []) if isinstance(row, dict)]\n"
    "        safe_videos: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        safe_video_eps: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        for row in planned:\n"
    "            path = str(row.get(\"effective_path\") or row.get(\"relative_path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\")\n"
    "            if not _is_video(path):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            episodes = self._resolved_episode_set_v11214(subscribe, path, package_paths)\n"
    "            if _physical_episode_subset_v11214(episodes, allowed):\n"
    "                safe_videos.append(row)\n"
    "                safe_video_eps.update(episodes)\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【物理缺集栅栏v1.12.14】光鸭分享拒绝不可分割文件：%s actual=%s allowed=%s\",\n"
    "                    path[:220], sorted(episodes), sorted(allowed),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "        if not safe_videos:\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "        safe: List[Dict[str, Any]] = list(safe_videos)\n"
    "        parents = {\n"
    "            str(row.get(\"effective_path\") or row.get(\"relative_path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").rsplit(\"/\", 1)[0].lower()\n"
    "            for row in safe_videos\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        for row in planned:\n"
    "            path = str(row.get(\"effective_path\") or row.get(\"relative_path\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\")\n"
    "            if not _is_subtitle(path):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            episodes = self._resolved_episode_set_v11214(subscribe, path, package_paths)\n"
    "            parent = path.replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").rsplit(\"/\", 1)[0].lower() if \"/\" in path.replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\") else \"\"\n"
    "            if episodes:\n"
    "                if _physical_episode_subset_v11214(episodes, allowed) and episodes.intersection(safe_video_eps):\n"
    "                    safe.append(row)\n"
    "            elif len(safe_videos) == 1 and parent in parents:\n"
    "                safe.append(row)\n"
    "        return safe\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # Magnet / ED2K: final physical file set must be a subset of authoritative missing.\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _resolve_offline_source(self, source: Dict[str, Any], subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        result = dict(super()._resolve_offline_source(source, subscribe) or {})\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        source_id = str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\")\n"
    "        allowed = self._authoritative_missing_v11214(subscribe, current_source_id=source_id)\n"
    "        resolve_data = result.get(\"resolve_data\") if isinstance(result.get(\"resolve_data\"), dict) else {}\n"
    "        rows = self._media_rows(resolve_data)\n"
    "        media_rows = [row for row in rows if row.get(\"video\") or row.get(\"subtitle\")]\n"
    "        package_paths = [str(row.get(\"name\") or \"\") for row in media_rows]\n"
    "        selected = {int(v) for v in (result.get(\"selected_indexes\") or [])}\n"
    "        episode_hint = str(source.get(\"episode_hint\") or \"\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        # ED2K single-file resolve may not expose btResInfo.subfiles.\n"
    "        if not media_rows:\n"
    "            actual_name = str(result.get(\"resolved_name\") or source.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            episodes = self._resolved_episode_set_v11214(subscribe, actual_name, [actual_name] if actual_name else [], episode_hint)\n"
    "            if not _physical_episode_subset_v11214(episodes, allowed):\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "                    f\"EPISODE_AMBIGUOUS:物理缺集栅栏拒绝 {source.get('type') or '外部来源'} 文件：\"\n"
    "                    f\"{actual_name or '-'} actual={sorted(episodes)} allowed={sorted(allowed)}\"\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            if source_id:\n"
    "                self._update_source(source_id, resolved_episodes=sorted(episodes))\n"
    "            result[\"physical_episodes_v11214\"] = sorted(episodes)\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        safe_video_indexes: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        safe_video_eps: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        resolutions: Dict[int, Set[int]] = {}\n"
    "        for row in media_rows:\n"
    "            index = int(row.get(\"index\") or 0)\n"
    "            if selected and index not in selected:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            episodes = self._resolved_episode_set_v11214(subscribe, str(row.get(\"name\") or \"\"), package_paths, episode_hint)\n"
    "            resolutions[index] = episodes\n"
    "            if row.get(\"video\") and _physical_episode_subset_v11214(episodes, allowed):\n"
    "                safe_video_indexes.add(index)\n"
    "                safe_video_eps.update(episodes)\n"
    "            elif row.get(\"video\"):\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【物理缺集栅栏v1.12.14】%s 拒绝不可分割文件：%s actual=%s allowed=%s\",\n"
    "                    str(source.get(\"type\") or \"外部来源\"), str(row.get(\"name\") or \"\")[:220], sorted(episodes), sorted(allowed),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "        if not safe_video_indexes:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"EPISODE_AMBIGUOUS:解析成功但没有物理文件能够完整落在当前真实缺集集合内\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        safe_indexes: Set[int] = set(safe_video_indexes)\n"
    "        safe_parents = {\n"
    "            str(row.get(\"name\") or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").rsplit(\"/\", 1)[0].lower()\n"
    "            for row in media_rows if int(row.get(\"index\") or 0) in safe_video_indexes\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        for row in media_rows:\n"
    "            if not row.get(\"subtitle\"):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            index = int(row.get(\"index\") or 0)\n"
    "            if selected and index not in selected:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            episodes = resolutions.get(index)\n"
    "            if episodes is None:\n"
    "                episodes = self._resolved_episode_set_v11214(subscribe, str(row.get(\"name\") or \"\"), package_paths, episode_hint)\n"
    "            parent = str(row.get(\"name\") or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").rsplit(\"/\", 1)[0].lower() if \"/\" in str(row.get(\"name\") or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\") else \"\"\n"
    "            if episodes:\n"
    "                if _physical_episode_subset_v11214(episodes, allowed) and episodes.intersection(safe_video_eps):\n"
    "                    safe_indexes.add(index)\n"
    "            elif len(safe_video_indexes) == 1 and parent in safe_parents:\n"
    "                safe_indexes.add(index)\n"
    "\n"
    "        result[\"selected_indexes\"] = sorted(safe_indexes)\n"
    "        result[\"physical_episodes_v11214\"] = sorted(safe_video_eps)\n"
    "        if source_id:\n"
    "            self._update_source(\n"
    "                source_id,\n"
    "                resolved_episodes=sorted(safe_video_eps),\n"
    "                selected_indexes=sorted(safe_indexes),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【物理缺集栅栏v1.12.14】#%s %s source=%s allowed=%s physical=%s indexes=%s\",\n"
    "            getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0), getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\"), str(source.get(\"type\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            sorted(allowed), sorted(safe_video_eps), sorted(safe_indexes),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\n"
    "    \"GuangYaCorePipelineV11214Mixin\",\n"
    "    \"_physical_episode_subset_v11214\",\n"
    "]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== gying_auth_v1107.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["gying_auth_v1107"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.10.7 观影人工汉字验证码与会话恢复层。\n"
    "\n"
    "依据用户提供的 2026-09-02 观影前端代码：\n"
    "- POST /res/captcha/2 with webp=1 -> {type, img, text}\n"
    "- 用户按 text 顺序点击画布，提交 do=check & info=x,y-...;width;height\n"
    "- check 返回 code == 200 后，同一 captchainfo 直接作为 /user/login 的 code\n"
    "- 所有请求继续复用同一个 requests.Session；站点 refresh=1 / 浏览器计算验证允许连续恢复最多 3 次\n"
    "\n"
    "本层不会自动识别或点击汉字验证码。验证码图片和提示文字只回传给当前 MoviePilot\n"
    "页面，点击动作由用户本人完成。Cookie、账号密码、PoW 参数和点击坐标不会写入公开状态或日志。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import base64\n"
    "import json\n"
    "import re\n"
    "import secrets\n"
    "import threading\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple\n"
    "from urllib.parse import quote\n"
    "\n"
    "import requests\n"
    "\n"
    "from .gying_hardening_v193 import canonical_gying_node\n"
    "from .gying_runtime_v193 import (\n"
    "    GuangYaGyingRuntimeMixin,\n"
    "    _is_challenge_text,\n"
    "    _normalize_node_url,\n"
    ")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_JSON_IMAGE_TYPE_RE = re.compile(r\"^[A-Za-z0-9.+-]{1,24}$\")\n"
    "_CAPTCHA_WIDTH = 315\n"
    "_CAPTCHA_HEIGHT = 180\n"
    "_CAPTCHA_GRID = 15\n"
    "_CAPTCHA_TTL_SECONDS = 180\n"
    "_AUTH_COOKIE_MODES_V1107 = {\n"
    "    \"manual_captcha\",\n"
    "    \"password\",\n"
    "    \"cookie\",\n"
    "    \"cookie_reuse\",\n"
    "    \"configured_cookie\",\n"
    "}\n"
    "_LANDING_MARKERS_V1107 = (\n"
    "    \"当前网址将在不久后失效\",\n"
    "    \"获取新网址\",\n"
    "    \"地址发布页\",\n"
    ")\n"
    "_MAINTENANCE_MARKERS_V1107 = (\n"
    "    \"站点维护中\",\n"
    "    \"该站点维护中\",\n"
    "    \"站点正在维护\",\n"
    ")\n"
    "_BLOCK_PAGE_MARKERS_V1107 = (\n"
    "    \"Angie\",\n"
    "    \"request forbidden\",\n"
    "    \"access denied\",\n"
    ")\n"
    "_CURRENT_FRONTEND_SEEDS_V1107 = (\n"
    "    \"https://www.肖申克的救赎.com\",\n"
    "    \"https://www.阿甘正传.com\",\n"
    "    \"https://www.盗梦空间.com\",\n"
    "    \"https://www.星际穿越.com\",\n"
    ")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _safe_json(response: requests.Response) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        payload = response.json()\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            payload = json.loads(str(getattr(response, \"text\", \"\") or \"{}\"))\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            payload = {}\n"
    "    return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _json_refresh_required(response: requests.Response) -> bool:\n"
    "    payload = _safe_json(response)\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        return int(payload.get(\"refresh\") or 0) == 1\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "        return False\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _looks_like_content_node(response: requests.Response) -> bool:\n"
    "    text = str(getattr(response, \"text\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "    if any(marker in text for marker in _LANDING_MARKERS_V1107):\n"
    "        return False\n"
    "    if any(marker in text for marker in _MAINTENANCE_MARKERS_V1107):\n"
    "        return False\n"
    "    return int(getattr(response, \"status_code\", 0) or 0) < 400\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _captcha_info(points: List[Tuple[int, int]], width: int, height: int) -> str:\n"
    "    if not points:\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    return f\"{'-'.join(f'{x},{y}' for x, y in points)};{int(width)};{int(height)}\"\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _extract_node_values(payload: Any) -> List[str]:\n"
    "    rows: List[str] = []\n"
    "\n"
    "    def visit(value: Any) -> None:\n"
    "        if isinstance(value, dict):\n"
    "            for child in value.values():\n"
    "                visit(child)\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):\n"
    "            for child in value:\n"
    "                visit(child)\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        text = str(value or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not text:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        if text.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n"
    "            rows.append(text)\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        if \".\" in text and \" \" not in text and len(text) <= 255:\n"
    "            rows.append(\"https://\" + text)\n"
    "\n"
    "    visit(payload)\n"
    "    return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaGyingAuthV1107Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"人工点击验证码、PoW 连续恢复、会话验证与 MoviePilot 交互 UI。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260902-r18\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        super().init_plugin(dict(config or {}))\n"
    "        self._gying_auth_sessions: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        self._gying_auth_active_id = \"\"\n"
    "        self._gying_auth_lock = threading.RLock()\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 日志 / 临时会话\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _gying_auth_log(self, level: str, message: str, *args: Any) -> None:\n"
    "        writer = getattr(self, \"_gying_obs_log\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(writer):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                writer(level, message, *args)\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "        fallback = getattr(self, \"_plugin_log\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(fallback):\n"
    "            fallback(level, \"【光鸭转存助手】【观影】\" + message, *args)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_auth_cleanup(self) -> None:\n"
    "        store = getattr(self, \"_gying_auth_sessions\", None)\n"
    "        if not isinstance(store, dict):\n"
    "            self._gying_auth_sessions = {}\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "        expired = []\n"
    "        for auth_id, row in list(store.items()):\n"
    "            updated = float((row or {}).get(\"updated_ts\") or (row or {}).get(\"created_ts\") or 0)\n"
    "            if not updated or now - updated > _CAPTCHA_TTL_SECONDS:\n"
    "                expired.append(auth_id)\n"
    "        for auth_id in expired:\n"
    "            row = store.pop(auth_id, None) or {}\n"
    "            session = row.get(\"session\")\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                if session:\n"
    "                    session.close()\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "            if str(getattr(self, \"_gying_auth_active_id\", \"\") or \"\") == auth_id:\n"
    "                self._gying_auth_active_id = \"\"\n"
    "        if len(store) > 4:\n"
    "            ordered = sorted(\n"
    "                store.items(),\n"
    "                key=lambda pair: float((pair[1] or {}).get(\"updated_ts\") or 0),\n"
    "                reverse=True,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            for auth_id, row in ordered[4:]:\n"
    "                store.pop(auth_id, None)\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    session = (row or {}).get(\"session\")\n"
    "                    if session:\n"
    "                        session.close()\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_auth_get(self, auth_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        with getattr(self, \"_gying_auth_lock\", threading.RLock()):\n"
    "            self._gying_auth_cleanup()\n"
    "            row = (getattr(self, \"_gying_auth_sessions\", {}) or {}).get(str(auth_id or \"\"))\n"
    "            if isinstance(row, dict):\n"
    "                row[\"updated_ts\"] = time.time()\n"
    "                return row\n"
    "        return None\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_auth_public(self, row: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        if not isinstance(row, dict):\n"
    "            return {\"stage\": \"idle\", \"active\": False}\n"
    "        captcha = dict(row.get(\"captcha\") or {})\n"
    "        stage = str(row.get(\"stage\") or \"idle\")\n"
    "        data: Dict[str, Any] = {\n"
    "            \"active\": True,\n"
    "            \"auth_id\": str(row.get(\"auth_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"stage\": stage,\n"
    "            \"node\": str(row.get(\"node\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"message\": str(row.get(\"message\") or \"\")[:300],\n"
    "            \"clicked\": len(list(row.get(\"points\") or [])),\n"
    "            \"required\": len(str(captcha.get(\"text\") or \"\")),\n"
    "            \"expires_in\": max(\n"
    "                0,\n"
    "                int(\n"
    "                    _CAPTCHA_TTL_SECONDS\n"
    "                    - (time.time() - float(row.get(\"updated_ts\") or row.get(\"created_ts\") or time.time()))\n"
    "                ),\n"
    "            ),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        if stage == \"captcha\" and captcha:\n"
    "            image_type = str(captcha.get(\"type\") or \"webp\")\n"
    "            image = str(captcha.get(\"img\") or \"\")\n"
    "            data[\"captcha\"] = {\n"
    "                \"type\": image_type,\n"
    "                \"image\": f\"data:image/{image_type};base64,{image}\" if image else \"\",\n"
    "                \"text\": str(captcha.get(\"text\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                \"width\": int(captcha.get(\"width\") or _CAPTCHA_WIDTH),\n"
    "                \"height\": int(captcha.get(\"height\") or _CAPTCHA_HEIGHT),\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        return data\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 节点发现：保留旧节点池，再读取 /urlop/，并吸收当前站点前端公布的内容域名\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _discover_gying_nodes(self, force: bool = False) -> List[str]:\n"
    "        rows = list(super()._discover_gying_nodes(force=force) or [])\n"
    "        registry_rows = []\n"
    "        for raw in str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_registry_urls\", \"\") or \"\").splitlines():\n"
    "            node = canonical_gying_node(raw)\n"
    "            if node:\n"
    "                registry_rows.append(node)\n"
    "        session = self._gying_new_session(\"\")\n"
    "        timeout = min(int(getattr(self, \"_provider_timeout\", 15) or 15), 20)\n"
    "        for registry in registry_rows[:6]:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                response = session.get(\n"
    "                    registry.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/urlop/\",\n"
    "                    timeout=timeout,\n"
    "                    allow_redirects=True,\n"
    "                    headers={\"Accept\": \"application/json,text/plain,*/*\"},\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                payload = _safe_json(response)\n"
    "                for value in _extract_node_values(payload):\n"
    "                    node = canonical_gying_node(value)\n"
    "                    if node and node not in rows:\n"
    "                        rows.append(node)\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            session.close()\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        for value in _CURRENT_FRONTEND_SEEDS_V1107:\n"
    "            node = canonical_gying_node(value)\n"
    "            if node and node not in rows:\n"
    "                rows.append(node)\n"
    "        state = self._gying_state()\n"
    "        state[\"discovered_nodes\"] = rows[:40]\n"
    "        state[\"discovered_at\"] = time.time()\n"
    "        self._save_gying_state(state)\n"
    "        return rows[:40]\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # PoW：一次原请求最多连续恢复 3 次，只有原请求恢复正常才记录“确认通过”\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _gying_solve_challenge(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        session: requests.Session,\n"
    "        node: str,\n"
    "        response: requests.Response,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        label = str(node or \"\")\n"
    "        self._gying_auth_log(\"INFO\", \"检测到浏览器 PoW：节点=%s，开始计算提交\", label)\n"
    "        started = time.monotonic()\n"
    "        result = GuangYaGyingRuntimeMixin._gying_solve_challenge(self, session, node, response)\n"
    "        self._gying_auth_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"PoW计算提交完成：节点=%s 模式=%s 耗时=%.2fs，等待原请求确认\",\n"
    "            label,\n"
    "            str((result or {}).get(\"mode\") or \"unknown\"),\n"
    "            time.monotonic() - started,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return dict(result or {})\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_request(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        session: requests.Session,\n"
    "        node: str,\n"
    "        method: str,\n"
    "        url: str,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        retry_challenge: bool = True,\n"
    "        **kwargs: Any,\n"
    "    ) -> requests.Response:\n"
    "        timeout = kwargs.pop(\"timeout\", int(getattr(self, \"_provider_timeout\", 15) or 15))\n"
    "        max_pow = 3 if retry_challenge else 0\n"
    "        solved = 0\n"
    "        for attempt in range(max_pow + 1):\n"
    "            response = session.request(\n"
    "                str(method or \"GET\").upper(),\n"
    "                url,\n"
    "                timeout=timeout,\n"
    "                allow_redirects=True,\n"
    "                **kwargs,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            text = str(response.text or \"\")\n"
    "            lowered = text.lower()\n"
    "            if response.status_code in {403, 404} and any(\n"
    "                marker.lower() in lowered for marker in _BLOCK_PAGE_MARKERS_V1107\n"
    "            ):\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(f\"观影节点当前出口被阻断：HTTP {response.status_code}\")\n"
    "            challenge = _is_challenge_text(text) or _json_refresh_required(response)\n"
    "            if not challenge:\n"
    "                if solved:\n"
    "                    self._gying_auth_log(\n"
    "                        \"INFO\",\n"
    "                        \"浏览器计算验证确认通过：节点=%s 连续挑战=%s\",\n"
    "                        str(node or \"\"),\n"
    "                        solved,\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                return response\n"
    "            if attempt >= max_pow:\n"
    "                break\n"
    "            solved += 1\n"
    "            self._gying_solve_challenge(session, node, response)\n"
    "        raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "            f\"观影浏览器验证连续 {solved or 1} 次计算后原请求仍要求验证；\"\n"
    "            \"请稍后重试或检查当前出口/节点\"\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 登录状态：当前站点已确认点击验证码，账号密码模式不再先 POST code=''\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _gying_authenticated_probe(self, session: requests.Session, node: str) -> bool:\n"
    "        node = str(node or \"\").rstrip(\"/\")\n"
    "        if not node:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        url = f\"{node}/search?q={quote('肖申克的救赎')}&type=&mode=1\"\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            response = self._gying_request(\n"
    "                session,\n"
    "                node,\n"
    "                \"GET\",\n"
    "                url,\n"
    "                headers={\"Referer\": node + \"/\", \"Accept\": \"text/html,*/*\"},\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        text = str(response.text or \"\")\n"
    "        if self._gying_login_required(response):\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        if \"未登录，访问受限\" in text or \"_BT.PC.HTML('nologin')\" in text or '_BT.PC.HTML(\"nologin\")' in text:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        return int(response.status_code or 0) < 400\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_login_password(self, session: requests.Session, node: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        username = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_username\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        password = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_password\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        if not (username and password):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": False,\n"
    "                \"mode\": \"credentials_missing\",\n"
    "                \"manual_login_required\": True,\n"
    "                \"message\": \"观影登录态已失效，且未配置用户名/密码\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": False,\n"
    "            \"mode\": \"captcha_required\",\n"
    "            \"manual_login_required\": True,\n"
    "            \"message\": \"观影账号需要汉字点击验证码；请在插件页“观影人工认证”中按提示点击\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_login(self, session: requests.Session, node: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"任何 Cookie 都先通过受限搜索验真；失败后再进入账号/匿名路径。\"\"\"\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            state = self._gying_state()\n"
    "            row = dict(((state.get(\"nodes\") or {}).get(node) or {}))\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            state, row = {}, {}\n"
    "\n"
    "        login_mode = str(row.get(\"login_mode\") or \"\")\n"
    "        has_cookie = bool(len(session.cookies))\n"
    "        if has_cookie and self._gying_authenticated_probe(session, node):\n"
    "            mode = login_mode if login_mode in _AUTH_COOKIE_MODES_V1107 else \"cookie_reuse\"\n"
    "            self._gying_persist_session(\n"
    "                node,\n"
    "                session,\n"
    "                status=\"ok\",\n"
    "                login_mode=mode,\n"
    "                authenticated=True,\n"
    "                verified=bool(\n"
    "                    session.cookies.get(\"browser_verified\")\n"
    "                    or session.cookies.get(\"browser_pow\")\n"
    "                ),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"mode\": \"cookie_reuse\" if mode == \"manual_captcha\" else mode,\n"
    "                \"message\": \"观影 Cookie 已通过受限搜索验真并复用\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "        if has_cookie:\n"
    "            row[\"authenticated\"] = False\n"
    "            row[\"status\"] = \"login_expired\"\n"
    "            if isinstance(state, dict):\n"
    "                state.setdefault(\"nodes\", {})[node] = row\n"
    "                self._save_gying_state(state)\n"
    "\n"
    "        username = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_username\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        password = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_password\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        if username and password:\n"
    "            return self._gying_login_password(session, node)\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": True,\n"
    "            \"mode\": \"anonymous\",\n"
    "            \"message\": \"未配置观影账号；仅尝试公开访问\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 验证码 / 人工登录\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _gying_request_captcha(self, session: requests.Session, node: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        url = str(node or \"\").rstrip(\"/\") + \"/res/captcha/2\"\n"
    "        response = self._gying_request(\n"
    "            session,\n"
    "            node,\n"
    "            \"POST\",\n"
    "            url,\n"
    "            data={\"webp\": \"1\"},\n"
    "            headers={\n"
    "                \"Origin\": str(node or \"\").rstrip(\"/\"),\n"
    "                \"Referer\": str(node or \"\").rstrip(\"/\") + \"/user/login\",\n"
    "                \"Accept\": \"*/*\",\n"
    "            },\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        payload = _safe_json(response)\n"
    "        image_type = str(payload.get(\"type\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "        image = str(payload.get(\"img\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        text = str(payload.get(\"text\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not image_type or not _JSON_IMAGE_TYPE_RE.fullmatch(image_type):\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影验证码返回的图片类型无效\")\n"
    "        if not image or len(image) > 2_000_000:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影验证码图片为空或过大\")\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            base64.b64decode(image, validate=True)\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影验证码图片不是有效 Base64\") from err\n"
    "        if not text or len(text) > 8:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影验证码点击文字为空或异常\")\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"type\": image_type,\n"
    "            \"img\": image,\n"
    "            \"text\": text,\n"
    "            \"width\": _CAPTCHA_WIDTH,\n"
    "            \"height\": _CAPTCHA_HEIGHT,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_verify_captcha(self, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        session = row.get(\"session\")\n"
    "        node = str(row.get(\"node\") or \"\")\n"
    "        captcha = dict(row.get(\"captcha\") or {})\n"
    "        points = list(row.get(\"points\") or [])\n"
    "        width = int(captcha.get(\"width\") or _CAPTCHA_WIDTH)\n"
    "        height = int(captcha.get(\"height\") or _CAPTCHA_HEIGHT)\n"
    "        info = _captcha_info(points, width, height)\n"
    "        if not isinstance(session, requests.Session) or not node or not info:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影人工验证码会话已失效\")\n"
    "        response = self._gying_request(\n"
    "            session,\n"
    "            node,\n"
    "            \"POST\",\n"
    "            node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/res/captcha/2\",\n"
    "            data={\"do\": \"check\", \"info\": info},\n"
    "            headers={\n"
    "                \"Origin\": node.rstrip(\"/\"),\n"
    "                \"Referer\": node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/user/login\",\n"
    "                \"Accept\": \"*/*\",\n"
    "            },\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        payload = _safe_json(response)\n"
    "        return {\"success\": payload.get(\"code\") in (200, \"200\"), \"info\": info}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_finish_manual_login(self, row: Dict[str, Any], info: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        session = row.get(\"session\")\n"
    "        node = str(row.get(\"node\") or \"\")\n"
    "        username = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_username\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        password = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_password\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        if not isinstance(session, requests.Session) or not node:\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"mode\": \"expired\", \"message\": \"观影人工登录会话已失效\"}\n"
    "        if not (username and password):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": False,\n"
    "                \"mode\": \"credentials_missing\",\n"
    "                \"message\": \"未配置观影用户名/密码，无法完成登录\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        login_url = node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/user/login\"\n"
    "        response = self._gying_request(\n"
    "            session,\n"
    "            node,\n"
    "            \"POST\",\n"
    "            login_url,\n"
    "            data={\n"
    "                \"code\": str(info or \"\"),\n"
    "                \"siteid\": \"1\",\n"
    "                \"dosubmit\": \"1\",\n"
    "                \"cookietime\": \"10506240\",\n"
    "                \"username\": username,\n"
    "                \"password\": password,\n"
    "            },\n"
    "            headers={\n"
    "                \"Origin\": node.rstrip(\"/\"),\n"
    "                \"Referer\": login_url,\n"
    "                \"Accept\": \"*/*\",\n"
    "            },\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        payload = _safe_json(response)\n"
    "        if payload.get(\"code\") not in (200, \"200\"):\n"
    "            message = str(payload.get(\"msg\") or payload.get(\"message\") or \"站点未返回登录成功\")[:260]\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"mode\": \"login_failed\", \"message\": f\"观影登录失败：{message}\"}\n"
    "        if not self._gying_authenticated_probe(session, node):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": False,\n"
    "                \"mode\": \"login_unverified\",\n"
    "                \"message\": \"观影登录接口返回成功，但受限搜索仍显示未登录\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        self._gying_persist_session(\n"
    "            node,\n"
    "            session,\n"
    "            status=\"ok\",\n"
    "            login_mode=\"manual_captcha\",\n"
    "            authenticated=True,\n"
    "            verified=bool(\n"
    "                session.cookies.get(\"browser_verified\")\n"
    "                or session.cookies.get(\"browser_pow\")\n"
    "            ),\n"
    "            login_at=self._now_text(),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._gying_auth_log(\"INFO\", \"人工登录完成：节点=%s，会话已验证并保存\", node)\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": True,\n"
    "            \"mode\": \"manual_captcha\",\n"
    "            \"node\": node,\n"
    "            \"message\": \"观影登录成功，会话已保存；站点公告/最新网址弹窗不阻塞资源检索\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_auth_start(self, force: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        if not bool(getattr(self, \"_viewing_enabled\", False)):\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"stage\": \"disabled\", \"message\": \"观影未启用\"}\n"
    "        username = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_username\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        password = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_password\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        if not (username and password):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": False,\n"
    "                \"stage\": \"credentials_missing\",\n"
    "                \"message\": \"请先在插件配置中填写观影用户名和密码\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        with getattr(self, \"_gying_auth_lock\", threading.RLock()):\n"
    "            self._gying_auth_cleanup()\n"
    "            old_id = str(getattr(self, \"_gying_auth_active_id\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "            old = (getattr(self, \"_gying_auth_sessions\", {}) or {}).pop(old_id, None) if old_id else None\n"
    "            if old:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    old_session = old.get(\"session\")\n"
    "                    if old_session:\n"
    "                        old_session.close()\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "            self._gying_auth_active_id = \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "        state = self._gying_state()\n"
    "        node_order = []\n"
    "        chooser = getattr(self, \"_gying_node_order\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(chooser):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                node_order = list(chooser() or [])\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                node_order = []\n"
    "        if not node_order:\n"
    "            node_order = list(self._discover_gying_nodes(force=False) or [])\n"
    "        errors: List[str] = []\n"
    "        for raw_node in node_order[:12]:\n"
    "            node = canonical_gying_node(raw_node) or _normalize_node_url(raw_node)\n"
    "            if not node:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            saved = \"\" if force else str(((state.get(\"nodes\") or {}).get(node) or {}).get(\"cookie\") or \"\")\n"
    "            session = self._gying_new_session(node, saved_cookie=saved)\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                home = self._gying_request(\n"
    "                    session,\n"
    "                    node,\n"
    "                    \"GET\",\n"
    "                    node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/\",\n"
    "                    headers={\"Accept\": \"text/html,*/*\"},\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if not _looks_like_content_node(home):\n"
    "                    errors.append(f\"{node}: 非内容节点\")\n"
    "                    session.close()\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                if not force and self._gying_authenticated_probe(session, node):\n"
    "                    self._gying_persist_session(\n"
    "                        node,\n"
    "                        session,\n"
    "                        status=\"ok\",\n"
    "                        login_mode=\"manual_captcha\",\n"
    "                        authenticated=True,\n"
    "                        verified=bool(\n"
    "                            session.cookies.get(\"browser_verified\")\n"
    "                            or session.cookies.get(\"browser_pow\")\n"
    "                        ),\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                    try:\n"
    "                        session.close()\n"
    "                    except Exception:\n"
    "                        pass\n"
    "                    return {\n"
    "                        \"success\": True,\n"
    "                        \"stage\": \"authenticated\",\n"
    "                        \"node\": node,\n"
    "                        \"message\": \"现有观影会话仍有效，无需再次验证码\",\n"
    "                    }\n"
    "\n"
    "                self._gying_request(\n"
    "                    session,\n"
    "                    node,\n"
    "                    \"GET\",\n"
    "                    node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/user/login\",\n"
    "                    headers={\"Referer\": node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/\"},\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                captcha = self._gying_request_captcha(session, node)\n"
    "                auth_id = secrets.token_urlsafe(18)\n"
    "                row = {\n"
    "                    \"auth_id\": auth_id,\n"
    "                    \"session\": session,\n"
    "                    \"node\": node,\n"
    "                    \"stage\": \"captcha\",\n"
    "                    \"captcha\": captcha,\n"
    "                    \"points\": [],\n"
    "                    \"created_ts\": time.time(),\n"
    "                    \"updated_ts\": time.time(),\n"
    "                    \"message\": \"请按提示顺序点击汉字；点满后自动校验并登录\",\n"
    "                }\n"
    "                with getattr(self, \"_gying_auth_lock\", threading.RLock()):\n"
    "                    self._gying_auth_sessions[auth_id] = row\n"
    "                    self._gying_auth_active_id = auth_id\n"
    "                self._gying_auth_log(\n"
    "                    \"INFO\",\n"
    "                    \"人工登录：验证码已生成，节点=%s，需要点击=%s个\",\n"
    "                    node,\n"
    "                    len(str(captcha.get(\"text\") or \"\")),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                return {\"success\": True, **self._gying_auth_public(row)}\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                errors.append(f\"{node}: {str(err)[:120]}\")\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    session.close()\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": False,\n"
    "            \"stage\": \"unavailable\",\n"
    "            \"message\": (\"；\".join(errors[:5]) or \"没有可用于人工登录的观影内容节点\")[:500],\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # API\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def api_viewing_auth_start(self, force: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        return self._gying_auth_start(bool(force))\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_viewing_auth_status(self, auth_id: str = \"\") -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        target = str(auth_id or getattr(self, \"_gying_auth_active_id\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        row = self._gying_auth_get(target) if target else None\n"
    "        if row:\n"
    "            return {\"success\": True, **self._gying_auth_public(row)}\n"
    "        state = self._gying_state()\n"
    "        active = str(state.get(\"active_node\") or \"\")\n"
    "        saved = dict(((state.get(\"nodes\") or {}).get(active) or {})) if active else {}\n"
    "        authenticated = bool(saved.get(\"authenticated\")) and str(saved.get(\"login_mode\") or \"\") in _AUTH_COOKIE_MODES_V1107\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": True,\n"
    "            \"active\": False,\n"
    "            \"stage\": \"authenticated\" if authenticated else \"idle\",\n"
    "            \"node\": active,\n"
    "            \"message\": \"观影会话已认证\" if authenticated else \"等待人工认证\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_viewing_auth_click(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        auth_id: str = \"\",\n"
    "        x: int = -1,\n"
    "        y: int = -1,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        row = self._gying_auth_get(auth_id)\n"
    "        if not row or str(row.get(\"stage\") or \"\") != \"captcha\":\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"stage\": \"expired\", \"message\": \"验证码会话已过期，请重新开始认证\"}\n"
    "        captcha = dict(row.get(\"captcha\") or {})\n"
    "        width = int(captcha.get(\"width\") or _CAPTCHA_WIDTH)\n"
    "        height = int(captcha.get(\"height\") or _CAPTCHA_HEIGHT)\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            px, py = int(x), int(y)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"stage\": \"captcha\", \"message\": \"点击坐标无效\"}\n"
    "        if px < 0 or py < 0 or px >= width or py >= height:\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"stage\": \"captcha\", \"message\": \"点击位置超出验证码范围\"}\n"
    "        required = len(str(captcha.get(\"text\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        points = list(row.get(\"points\") or [])\n"
    "        if len(points) >= required:\n"
    "            points = []\n"
    "        points.append((px, py))\n"
    "        row[\"points\"] = points\n"
    "        row[\"updated_ts\"] = time.time()\n"
    "        if len(points) < required:\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"stage\": \"captcha\",\n"
    "                \"message\": f\"已记录第 {len(points)}/{required} 个点击\",\n"
    "                \"clicked\": len(points),\n"
    "                \"required\": required,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "        self._gying_auth_log(\"INFO\", \"人工登录：收到完整点击序列，开始校验\")\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            checked = self._gying_verify_captcha(row)\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            row[\"points\"] = []\n"
    "            row[\"message\"] = f\"验证码校验请求失败：{str(err)[:180]}\"\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, **self._gying_auth_public(row)}\n"
    "        if not checked.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                row[\"captcha\"] = self._gying_request_captcha(row[\"session\"], str(row.get(\"node\") or \"\"))\n"
    "                row[\"points\"] = []\n"
    "                row[\"message\"] = \"未点中正确区域，已刷新验证码，请重新点击\"\n"
    "                row[\"updated_ts\"] = time.time()\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                row[\"points\"] = []\n"
    "                row[\"message\"] = f\"验证码错误，刷新失败：{str(err)[:180]}\"\n"
    "            self._gying_auth_log(\"WARNING\", \"人工登录：验证码点击错误，已请求刷新\")\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, **self._gying_auth_public(row)}\n"
    "\n"
    "        result = self._gying_finish_manual_login(row, str(checked.get(\"info\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        if result.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "            row[\"stage\"] = \"authenticated\"\n"
    "            row[\"points\"] = []\n"
    "            row[\"captcha\"] = {}\n"
    "            row[\"message\"] = str(result.get(\"message\") or \"\")\n"
    "            row[\"updated_ts\"] = time.time()\n"
    "            return {\"success\": True, **self._gying_auth_public(row), **result}\n"
    "        row[\"stage\"] = \"login_failed\"\n"
    "        row[\"points\"] = []\n"
    "        row[\"message\"] = str(result.get(\"message\") or \"观影登录失败\")\n"
    "        row[\"updated_ts\"] = time.time()\n"
    "        return {\"success\": False, **self._gying_auth_public(row), **result}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_viewing_auth_undo(self, auth_id: str = \"\") -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        row = self._gying_auth_get(auth_id)\n"
    "        if not row or str(row.get(\"stage\") or \"\") != \"captcha\":\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"message\": \"没有可撤销的验证码会话\"}\n"
    "        points = list(row.get(\"points\") or [])\n"
    "        if points:\n"
    "            points.pop()\n"
    "        row[\"points\"] = points\n"
    "        row[\"updated_ts\"] = time.time()\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": True,\n"
    "            \"stage\": \"captcha\",\n"
    "            \"message\": f\"已撤销，当前 {len(points)}/{len(str((row.get('captcha') or {}).get('text') or ''))}\",\n"
    "            \"clicked\": len(points),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_viewing_auth_refresh(self, auth_id: str = \"\") -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        row = self._gying_auth_get(auth_id)\n"
    "        if not row or str(row.get(\"stage\") or \"\") != \"captcha\":\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"message\": \"验证码会话已过期，请重新开始认证\"}\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            row[\"captcha\"] = self._gying_request_captcha(row[\"session\"], str(row.get(\"node\") or \"\"))\n"
    "            row[\"points\"] = []\n"
    "            row[\"updated_ts\"] = time.time()\n"
    "            row[\"message\"] = \"验证码已刷新\"\n"
    "            return {\"success\": True, **self._gying_auth_public(row)}\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"message\": f\"刷新验证码失败：{str(err)[:240]}\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_viewing_auth_cancel(self, auth_id: str = \"\") -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        target = str(auth_id or getattr(self, \"_gying_auth_active_id\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        row = None\n"
    "        with getattr(self, \"_gying_auth_lock\", threading.RLock()):\n"
    "            row = (getattr(self, \"_gying_auth_sessions\", {}) or {}).pop(target, None)\n"
    "            if str(getattr(self, \"_gying_auth_active_id\", \"\") or \"\") == target:\n"
    "                self._gying_auth_active_id = \"\"\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            if row and row.get(\"session\"):\n"
    "                row[\"session\"].close()\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        return {\"success\": True, \"stage\": \"idle\", \"message\": \"已取消观影人工认证\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_api(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        apis = list(super().get_api() or [])\n"
    "        paths = {str(item.get(\"path\") or \"\") for item in apis if isinstance(item, dict)}\n"
    "        extras = [\n"
    "            {\"path\": \"/viewing/auth/start\", \"endpoint\": self.api_viewing_auth_start, \"methods\": [\"POST\"], \"summary\": \"开始观影人工汉字验证码登录\"},\n"
    "            {\"path\": \"/viewing/auth/status\", \"endpoint\": self.api_viewing_auth_status, \"methods\": [\"GET\"], \"summary\": \"查看观影人工认证状态\"},\n"
    "            {\"path\": \"/viewing/auth/click\", \"endpoint\": self.api_viewing_auth_click, \"methods\": [\"POST\"], \"summary\": \"提交一次人工验证码点击\"},\n"
    "            {\"path\": \"/viewing/auth/undo\", \"endpoint\": self.api_viewing_auth_undo, \"methods\": [\"POST\"], \"summary\": \"撤销上一次人工验证码点击\"},\n"
    "            {\"path\": \"/viewing/auth/refresh\", \"endpoint\": self.api_viewing_auth_refresh, \"methods\": [\"POST\"], \"summary\": \"刷新观影汉字验证码\"},\n"
    "            {\"path\": \"/viewing/auth/cancel\", \"endpoint\": self.api_viewing_auth_cancel, \"methods\": [\"POST\"], \"summary\": \"取消观影人工认证\"},\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        apis.extend(item for item in extras if item[\"path\"] not in paths)\n"
    "        return apis\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # MoviePilot 控制台：用 15px 透明网格覆盖 315x180 验证码。\n"
    "    # 用户点击的是图片位置；每个格子的中心坐标作为固定 API 参数回传。\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _gying_auth_api_button(\n"
    "        text: str,\n"
    "        path: str,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n"
    "        icon: str = \"\",\n"
    "        color: str = \"primary\",\n"
    "        variant: str = \"tonal\",\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        props: Dict[str, Any] = {\n"
    "            \"size\": \"small\",\n"
    "            \"variant\": variant,\n"
    "            \"color\": color,\n"
    "            \"class\": \"mr-2 mb-2\",\n"
    "            \"style\": \"border-radius:11px;min-height:36px;font-weight:650;\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        if icon:\n"
    "            props[\"prepend-icon\"] = icon\n"
    "        event: Dict[str, Any] = {\n"
    "            \"api\": f\"plugin/GuangYaTransferAssistant{path}\",\n"
    "            \"method\": \"post\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        if params:\n"
    "            event[\"params\"] = dict(params)\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VBtn\",\n"
    "            \"props\": props,\n"
    "            \"text\": text,\n"
    "            \"events\": {\"click\": event},\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_auth_grid(self, auth_id: str, image: str, width: int, height: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        cells: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [\n"
    "            {\n"
    "                \"component\": \"VImg\",\n"
    "                \"props\": {\n"
    "                    \"src\": image,\n"
    "                    \"width\": width,\n"
    "                    \"height\": height,\n"
    "                    \"cover\": True,\n"
    "                    \"style\": f\"position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:{width}px;height:{height}px;\",\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        for top in range(0, height, _CAPTCHA_GRID):\n"
    "            for left in range(0, width, _CAPTCHA_GRID):\n"
    "                cell_w = min(_CAPTCHA_GRID, width - left)\n"
    "                cell_h = min(_CAPTCHA_GRID, height - top)\n"
    "                x = min(width - 1, left + cell_w // 2)\n"
    "                y = min(height - 1, top + cell_h // 2)\n"
    "                cells.append({\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VBtn\",\n"
    "                    \"props\": {\n"
    "                        \"variant\": \"text\",\n"
    "                        \"ripple\": False,\n"
    "                        \"aria-label\": f\"验证码位置 {x},{y}\",\n"
    "                        \"style\": (\n"
    "                            f\"position:absolute;left:{left}px;top:{top}px;\"\n"
    "                            f\"width:{cell_w}px;height:{cell_h}px;min-width:0;padding:0;\"\n"
    "                            \"border-radius:0;background:transparent;box-shadow:none;\"\n"
    "                            \"border:1px solid rgba(var(--v-border-color),.025);\"\n"
    "                        ),\n"
    "                    },\n"
    "                    \"events\": {\n"
    "                        \"click\": {\n"
    "                            \"api\": \"plugin/GuangYaTransferAssistant/viewing/auth/click\",\n"
    "                            \"method\": \"post\",\n"
    "                            \"params\": {\"auth_id\": auth_id, \"x\": x, \"y\": y},\n"
    "                        }\n"
    "                    },\n"
    "                })\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VSheet\",\n"
    "            \"props\": {\n"
    "                \"style\": (\n"
    "                    f\"position:relative;width:{width}px;height:{height}px;max-width:100%;\"\n"
    "                    \"overflow:hidden;border-radius:14px;border:1px solid \"\n"
    "                    \"rgba(var(--v-border-color),.14);margin:0 auto;\"\n"
    "                ),\n"
    "            },\n"
    "            \"content\": cells,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_auth_panel(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        status = self.api_viewing_auth_status()\n"

    "                        \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                        \"density\": \"compact\",\n"
    "                        \"class\": \"mb-3\",\n"
    "                        \"style\": \"border-radius:12px;\",\n"
    "                        \"text\": \"请依次点击：\" + \"、\".join(f\"“{char}”\" for char in text),\n"
    "                    },\n"
    "                },\n"
    "                self._gying_auth_grid(auth_id, str(captcha.get(\"image\") or \"\"), width, height),\n"
    "                {\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"div\",\n"
    "                    \"props\": {\n"
    "                        \"class\": \"mt-3 mb-2\",\n"
    "                        \"style\": \"font-size:12px;line-height:1.6;opacity:.72;\",\n"
    "                    },\n"
    "                    \"text\": (\n"
    "                        f\"已记录 {int(status.get('clicked') or 0)}/{int(status.get('required') or 0)}。\"\n"
    "                        \"每次点击会回传对应位置；点满后自动校验并登录。点击错误时会刷新验证码。\"\n"
    "                    ),\n"
    "                },\n"
    "                {\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"div\",\n"
    "                    \"props\": {\"class\": \"d-flex flex-wrap\"},\n"

    "                        ),\n"
    "                        self._gying_auth_api_button(\n"
    "                            \"取消认证\",\n"
    "                            \"/viewing/auth/cancel\",\n"
    "                            params={\"auth_id\": auth_id},\n"
    "                            icon=\"mdi-close\",\n"
    "                            color=\"secondary\",\n"
    "                            variant=\"text\",\n"
    "                        ),\n"
    "                    ],\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            ])\n"
    "        elif stage == \"authenticated\":\n"
    "            content.extend([\n"
    "                {\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "                    \"props\": {\n"
    "                        \"type\": \"success\",\n"

    "                            variant=\"outlined\",\n"
    "                        ),\n"
    "                    ],\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            ])\n"

    "            \"component\": \"VCard\",\n"

    "                \"class\": \"mb-4\",\n"
    "                \"style\": (\n"
    "                    \"border:1px solid rgba(var(--v-theme-primary),.16);border-radius:18px;\"\n"
    "                    \"box-shadow:0 10px 30px rgba(15,23,42,.06);overflow:hidden;\"\n"

    "                            },\n"

    "                                    {\n"

    "                                        \"text\": \"PoW 自动恢复；汉字点击由你本人完成；成功后复用同一 Session 并持久化登录态。\",\n"
    "                                    },\n"
    "                                ],\n"
    "                            },\n"
    "                        ],\n"
    "                    },\n"
    "                    *content,\n"
    "                ],\n"
    "            }],\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_page(self):\n"
    "        pages = list(super().get_page() or [])\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            panel = self._gying_auth_panel()\n"
    "            insert_at = 3 if len(pages) >= 3 else len(pages)\n"
    "            pages.insert(insert_at, panel)\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._gying_auth_log(\"WARNING\", \"人工认证面板构建失败：%s\", err)\n"
    "        return pages\n"
    "\n"

    "\n"
