# ===== gying_pansou_v1110.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["gying_pansou_v1110"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.10.10 对齐 PanSou 的 GYING 挑战与重试语义。\n"
    "\n"
    "这一层针对 v1.10.9 线上出现的“PoW 未被服务器确认”做收口：\n"
    "\n"
    "- 挑战识别按 PanSou 当前公开实现收紧：必须同时出现验证文案和真实 PoW/worker\n"
    "  特征，或存在内嵌 ``const json={...}; const jss=...``；正常 ``_obj.`` 页面优先放行。\n"
    "- ``refresh=1`` 是观影网页自己的动态 overlay 中间件协议，不能直接等价成远程\n"
    "  ``/res/pow``。遇到它时先回到同节点根页建立/恢复 ``browser_pow`` 挑战，再按\n"
    "  PanSou 的挑战页流程计算，之后重试原请求。\n"
    "- 远程 PoW 完全按 PanSou 公开协议：GET ``/res/pow`` 取 N/x/t，计算至少 3 秒，\n"
    "  POST form ``y=<hex>``，响应必须 ``success=true``，随后由“原请求重试成功”作为\n"
    "  最终验证依据。\n"
    "- 不主动删除 ``browser_pow``；Cookie 生命周期交给服务端 Set-Cookie/cookie jar，\n"
    "  与 PanSou 的 cloudscraper 行为一致。\n"
    "\n"
    "不执行远端 overlay JavaScript，不使用 OCR/浏览器自动化，也不记录 Cookie、PoW 参数、\n"
    "验证码或账号密码。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import json\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, Optional\n"
    "\n"
    "import requests\n"
    "\n"
    "from .gying_hardening_v193 import canonical_gying_node\n"
    "from .gying_runtime_v193 import _GYING_CHALLENGE_RE, _safe_int, _solve_pow_hex\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_VERIFY_TEXT_V1110 = (\n"
    "    \"正在确认你是不是机器人\",\n"
    "    \"浏览器安全验证\",\n"
    "    \"安全验证\",\n"
    "    \"正在进行浏览器计算验证\",\n"
    ")\n"
    "_REMOTE_SIG_V1110 = (\n"
    "    \"powSolve-\",\n"
    "    \"pow.worker-\",\n"
    "    \"const jss=\",\n"
    "    \"/res/pow\",\n"
    ")\n"
    "_BLOCK_MARKERS_V1110 = (\"angie\", \"request forbidden\", \"access denied\")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _json_v1110(response: requests.Response) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        data = response.json()\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            data = json.loads(str(getattr(response, \"text\", \"\") or \"{}\"))\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            data = {}\n"
    "    return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _challenge_kind_v1110(response: requests.Response) -> str:\n"
    "    \"\"\"返回 embedded_pow / legacy_hash / remote_pow / refresh_overlay / 空字符串。\"\"\"\n"
    "    text = str(getattr(response, \"text\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "    # 数据页里可能引用验证静态资源；PanSou 的核心经验是不能因此误判。\n"
    "    if \"_obj.\" in text:\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    match = _GYING_CHALLENGE_RE.search(text)\n"
    "    if match:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            data = json.loads(match.group(1))\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            data = {}\n"
    "        if isinstance(data, dict):\n"
    "            if data.get(\"id\") and data.get(\"N\") and data.get(\"x\") and _safe_int(data.get(\"t\"), 0) > 0:\n"
    "                return \"embedded_pow\"\n"
    "            if data.get(\"id\") and data.get(\"challenge\") and data.get(\"salt\") is not None and _safe_int(data.get(\"diff\"), 0) > 0:\n"
    "                return \"legacy_hash\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    has_verify_text = any(token in text for token in _VERIFY_TEXT_V1110)\n"
    "    if has_verify_text and any(token in text for token in _REMOTE_SIG_V1110):\n"
    "        return \"remote_pow\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    payload = _json_v1110(response)\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        refresh = int(payload.get(\"refresh\") or 0) == 1\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "        refresh = False\n"
    "    if refresh:\n"
    "        # 真实网页由 refreshMiddleware -> data.overlay -> PowOverlay.run(data) 处理。\n"
    "        # 它不是“看到 refresh 就 POST /res/pow”的同义词。\n"
    "        return \"refresh_overlay\"\n"
    "    return \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _truthy_success_v1110(value: Any) -> bool:\n"
    "    return value in (True, 1, \"1\", \"true\", \"True\")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaGyingPanSouV1110Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"最外层观影请求覆盖：复刻 PanSou 已验证的 challenge -> solve -> retry 模型。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260902-r21\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_solve_challenge_v1110(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        session: requests.Session,\n"
    "        node: str,\n"
    "        response: requests.Response,\n"
    "        kind: Optional[str] = None,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        if not bool(getattr(self, \"_viewing_auto_challenge\", True)):\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影返回浏览器安全验证；自动计算验证已关闭\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        kind = str(kind or _challenge_kind_v1110(response) or \"\")\n"
    "        node = canonical_gying_node(node) or str(node or \"\").rstrip(\"/\")\n"
    "        if not node:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影 PoW 节点无效\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        if kind in {\"embedded_pow\", \"legacy_hash\"}:\n"
    "            # 旧运行时已经正确实现 action=verify/id/y 与 nonce[]，继续复用。\n"
    "            result = dict(super()._gying_solve_challenge(session, node, response) or {})\n"
    "            sync_cookie = getattr(self, \"_gying_sync_cookie_v1108\", None)\n"
    "            if callable(sync_cookie):\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    sync_cookie(session, node)\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        if kind != \"remote_pow\":\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影当前响应不是可直接提交 /res/pow 的挑战页\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        timeout = min(max(int(getattr(self, \"_provider_timeout\", 15) or 15), 5), 20)\n"
    "        pow_url = node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/res/pow\"\n"
    "        started = time.monotonic()\n"
    "        has_pow_cookie = any(str(getattr(item, \"name\", \"\") or \"\") == \"browser_pow\" for item in session.cookies)\n"
    "        self._gying_auth_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"PanSou PoW：节点=%s，挑战页已确认，browser_pow=%s，开始读取参数\",\n"
    "            node,\n"
    "            bool(has_pow_cookie),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "        challenge = session.get(\n"
    "            pow_url,\n"
    "            headers={\"Referer\": str(getattr(response, \"url\", \"\") or node + \"/\")},\n"
    "            timeout=timeout,\n"
    "            allow_redirects=True,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        sync_cookie = getattr(self, \"_gying_sync_cookie_v1108\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(sync_cookie):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                sync_cookie(session, node)\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "        if int(getattr(challenge, \"status_code\", 0) or 0) != 200:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(f\"观影获取 PoW 参数失败：HTTP {int(getattr(challenge, 'status_code', 0) or 0)}\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        data = _json_v1110(challenge)\n"
    "        if not data.get(\"N\") or not data.get(\"x\") or _safe_int(data.get(\"t\"), 0) <= 0:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影 PoW 参数无效\")\n"
    "        y = _solve_pow_hex(str(data.get(\"N\")), str(data.get(\"x\")), _safe_int(data.get(\"t\"), 0))\n"
    "        elapsed = time.monotonic() - started\n"
    "        if elapsed < 3.0:\n"
    "            time.sleep(3.0 - elapsed)\n"
    "\n"
    "        # PanSou submitChallengeVerification 只提交 form，不主动清理 browser_pow。\n"
    "        verified = session.post(\n"
    "            pow_url,\n"
    "            data={\"y\": y},\n"
    "            headers={\"Referer\": str(getattr(response, \"url\", \"\") or node + \"/\")},\n"
    "            timeout=timeout + 15,\n"
    "            allow_redirects=True,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if callable(sync_cookie):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                sync_cookie(session, node)\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "        result = _json_v1110(verified)\n"
    "        if int(getattr(verified, \"status_code\", 0) or 0) >= 400 or not _truthy_success_v1110(result.get(\"success\")):\n"
    "            message = str(result.get(\"msg\") or result.get(\"message\") or \"\").strip()[:120]\n"
    "            suffix = f\"：{message}\" if message else \"\"\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "                f\"观影 PoW 未被服务器确认：HTTP {int(getattr(verified, 'status_code', 0) or 0)}{suffix}\"\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "        has_verified_cookie = any(\n"
    "            str(getattr(item, \"name\", \"\") or \"\") == \"browser_verified\" for item in session.cookies\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._gying_auth_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"PanSou PoW：服务器已确认提交，节点=%s browser_verified=%s，准备重试原请求\",\n"
    "            node,\n"
    "            bool(has_verified_cookie),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"mode\": \"remote_pow_pansou\",\n"
    "            \"success\": True,\n"
    "            \"challenge_id_present\": bool(str(result.get(\"challenge_id\") or \"\")),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_refresh_bootstrap_v1110(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        session: requests.Session,\n"
    "        node: str,\n"
    "        response: requests.Response,\n"
    "        timeout: int,\n"
    "    ) -> bool:\n"
    "        \"\"\"把网页 refresh=1 转回可验证的根页 challenge，而不是误投 /res/pow。\"\"\"\n"
    "        node = canonical_gying_node(node) or str(node or \"\").rstrip(\"/\")\n"
    "        payload = _json_v1110(response)\n"
    "        self._gying_auth_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"PanSou PoW：收到网页 refresh=1，先回根页建立挑战；overlay=%s\",\n"
    "            bool(str(payload.get(\"overlay\") or \"\")),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        bootstrap = session.get(\n"
    "            node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/\",\n"
    "            timeout=timeout,\n"
    "            allow_redirects=True,\n"
    "            headers={\"Accept\": \"text/html,*/*\"},\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        sync_cookie = getattr(self, \"_gying_sync_cookie_v1108\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(sync_cookie):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                sync_cookie(session, node)\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "        kind = _challenge_kind_v1110(bootstrap)\n"
    "        if kind in {\"embedded_pow\", \"legacy_hash\", \"remote_pow\"}:\n"
    "            self._gying_solve_challenge_v1110(session, node, bootstrap, kind=kind)\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "        if any(str(getattr(item, \"name\", \"\") or \"\") == \"browser_verified\" for item in session.cookies):\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "        # 根页没有 challenge 时仍允许原请求再试一次；若仍 refresh，外层会明确失败。\n"
    "        return int(getattr(bootstrap, \"status_code\", 0) or 0) < 400\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_request(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        session: requests.Session,\n"
    "        node: str,\n"
    "        method: str,\n"
    "        url: str,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        retry_challenge: bool = True,\n"
    "        **kwargs: Any,\n"
    "    ) -> requests.Response:\n"
    "        timeout = int(kwargs.pop(\"timeout\", int(getattr(self, \"_provider_timeout\", 15) or 15)) or 15)\n"
    "        attempts = 2 if retry_challenge else 1\n"
    "        solved_kind = \"\"\n"
    "        for attempt in range(attempts):\n"
    "            response = session.request(\n"
    "                str(method or \"GET\").upper(),\n"
    "                url,\n"
    "                timeout=timeout,\n"
    "                allow_redirects=True,\n"
    "                **kwargs,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            sync_cookie = getattr(self, \"_gying_sync_cookie_v1108\", None)\n"
    "            if callable(sync_cookie):\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    sync_cookie(session, node)\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "\n"
    "            text = str(getattr(response, \"text\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "            lowered = text.lower()\n"
    "            if int(getattr(response, \"status_code\", 0) or 0) in {403, 404} and any(\n"
    "                marker in lowered for marker in _BLOCK_MARKERS_V1110\n"
    "            ):\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(f\"观影节点当前出口被阻断：HTTP {response.status_code}\")\n"
    "\n"
    "            kind = _challenge_kind_v1110(response)\n"
    "            if not kind:\n"
    "                if solved_kind:\n"
    "                    self._gying_auth_log(\n"
    "                        \"INFO\",\n"
    "                        \"PanSou PoW：原请求重试成功，节点=%s 类型=%s\",\n"
    "                        canonical_gying_node(node) or str(node or \"\"),\n"
    "                        solved_kind,\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                return response\n"
    "\n"
    "            if not retry_challenge or attempt + 1 >= attempts:\n"
    "                if kind == \"refresh_overlay\":\n"
    "                    raise RuntimeError(\"观影动态 refresh 验证重试后仍未恢复\")\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\"观影机器人验证完成后原请求仍返回挑战页\")\n"
    "\n"
    "            if kind == \"refresh_overlay\":\n"
    "                solved_kind = \"refresh_overlay\"\n"
    "                if not self._gying_refresh_bootstrap_v1110(session, node, response, timeout):\n"
    "                    raise RuntimeError(\"观影动态 refresh 验证无法建立 browser_pow 挑战\")\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "\n"
    "            solved_kind = kind\n"
    "            self._gying_solve_challenge_v1110(session, node, response, kind=kind)\n"
    "\n"
    "        raise RuntimeError(\"观影请求重试次数已耗尽\")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaGyingPanSouV1110Mixin\", \"_challenge_kind_v1110\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

    "\"\"\"v1.12.5 迅雷召回最终可用性门禁。\n"
    "\n"
    "只修正“搜索结果是否足以停止关键词降级”的集数判定，不复制媒体身份规则：\n"
    "- 标题/别名/年份/季号继续统一调用 v1.11.1 MediaIdentityGuard 的运行时权威；\n"
    "- TV 候选若显式标出了集号且与 MoviePilot 当前真实缺集完全不相交，视为不可用；\n"
    "- 未标集号的整季包/合集仍保留为潜在可用候选，避免为了扩大关键词无意义增加请求；\n"
    "- 关键词降级后，把本轮真正成功请求过的各级详情结果合并回原关键词 120 秒缓存，\n"
    "  即使没有可用迅雷，后续 Magnet 也能复用已经付出的 downurl 请求；SearchBundle 使用\n"
    "  最老的有效成员请求时间作为 TTL，不把旧严格查询借较新的宽搜结果延寿；\n"
    "- 精准迅雷搜索保持原 GYING 节点故障切换：单节点搜索异常最多换 3 次节点，但账号/验证码\n"
    "  或 viewing_session 已确认全节点不可用时立即停止，不能把认证故障放大成重试风暴；\n"
    "- 某一级迅雷候选通过预筛但实际秒传失败时，才继续下一档关键词；同一 share+passcode\n"
    "  本轮不重复尝试，但同一分享在宽关键词补出了新的提取码时允许重试；\n"
    "- 若某一级搜索本身失败（节点/登录/HTTP 且节点切换仍失败），立即停止继续放宽；\n"
    "- Magnet/ED2K 以“实际执行结果”为准：严格候选存在但因旧集、错媒体或历史失败未产生 action 时，\n"
    "  才逐级补查宽关键词；若严格搜索本身没有成功缓存则不继续放宽；\n"
    "- 真正的媒体身份与文件级缺集校验仍由现有迅雷 JSON / Episode Planner 最终确认。\n"
    "\n"
    "v1.12.19 开发阶段只增加安全排序与来源质量学习：\n"
    "- 固定来源层级永远先于学习分，外部候选仍保持 Magnet > ED2K；\n"
    "- 排序只作用于已召回候选，不改变 MediaIdentity / library missing / reservation / source claim / 物理文件硬栅栏；\n"
    "- 来源质量只统计插件自身来源从非终态进入 completed / failed 的真实终态，不把 retry/waiting 当失败；\n"
    "- 学习分使用带先验的有界分数，只在同一来源层级和安全覆盖特征之后作为 tie-break；\n"
    "- 通过线程本地上下文把 subscribe/uncovered 只读传给搜索排序，不重写既有 dispatch 执行链。\n"
    "\n"
    "本层是标准 cooperative mixin，不继承旧 Hardening；运行时显式放在 Hardening 前面。\n"

    "    try:\n"
    "        return max(0, int(value or 0))\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "        return 0\n"

    "        return max(0.0, float(value or 0))\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "        return 0.0\n"

    "def candidate_quality_score_v11219(stats: Dict[str, Any]) -> int:\n"
    "    \"\"\"Beta(2,2) 平滑后的有界质量分；无样本严格为 0。\"\"\"\n"
    "    success = _nonnegative_int_v11219((stats or {}).get(\"success\"))\n"
    "    failure = _nonnegative_int_v11219((stats or {}).get(\"failure\"))\n"
    "    if success + failure <= 0:\n"
    "        return 0\n"
    "    posterior = (success + 2.0) / (success + failure + 4.0)\n"
    "    return max(-100, min(100, int(round((posterior - 0.5) * 200.0))))\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def candidate_rank_key_v11219(\n"
    "    source_type: str,\n"
    "    eligible: bool,\n"
    "    coverage_penalty: int,\n"

    "    hit_episode_count: int,\n"

    "        max(0, int(candidate_rank or 0)),\n"
    "        max(0, int(original_index or 0)),\n"
    "    )\n"

    "class GuangYaGyingRecallGuardV1125Mixin:\n"

    "        local = getattr(self, \"_gying_recall_retry_local_v1125\", None)\n"
    "        if local is None:\n"
    "            local = threading.local()\n"
    "            self._gying_recall_retry_local_v1125 = local\n"
    "        return local\n"

    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _candidate_episode_hint_v1125(subscribe: Any, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        label = \" \".join(\n"
    "            str(value or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            for value in (row.get(\"name\"), row.get(\"search_title\"))\n"
    "            if str(value or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if not label:\n"
    "            return set()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            parsed = resolve_episode(label, season_hint=getattr(subscribe, \"season\", None))\n"
    "            return set(reliable_episode_set(parsed, AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE))\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return set()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _candidate_can_cover_missing_v1125(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        row: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        missing: Set[int],\n"
    "    ) -> bool:\n"
    "        \"\"\"显式旧集直接排除；无集号候选保留给分享文件级 planner 最终确认。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe) or not missing:\n"

    "        explicit = self._candidate_episode_hint_v1125(subscribe, row)\n"
    "        return not explicit or bool(explicit.intersection(missing))\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _promote_search_bundle_v1125(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        primary: str,\n"
    "        variants: Iterable[str],\n"
    "        subscribe: Any = None,\n"
    "    ) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"只合并仍在 TTL 内的真实成功缓存；任何成员都不能因 bundle 合并被延长寿命。\"\"\"\n"
    "        cache = getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache\", None)\n"
    "        primary = \" \".join(str(primary or \"\").split())\n"
    "        if not isinstance(cache, dict) or not primary:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        cache_key_getter = getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache_key_v11223\", None)\n"
    "\n"
    "        def scoped_key(value: str) -> str:\n"
    "            return cache_key_getter(value, subscribe) if callable(cache_key_getter) else value\n"
    "\n"
    "        valid_variants: List[str] = []\n"
    "        merged: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        seen = set()\n"
    "        final_state: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n"
    "        oldest_ts = 0.0\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "        ttl = 120.0\n"
    "        for raw_variant in variants or []:\n"
    "            variant = \" \".join(str(raw_variant or \"\").split())\n"
    "            if not variant or variant in valid_variants:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            entry = dict(cache.get(scoped_key(variant)) or {})\n"
    "            if not entry:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            state = entry.get(\"state\")\n"
    "            if isinstance(state, dict) and state.get(\"success\") is False:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                entry_ts = float(entry.get(\"ts\") or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                entry_ts = 0.0\n"
    "            # Protocol 的普通读取也只接受 120 秒缓存；bundle 不能把已经过期的成员重新复活。\n"
    "            if entry_ts <= 0 or now - entry_ts >= ttl:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            valid_variants.append(variant)\n"
    "            oldest_ts = entry_ts if oldest_ts <= 0 else min(oldest_ts, entry_ts)\n"
    "            if isinstance(state, dict):\n"
    "                final_state = dict(state)\n"
    "            for raw_row in entry.get(\"rows\") or []:\n"
    "                if not isinstance(raw_row, dict):\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                row = dict(raw_row)\n"
    "                url = str(row.get(\"url\") or row.get(\"uri\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                passcode = str(row.get(\"passcode\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                key = (url, passcode)\n"
    "                if not url or key in seen:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                seen.add(key)\n"
    "                merged.append(row)\n"
    "\n"
    "        if not valid_variants or oldest_ts <= 0:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        effective = valid_variants[-1]\n"
    "        final_state.update({\n"
    "            \"success\": True,\n"
    "            \"query_fallback\": effective if effective != primary else final_state.get(\"query_fallback\"),\n"
    "            \"search_bundle_v1125\": True,\n"
    "            \"bundle_variants\": valid_variants,\n"
    "            \"bundle_resources\": len(merged),\n"
    "        })\n"
    "        cache[scoped_key(primary)] = {\n"
    "            # 取最老有效成员的真实请求时间：所有合并行都会在各自原 TTL 之前一起失效。\n"
    "            \"ts\": oldest_ts,\n"
    "            \"rows\": merged[:800],\n"
    "            \"state\": final_state,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_xunlei_precise_variant_v1125(self, keyword: str):\n"
    "        \"\"\"给精准搜索补回 Failover 语义；认证失败和全节点不可用不做无意义重试。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if not bool(getattr(self, \"_viewing_auto_switch\", True)):\n"
    "            return super()._gying_xunlei_precise_variant_v1125(keyword)\n"
    "\n"
    "        attempted_nodes = set()\n"
    "        last_candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        last_state: Dict[str, Any] = {\"success\": False, \"message\": \"观影迅雷搜索失败\"}\n"
    "        for _attempt in range(3):\n"
    "            candidates, state = super()._gying_xunlei_precise_variant_v1125(keyword)\n"
    "            last_candidates, last_state = list(candidates or []), dict(state or {})\n"
    "            if last_state.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "                return last_candidates, last_state\n"
    "\n"
    "            mode = str(last_state.get(\"mode\") or \"\").lower()\n"
    "            message = str(last_state.get(\"message\") or \"\")\n"
    "            bad_credentials = getattr(self, \"_gying_bad_credentials\", None)\n"
    "            if mode in {\"disabled\", \"unavailable\"} or (\n"
    "                callable(bad_credentials) and bool(bad_credentials(message))\n"
    "            ):\n"
    "                return last_candidates, last_state\n"
    "\n"
    "            failed_node = str(last_state.get(\"node\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if not failed_node or failed_node in attempted_nodes:\n"
    "                return last_candidates, last_state\n"
    "            attempted_nodes.add(failed_node)\n"
    "\n"
    "            # Hardening 已把当前节点标记为 search_error；这里清掉 active 粘性并清理本关键词缓存，\n"
    "            # 下一轮 _viewing_session 会按 Failover 健康顺序选择其它节点。\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                store = dict(self._gying_state() or {})\n"
    "                if str(store.get(\"active_node\") or \"\") == failed_node:\n"
    "                    store[\"active_node\"] = \"\"\n"
    "                    self._save_gying_state(store)\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "            cache = getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache\", None)\n"
    "            if isinstance(cache, dict):\n"
    "                clean_keyword = \" \".join(str(keyword or \"\").split())\n"
    "                cache_key_getter = getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache_key_v11223\", None)\n"
    "                cache_key = cache_key_getter(clean_keyword) if callable(cache_key_getter) else clean_keyword\n"
    "                cache.pop(cache_key, None)\n"
    "        return last_candidates, last_state\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _search_viewing_xunlei(self, keyword: str):\n"
    "        \"\"\"只有当前媒体且可能覆盖当前缺集的迅雷候选，才能停止当前一档搜索。\"\"\"\n"
    "        all_variants = gying_keyword_variants(keyword)\n"
    "        if not all_variants:\n"
    "            return super()._search_viewing_xunlei(keyword)\n"
    "\n"
    "        retry_local = self._recall_retry_local_v1125()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            start_index = max(0, int(getattr(retry_local, \"start_index\", 0) or 0))\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            start_index = 0\n"
    "        start_index = min(start_index, len(all_variants))\n"
    "        variants = all_variants[start_index:]\n"
    "        if not variants:\n"
    "            return [], {\n"
    "                \"provider\": \"viewing_xunlei\",\n"
    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"message\": \"观影迅雷关键词层级已全部尝试\",\n"
    "                \"searched_variants\": all_variants,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "        context = getattr(self, \"_gying_xunlei_context_v1125\", None)\n"
    "        subscribe = getattr(context, \"subscribe\", None) if context is not None else None\n"
    "        seen_identities = set(getattr(retry_local, \"seen_identities\", set()) or set())\n"
    "        last_state: Dict[str, Any] = {\n"
    "            \"provider\": \"viewing_xunlei\",\n"
    "            \"success\": False,\n"
    "            \"message\": \"观影迅雷搜索失败\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        successful: List[str] = []\n"
    "        for relative_index, variant in enumerate(variants):\n"
    "            absolute_index = start_index + relative_index\n"
    "            retry_local.last_attempted_index = absolute_index\n"
    "            candidates, state = self._gying_xunlei_precise_variant_v1125(variant)\n"
    "            last_state = dict(state or {})\n"
    "            if not last_state.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "                retry_local.stop_after_failure = True\n"
    "                bundle_variants = [all_variants[0], *successful] if start_index else successful\n"
    "                if len(set(bundle_variants)) > 1:\n"
    "                    self._promote_search_bundle_v1125(all_variants[0], bundle_variants)\n"
    "                return candidates, last_state\n"
    "            successful.append(variant)\n"
    "\n"
    "            matched: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "            for row in list(candidates or []):\n"
    "                identity = str((row or {}).get(\"share_id\") or (row or {}).get(\"identity\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                passcode = str((row or {}).get(\"passcode\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                retry_key = (identity, passcode)\n"
    "                if identity and retry_key in seen_identities:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                matched.append(row)\n"
    "\n"
    "            missing: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "            if subscribe is not None:\n"
    "                # 不在这里重写标题/年份/季号规则；运行时 self 会优先解析到\n"
    "                # GuangYaMediaIdentityGuardV1111Mixin._provider_candidate_matches。\n"
    "                matched = [row for row in matched if self._provider_candidate_matches(subscribe, row)]\n"
    "                if not self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "                    try:\n"
    "                        missing = {\n"
    "                            int(value)\n"
    "                            for value in (self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or [])\n"
    "                            if int(value or 0) > 0\n"
    "                        }\n"
    "                    except Exception:\n"
    "                        missing = set()\n"
    "                    matched = [\n"
    "                        row for row in matched\n"
    "                        if self._candidate_can_cover_missing_v1125(subscribe, row, missing)\n"
    "                    ]\n"
    "\n"
    "            if matched:\n"
    "                if subscribe is not None and missing:\n"
    "                    matched.sort(key=lambda row: self._xunlei_candidate_priority_v1125(subscribe, row, missing))\n"
    "                for row in matched:\n"
    "                    identity = str((row or {}).get(\"share_id\") or (row or {}).get(\"identity\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                    passcode = str((row or {}).get(\"passcode\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                    if identity:\n"
    "                        seen_identities.add((identity, passcode))\n"
    "                retry_local.seen_identities = seen_identities\n"
    "                last_state[\"matched_candidates\"] = len(matched)\n"
    "                last_state[\"missing_candidates\"] = len(matched)\n"
    "                if absolute_index > 0:\n"
    "                    bundle_variants = [all_variants[0], *successful] if start_index else successful\n"
    "                    self._promote_search_bundle_v1125(all_variants[0], bundle_variants)\n"
    "                    cache_key_getter = getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache_key_v11223\", None)\n"
    "                    primary_key = cache_key_getter(all_variants[0]) if callable(cache_key_getter) else all_variants[0]\n"
    "                    promoted = dict(getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache\", {}).get(primary_key) or {})\n"
    "                    promoted_state = promoted.get(\"state\") if isinstance(promoted.get(\"state\"), dict) else {}\n"
    "                    if promoted_state:\n"
    "                        last_state.update({\n"
    "                            \"search_bundle_v1125\": True,\n"
    "                            \"bundle_variants\": list(promoted_state.get(\"bundle_variants\") or bundle_variants),\n"
    "                            \"bundle_resources\": int(promoted_state.get(\"bundle_resources\") or 0),\n"
    "                        })\n"
    "                    last_state[\"query_fallback\"] = variant\n"
    "                    last_state[\"message\"] = (\n"
    "                        f\"{last_state.get('message') or '观影迅雷搜索成功'} · \"\n"
    "                        f\"前一档迅雷未完成，已降级到 {variant}\"\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                return matched, last_state\n"
    "\n"
    "        bundle_variants = [all_variants[0], *successful] if start_index else successful\n"
    "        if len(set(bundle_variants)) > 1:\n"
    "            self._promote_search_bundle_v1125(all_variants[0], bundle_variants)\n"
    "            cache_key_getter = getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache_key_v11223\", None)\n"
    "            primary_key = cache_key_getter(all_variants[0]) if callable(cache_key_getter) else all_variants[0]\n"
    "            promoted = dict(getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache\", {}).get(primary_key) or {})\n"
    "            promoted_state = promoted.get(\"state\") if isinstance(promoted.get(\"state\"), dict) else {}\n"
    "            if promoted_state:\n"
    "                last_state.update({\n"
    "                    \"search_bundle_v1125\": True,\n"
    "                    \"bundle_variants\": list(promoted_state.get(\"bundle_variants\") or bundle_variants),\n"
    "                    \"bundle_resources\": int(promoted_state.get(\"bundle_resources\") or 0),\n"
    "                })\n"
    "        last_state[\"searched_variants\"] = list(successful)\n"
    "        last_state[\"message\"] = (\n"
    "            f\"观影可访问，但本轮 {len(successful)} 级关键词均没有当前订阅可用迅雷分享\"\n"
    "            if last_state.get(\"success\") else last_state.get(\"message\")\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return [], last_state\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _merge_xunlei_rounds_v1125(base: Dict[str, Any], extra: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        if not base:\n"
    "            return dict(extra or {})\n"
    "        if not extra:\n"
    "            return dict(base or {})\n"
    "        merged = {**dict(base), **dict(extra)}\n"
    "        for key in (\"shares\", \"attempted_files\", \"successful_files\"):\n"
    "            merged[key] = int((base or {}).get(key) or 0) + int((extra or {}).get(key) or 0)\n"
    "        merged[\"success\"] = bool((base or {}).get(\"success\")) or bool((extra or {}).get(\"success\"))\n"
    "        merged[\"handled\"] = bool((base or {}).get(\"handled\")) or bool((extra or {}).get(\"handled\"))\n"
    "        merged[\"movie\"] = bool((base or {}).get(\"movie\")) or bool((extra or {}).get(\"movie\"))\n"
    "        merged[\"subscription_completed\"] = bool((base or {}).get(\"subscription_completed\")) or bool(\n"
    "            (extra or {}).get(\"subscription_completed\")\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        episodes = {\n"
    "            int(value)\n"
    "            for value in [*((base or {}).get(\"episodes\") or []), *((extra or {}).get(\"episodes\") or [])]\n"
    "            if str(value).isdigit() and int(value) > 0\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        merged[\"episodes\"] = sorted(episodes)\n"
    "        merged[\"errors\"] = [\n"
    "            *list((base or {}).get(\"errors\") or []),\n"
    "            *list((extra or {}).get(\"errors\") or []),\n"
    "        ][:20]\n"
    "        messages = []\n"
    "        for row in (base, extra):\n"
    "            value = str((row or {}).get(\"message\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if value and value not in messages:\n"
    "                messages.append(value)\n"
    "        merged[\"message\"] = \"；\".join(messages)[:800]\n"
    "        return merged\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _dispatch_xunlei_flash(self, subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"真实秒传失败后才继续下一档关键词；搜索本身失败则立即停止。\"\"\"\n"
    "        all_variants = gying_keyword_variants(str(self._provider_keyword(subscribe) or \"\"))\n"
    "        local = self._recall_retry_local_v1125()\n"
    "        tracked = (\"start_index\", \"last_attempted_index\", \"seen_identities\", \"stop_after_failure\")\n"
    "        previous = {\n"
    "            key: (hasattr(local, key), getattr(local, key, None))\n"
    "            for key in tracked\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        local.start_index = 0\n"
    "        local.last_attempted_index = -1\n"
    "        local.seen_identities = set()\n"
    "        local.stop_after_failure = False\n"
    "        combined: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            while True:\n"
    "                before_index = int(getattr(local, \"start_index\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "                local.stop_after_failure = False\n"
    "                current = dict(super()._dispatch_xunlei_flash(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "                combined = self._merge_xunlei_rounds_v1125(combined, current)\n"
    "                if bool(combined.get(\"handled\")) or bool(combined.get(\"movie\")):\n"
    "                    break\n"
    "                if bool(getattr(local, \"stop_after_failure\", False)):\n"
    "                    break\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    last_index = int(getattr(local, \"last_attempted_index\", before_index) or 0)\n"
    "                except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                    last_index = before_index\n"
    "                next_index = last_index + 1\n"
    "                if next_index <= before_index or next_index >= len(all_variants):\n"
    "                    break\n"
    "                local.start_index = next_index\n"
    "            return combined\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            for key, (had_value, value) in previous.items():\n"
    "                if had_value:\n"
    "                    setattr(local, key, value)\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    try:\n"
    "                        delattr(local, key)\n"
    "                    except AttributeError:\n"
    "                        pass\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _dispatch_viewing_external_v1113(self, subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"只有实际没有产生可用 Magnet/ED2K action，才逐级扩大关键词。\"\"\"\n"
    "        result = dict(super()._dispatch_viewing_external_v1113(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "        if bool(result.get(\"success\")) or list(result.get(\"actions\") or []):\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        primary = \" \".join(str(self._provider_keyword(subscribe) or \"\").split())\n"
    "        variants = gying_keyword_variants(primary)\n"
    "        if len(variants) <= 1:\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 没有“严格关键词成功搜索”的事实时，不能因为资源站故障继续扩大请求。\n"
    "        cache = getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache\", None)\n"
    "        cache_key_getter = getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache_key_v11223\", None)\n"
    "        strict_key = cache_key_getter(variants[0], subscribe) if callable(cache_key_getter) else variants[0]\n"
    "        strict_entry = dict(cache.get(strict_key) or {}) if isinstance(cache, dict) else {}\n"
    "        strict_state = strict_entry.get(\"state\") if isinstance(strict_entry.get(\"state\"), dict) else {}\n"
    "        if not strict_entry or strict_state.get(\"success\") is False:\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        attempted: List[str] = [variants[0]]\n"
    "        last_result = result\n"
    "        for variant in variants[1:]:\n"
    "            alias_scope = getattr(self, \"_gying_alias_scope_v11212\", None)\n"
    "            if callable(alias_scope):\n"
    "                with alias_scope(subscribe):\n"
    "                    _unused_xunlei, state = self._gying_xunlei_precise_variant_v1125(variant)\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                _unused_xunlei, state = self._gying_xunlei_precise_variant_v1125(variant)\n"
    "            state = dict(state or {})\n"
    "            if not state.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "                return last_result\n"
    "            attempted.append(variant)\n"
    "            self._promote_search_bundle_v1125(variants[0], attempted, subscribe=subscribe)\n"
    "            current = dict(super()._dispatch_viewing_external_v1113(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "            current[\"query_fallback_v1125\"] = variant\n"
    "            current[\"search_bundle_v1125\"] = True\n"
    "            current[\"bundle_variants_v1125\"] = list(attempted)\n"
    "            last_result = current\n"
    "            if bool(current.get(\"success\")) or list(current.get(\"actions\") or []):\n"
    "                return current\n"
    "        return last_result\n"
    "\n"

    "_GuangYaGyingRecallGuardV1125Base = GuangYaGyingRecallGuardV1125Mixin\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaGyingRecallGuardV1125Mixin(_GuangYaGyingRecallGuardV1125Base):\n"
    "    \"\"\"v1.12.19 安全候选排序与终态来源质量学习，不修改任何最终门禁。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _candidate_rank_local_v11219(self):\n"
    "        local = getattr(self, \"_candidate_rank_local_state_v11219\", None)\n"
    "        if local is None:\n"
    "            local = threading.local()\n"
    "            self._candidate_rank_local_state_v11219 = local\n"
    "        return local\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _candidate_quality_key_v11219(row: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n"
    "        source_type = str((row or {}).get(\"type\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "        if source_type not in {\"guangya\", \"magnet\", \"ed2k\"}:\n"
    "            return \"\"\n"
    "        provider = str((row or {}).get(\"provider\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        origin = str((row or {}).get(\"origin\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        origin_key = origin.casefold()\n"
    "        source_label = str((row or {}).get(\"source_label\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not provider and origin_key.startswith(\"provider:\"):\n"
    "            provider = origin.split(\":\", 1)[1].strip()\n"
    "        if not provider and origin_key == \"viewing_auto\":\n"
    "            provider = source_label or \"GYING\"\n"
    "        if not provider and origin_key.startswith(\"channel\"):\n"
    "            provider = source_label or origin\n"
    "        if not provider:\n"
    "            return \"\"\n"
    "        provider = \" \".join(provider.split()).casefold()[:120]\n"
    "        if provider in {\"viewing\", \"gying\"}:\n"
    "            provider = \"gying\"\n"
    "        return f\"{source_type}|{provider}\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _candidate_quality_store_v11219(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        raw = self.get_data(_CANDIDATE_QUALITY_DATA_KEY_V11219) or {}\n"
    "        if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n"
    "            raw = {}\n"
    "        raw_items = raw.get(\"items\") or {}\n"
    "        items: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        if isinstance(raw_items, dict):\n"
    "            for key, value in raw_items.items():\n"
    "                if not isinstance(value, dict):\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                items[str(key)] = {\n"
    "                    \"success\": _nonnegative_int_v11219(value.get(\"success\")),\n"
    "                    \"failure\": _nonnegative_int_v11219(value.get(\"failure\")),\n"
    "                    \"updated_at\": _nonnegative_float_v11219(value.get(\"updated_at\")),\n"
    "                }\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"schema\": 1,\n"
    "            \"items\": items,\n"
    "            \"updated_at\": _nonnegative_float_v11219(raw.get(\"updated_at\")),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _candidate_quality_snapshot_v11219(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        now = time.monotonic()\n"
    "        cached = getattr(self, \"_candidate_quality_cache_v11219\", None)\n"
    "        if isinstance(cached, tuple) and len(cached) == 2:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                cached_at = float(cached[0])\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                cached_at = 0.0\n"
    "            if now - cached_at < _CANDIDATE_QUALITY_CACHE_TTL_V11219 and isinstance(cached[1], dict):\n"
    "                return dict(cached[1])\n"
    "        items = dict(self._candidate_quality_store_v11219().get(\"items\") or {})\n"
    "        self._candidate_quality_cache_v11219 = (now, items)\n"
    "        return dict(items)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _record_candidate_quality_outcome_v11219(self, row: Dict[str, Any], success: bool) -> None:\n"
    "        key = self._candidate_quality_key_v11219(row)\n"
    "        if not key:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            store = self._candidate_quality_store_v11219()\n"
    "            items = dict(store.get(\"items\") or {})\n"
    "            stats = dict(items.get(key) or {})\n"
    "            stats[\"success\"] = _nonnegative_int_v11219(stats.get(\"success\")) + (1 if success else 0)\n"
    "            stats[\"failure\"] = _nonnegative_int_v11219(stats.get(\"failure\")) + (0 if success else 1)\n"
    "            stats[\"updated_at\"] = time.time()\n"
    "            items[key] = stats\n"
    "            store[\"items\"] = items\n"
    "            store[\"updated_at\"] = time.time()\n"
    "            self.save_data(_CANDIDATE_QUALITY_DATA_KEY_V11219, store)\n"
    "            self._candidate_quality_cache_v11219 = (time.monotonic(), dict(items))\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"DEBUG\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【候选学习v1.12.19】质量统计写入失败 key=%s: %s\",\n"
    "                key,\n"
    "                err,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _update_source(self, source_id: str, **fields: Any):\n"
    "        \"\"\"只观察真实 terminal transition；所有非终态更新零额外读写。\"\"\"\n"
    "        target_state = str(fields.get(\"state\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "        if target_state not in _TERMINAL_SOURCE_OUTCOME_V11219:\n"
    "            return super()._update_source(source_id, **fields)\n"
    "\n"
    "        had_before = False\n"
    "        before_state = \"\"\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            before = dict(self._source_store().get(\"items\", {}).get(str(source_id)) or {})\n"
    "            had_before = bool(before)\n"
    "            before_state = str(before.get(\"state\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            had_before = False\n"
    "\n"
    "        updated = super()._update_source(source_id, **fields)\n"
    "        if updated and had_before and before_state != target_state:\n"
    "            self._record_candidate_quality_outcome_v11219(\n"
    "                dict(updated),\n"
    "                bool(_TERMINAL_SOURCE_OUTCOME_V11219[target_state]),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        return updated\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _dispatch_provider_candidate(self, subscribe: Any, uncovered: set[int]):\n"
    "        \"\"\"只给既有 Provider dispatch 注入只读排序上下文，不复制执行链。\"\"\"\n"
    "        local = self._candidate_rank_local_v11219()\n"
    "        previous_subscribe = (hasattr(local, \"subscribe\"), getattr(local, \"subscribe\", None))\n"
    "        previous_uncovered = (hasattr(local, \"uncovered\"), getattr(local, \"uncovered\", None))\n"
    "        local.subscribe = subscribe\n"
    "        local.uncovered = set(uncovered or set())\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return super()._dispatch_provider_candidate(subscribe, uncovered)\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            if previous_subscribe[0]:\n"
    "                local.subscribe = previous_subscribe[1]\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    delattr(local, \"subscribe\")\n"
    "                except AttributeError:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "            if previous_uncovered[0]:\n"
    "                local.uncovered = previous_uncovered[1]\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    delattr(local, \"uncovered\")\n"
    "                except AttributeError:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _search_external_providers(self, keyword: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"稳定重排 super 已召回候选；不扩容、不新增请求、不放宽 eligibility。\"\"\"\n"
    "        result = dict(super()._search_external_providers(keyword) or {})\n"
    "        rows = [dict(row) for row in (result.get(\"data\") or []) if isinstance(row, dict)]\n"
    "        if len(rows) <= 1:\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        local = self._candidate_rank_local_v11219()\n"
    "        subscribe = getattr(local, \"subscribe\", None)\n"
    "        uncovered = {\n"
    "            int(value)\n"
    "            for value in (getattr(local, \"uncovered\", set()) or set())\n"
    "            if str(value).isdigit() and int(value) > 0\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        quality = self._candidate_quality_snapshot_v11219()\n"
    "        ranked = []\n"
    "        is_movie = bool(subscribe is not None and self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe))\n"
    "        for index, row in enumerate(rows):\n"
    "            eligible = True\n"
    "            coverage_penalty = 1\n"
    "            extra_episode_count = 0\n"
    "            hit_episode_count = 0\n"
    "            if subscribe is not None:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    eligible = bool(self._provider_candidate_matches(subscribe, row))\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    eligible = False\n"
    "                if eligible and not is_movie and uncovered:\n"
    "                    explicit = self._candidate_episode_hint_v1125(subscribe, row)\n"
    "                    if explicit:\n"
    "                        hit = set(explicit).intersection(uncovered)\n"
    "                        extra = set(explicit) - uncovered\n"
    "                        hit_episode_count = len(hit)\n"
    "                        extra_episode_count = len(extra)\n"
    "                        if hit and not extra:\n"
    "                            coverage_penalty = 0\n"
    "                        elif hit:\n"
    "                            coverage_penalty = 2\n"
    "                        else:\n"
    "                            coverage_penalty = 3\n"
    "            key = self._candidate_quality_key_v11219(row)\n"
    "            quality_score = candidate_quality_score_v11219(dict(quality.get(key) or {})) if key else 0\n"
    "            candidate_rank = _nonnegative_int_v11219(row.get(\"candidate_rank\"))\n"
    "            sort_key = candidate_rank_key_v11219(\n"
    "                str(row.get(\"type\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                eligible,\n"
    "                coverage_penalty,\n"
    "                extra_episode_count,\n"
    "                hit_episode_count,\n"
    "                quality_score,\n"
    "                candidate_rank,\n"
    "                index,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            enriched = dict(row)\n"
    "            enriched[\"candidate_score_v11219\"] = quality_score\n"
    "            enriched[\"candidate_sort_v11219\"] = list(sort_key[:-1])\n"
    "            ranked.append((sort_key, enriched))\n"
    "\n"
    "        ranked.sort(key=lambda item: item[0])\n"
    "        result[\"data\"] = [row for _key, row in ranked]\n"
    "        result[\"candidate_ranking_v11219\"] = True\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_candidate_priority_v1125(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        row: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        missing: set[int],\n"
    "    ) -> Tuple[int, ...]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"严格继承旧缺集优先键，只在其后追加“无跨界集/覆盖更多/有提取码”细排。\"\"\"\n"
    "        base = tuple(super()._xunlei_candidate_priority_v1125(subscribe, row, missing))\n"
    "        explicit = self._candidate_episode_hint_v1125(subscribe, row)\n"
    "        overlap = set(explicit).intersection(missing or set())\n"
    "        extras = set(explicit) - set(missing or set())\n"
    "        package_penalty = 0 if explicit and overlap and not extras else (1 if not explicit else 2)\n"
    "        passcode_penalty = 0 if str((row or {}).get(\"passcode\") or \"\").strip() else 1\n"
    "        return (*base, package_penalty, len(extras), -len(overlap), passcode_penalty)\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\n"
    "    \"GuangYaGyingRecallGuardV1125Mixin\",\n"
    "    \"candidate_quality_score_v11219\",\n"
    "    \"candidate_rank_key_v11219\",\n"
    "]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== pow_singleflight_v209.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["pow_singleflight_v209"] = (
    "\"\"\"2.0.9-r93：同一 GYING 节点 PanSou PoW singleflight。\n"
    "\n"
    "禁止并发订阅各自重复求解同一节点 challenge；等待首个求解结果并复用会话。\n"
    "不改写 gying_browser / gying_pow / gying_pansou 核心算法，只在求解入口加锁合并。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import threading\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, Optional\n"
    "\n"
    "from .gying_hardening_v193 import canonical_gying_node\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaPowSingleflightV209Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"Per-node PoW singleflight around `_gying_solve_challenge_v1110`.\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    _pow_singleflight_master_v209 = threading.RLock()\n"
    "    _pow_singleflight_overflow_v209 = threading.RLock()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        self._pow_singleflight_locks_v209: Dict[str, threading.RLock] = {}\n"
    "        self._pow_singleflight_results_v209: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        return super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _pow_node_key_v209(self, node: str) -> str:\n"
    "        return canonical_gying_node(node) or str(node or \"\").rstrip(\"/\").lower()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _pow_lock_for_node_v209(self, node_key: str) -> threading.RLock:\n"
    "        master = getattr(self, \"_pow_singleflight_master_v209\", None) or threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._pow_singleflight_master_v209 = master\n"
    "        with master:\n"
    "            locks = getattr(self, \"_pow_singleflight_locks_v209\", None)\n"
    "            if not isinstance(locks, dict):\n"
    "                locks = {}\n"
    "                self._pow_singleflight_locks_v209 = locks\n"
    "            lock = locks.get(node_key)\n"
    "            if lock is not None:\n"
    "                return lock\n"
    "            if len(locks) >= 64:\n"
    "                return getattr(self, \"_pow_singleflight_overflow_v209\", None) or threading.RLock()\n"
    "            lock = threading.RLock()\n"
    "            locks[node_key] = lock\n"
    "            return lock\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_solve_challenge_v1110(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        session,\n"
    "        node: str,\n"
    "        response,\n"
    "        kind: Optional[str] = None,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        node_key = self._pow_node_key_v209(node)\n"
    "        kind_token = str(kind or \"\")\n"
    "        # Only coalesce remote_pow / empty kind (resolved inside); still serialize all solves per node.\n"
    "        lock = self._pow_lock_for_node_v209(node_key or \"-\")\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            cache = getattr(self, \"_pow_singleflight_results_v209\", None)\n"
    "            if not isinstance(cache, dict):\n"
    "                cache = {}\n"
    "                self._pow_singleflight_results_v209 = cache\n"
    "            cached = dict(cache.get(node_key) or {})\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                age = time.monotonic() - float(cached.get(\"at\") or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                age = 10**9\n"
    "            # Fresh verified solve within 45s can be reused by waiters on the same node.\n"
    "            if cached.get(\"ok\") and age < 45:\n"
    "                bump = getattr(self, \"_bump_metric_v209\", None)\n"
    "                if callable(bump):\n"
    "                    bump(\"cache_hits\")\n"
    "                return dict(cached.get(\"result\") or {\"success\": True, \"reused\": True, \"node\": node_key})\n"
    "\n"
    "            bump = getattr(self, \"_bump_metric_v209\", None)\n"
    "            if callable(bump):\n"
    "                bump(\"pansou_pow_challenges\")\n"
    "            result = dict(\n"
    "                super()._gying_solve_challenge_v1110(session, node, response, kind=kind_token or kind) or {}\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            cache[node_key] = {\n"
    "                \"at\": time.monotonic(),\n"
    "                \"ok\": True,\n"
    "                \"result\": dict(result),\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaPowSingleflightV209Mixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== core_pipeline_final_v11214.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["core_pipeline_final_v11214"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.12.14 核心链最终权威缺口实现。\n"
    "\n"
    "Core 候选层只表达统一规则；本层把它绑定到 v1.12.13 已验证的 MoviePilot 缺集 ABI：\n"
    "``_sync_media_library_progress`` 返回 library existing/missing，\n"
    "``_base_missing_without_due_scope_v11213`` 返回成功事实 + 订阅 note 的完整逻辑缺口。\n"
    "\n"
    "当前正在 resolve/create 的外部 source 必须从 active claims 中排除，否则它会用自己的\n"
    "``target_episodes`` 把自己从 allowed 集合中扣空。\n"
    "\n"
    "同时补足直接光鸭分享的 rootless 文件身份：只有文件名在明确 Episode 标记前存在可信\n"
    "标题前缀时，才把该前缀提升为 actual primary evidence。这样 ``Other.Show.S01E11.mkv``\n"
    "会作为真实冲突被拒绝，而 ``S01E11.mkv`` 仍保持“信息不足但无冲突”的保守语义；\n"
    "``Show.Name.S01E11-GROUP.mkv`` 只取 Episode 前的 ``Show.Name``，不会把发布组误当标题。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import re\n"
    "from typing import Any, List, Sequence, Set\n"
    "\n"
    "from .core_pipeline_v11214 import (\n"
    "    GuangYaCorePipelineV11214Mixin,\n"
    "    _positive_episode_set_v11214,\n"
    ")\n"
    "from .media_identity_v1111 import title_key_v1111\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_ACTUAL_EP_MARKER_V11214 = re.compile(\n"
    "    r\"(?i)(?:\\bS\\d{1,2}[ ._\\-]*E\\d{1,4}\\b|\\b(?:E|EP|Episode)[ ._\\-]*0*\\d{1,4}\\b|第\\s*\\d{1,4}\\s*(?:集|话))\"\n"
    ")\n"
    "_GENERIC_ACTUAL_TITLE_KEYS_V11214 = {\n"
    "    \"file\", \"files\", \"video\", \"videos\", \"tv\", \"season\", \"resource\", \"share\",\n"
    "    \"资源\", \"文件\", \"视频\", \"电视剧\", \"剧集\", \"分享\", \"全集\", \"全季\",\n"
    "}\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaCorePipelineFinalV11214Mixin(GuangYaCorePipelineV11214Mixin):\n"
    "    \"\"\"把所有 TV 最终写盘来源收紧到同一份 MoviePilot 权威缺口。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    plugin_version = \"1.12.14\"\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260905-r60\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _direct_share_primary_roots_v11214(paths: Sequence[str], expected_year: Any = None) -> List[str]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"在单文件无父目录时，仅抽取明确 Episode 标记之前的实际标题前缀。\"\"\"\n"
    "        rows = list(GuangYaCorePipelineV11214Mixin._direct_share_primary_roots_v11214(paths, expected_year))\n"
    "        seen = {str(value or \"\").casefold() for value in rows}\n"
    "        for raw in paths or []:\n"
    "            name = str(raw or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1].strip()\n"
    "            matched = _ACTUAL_EP_MARKER_V11214.search(name)\n"
    "            if not matched:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            prefix = name[:matched.start()].strip(\" ._-[]()（）【】\")\n"
    "            key = title_key_v1111(prefix, expected_year=expected_year).casefold()\n"
    "            if len(key) < 3 or key in _GENERIC_ACTUAL_TITLE_KEYS_V11214:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            marker = prefix.casefold()\n"
    "            if marker and marker not in seen:\n"
    "                seen.add(marker)\n"
    "                rows.append(prefix)\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _hydrate_viewing_guangya_shares_v11214(self, subscribe: Any) -> int:\n"
    "        \"\"\"让 GYING 光鸭分享与迅雷/Magnet 共用同一订阅 TMDB alias scope。\"\"\"\n"
    "        scope = getattr(self, \"_gying_alias_scope_v11212\", None)\n"
    "        if not callable(scope):\n"
    "            return int(super()._hydrate_viewing_guangya_shares_v11214(subscribe) or 0)\n"
    "        with scope(subscribe):\n"
    "            return int(super()._hydrate_viewing_guangya_shares_v11214(subscribe) or 0)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _authoritative_missing_v11214(self, subscribe: Any, *, current_source_id: str = \"\") -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return set()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            sync = dict(self._sync_media_library_progress(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            sync = {\"success\": False, \"missing\": [], \"message\": str(err)[:260]}\n"
    "        if not bool(sync.get(\"success\")):\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "                \"MoviePilot 媒体库缺集事实读取失败，最终写盘 fail closed：\"\n"
    "                + str(sync.get(\"message\") or \"unknown\")[:260]\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "        library_missing = _positive_episode_set_v11214(sync.get(\"missing\") or [])\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            logical_missing = set(self._base_missing_without_due_scope_v11213(subscribe) or set())\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            logical_missing = _positive_episode_set_v11214(self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or [])\n"
    "        # Emby/library missing is the base truth.\n"
    "        # When logical/note missing is non-empty, exclude pending-ingest episodes\n"
    "        # (Emby gap but note/receipt already claims done). When logical is empty\n"
    "        # (stale-complete note), keep the full Emby gap — never hard-cap to empty.\n"
    "        allowed = set(library_missing)\n"
    "        if library_missing and logical_missing:\n"
    "            allowed -= library_missing - _positive_episode_set_v11214(logical_missing)\n"
    "        elif not library_missing and logical_missing:\n"
    "            allowed = _positive_episode_set_v11214(logical_missing)\n"
    "\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            reservations = dict(self._pending_reservations(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "            allowed -= _positive_episode_set_v11214(reservations.get(\"episodes\") or [])\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        allowed -= self._other_source_claims_v11214(sid, current_source_id=current_source_id)\n"
    "        return allowed\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaCorePipelineFinalV11214Mixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

    "def _response_json_v1108(response: requests.Response) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        payload = response.json()\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            payload = json.loads(str(getattr(response, \"text\", \"\") or \"{}\"))\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            payload = {}\n"
    "    return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}\n"
    "\n"

    "def _challenge_required_v1108(response: requests.Response) -> bool:\n"
    "    \"\"\"按真实站点语义判 PoW；正常 _obj. 数据页永远优先于静态脚本关键字。\"\"\"\n"
    "    text = str(getattr(response, \"text\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "    if \"_obj.\" in text:\n"
    "        return False\n"
    "    status = int(getattr(response, \"status_code\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "    if status == 419:\n"
    "        return True\n"
    "    payload = _response_json_v1108(response)\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        if int(payload.get(\"code\") or 0) == 419:\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"

    "    try:\n"
    "        refresh = int(payload.get(\"refresh\") or 0) == 1\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "        refresh = False\n"

    "    if refresh and \"验证\" in message:\n"
    "        return True\n"
    "    return any(marker in text for marker in _CHALLENGE_TEXT_MARKERS_V1108)\n"

    "\n"
    "def _cookie_header_v1108(session: requests.Session) -> str:\n"
    "    rows: List[str] = []\n"
    "    seen = set()\n"
    "    for cookie in list(session.cookies):\n"
    "        name = str(getattr(cookie, \"name\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        value = str(getattr(cookie, \"value\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if name and value and name not in seen:\n"
    "            rows.append(f\"{name}={value}\")\n"
    "            seen.add(name)\n"
    "    return \"; \".join(rows)\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _drop_cookie_v1108(session: requests.Session, name: str) -> None:\n"
    "    target = str(name or \"\").lower()\n"
    "    for cookie in list(session.cookies):\n"
    "        if str(getattr(cookie, \"name\", \"\") or \"\").lower() != target:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            session.cookies.clear(cookie.domain, cookie.path, cookie.name)\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                session.cookies.set(cookie.name, None)\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"

    "        cached = str(getattr(self, \"_gying_shared_cookie_v1108\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"

    "            return cached\n"

    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return \"\"\n"

    "            node = canonical_gying_node(str(raw_node or \"\"))\n"

    "                ranked.append((float(row.get(\"last_ok_ts\") or 0), cookie))\n"
    "        if not ranked:\n"
    "            return \"\"\n"
    "        ranked.sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)\n"
    "        return ranked[0][1]\n"

    "    def _gying_sync_cookie_v1108(self, session: requests.Session, node: str) -> None:\n"
